ZensusTech Microsoft 365 Managed Services offering, I would organize them into these major categories:

| License family             | Main plans                                              | Typical customer                                      |
| -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- |
| **1. Business**            | Business Basic, Standard, Premium, Apps for business    | SMBs up to 300 users                                  |
| **2. Enterprise**          | M365 E3, E5; Office 365 E1/E3/E5                        | Medium/large enterprises                              |
| **3. Frontline**           | F1, F3                                                  | Factory, retail, healthcare, field workers            |
| **4. Education**           | A1, A3, A5                                              | Schools, colleges, universities                       |
| **5. Government**          | Government-specific G plans                             | Government organizations                              |
| **6. Nonprofit**           | Nonprofit variants of Business/Enterprise               | NGOs/nonprofits                                       |
| **7. Individual/Consumer** | Personal, Family                                        | Individual users                                      |
| **8. Apps-only**           | Microsoft 365 Apps for business/enterprise              | Customers needing Office apps without full M365 suite |
| **9. Add-ons**             | Copilot, Teams Phone, security/compliance add-ons, etc. | Existing M365 customers                               |

Start with 01 — License Inventory

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 01 - LICENSE INVENTORY
# ==============================================================
# Requirements:
#   1. Azure CLI
#   2. az login
#   3. Permission to read Microsoft Graph organization/license data
#
# API:
#   Microsoft Graph /v1.0/subscribedSkus
# ==============================================================

set -euo pipefail

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | M365 LICENSE INVENTORY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Check Azure CLI
# ------------------------------------------------------------
if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Azure CLI is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Check Azure login
# ------------------------------------------------------------
if ! az account show >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Please run 'az login' first."
    exit 1
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get Tenant Information
# ------------------------------------------------------------
TENANT_ID=$(az account show --query tenantId -o tsv)

echo "Tenant ID : $TENANT_ID"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get Microsoft 365 License Information
# ------------------------------------------------------------
echo "Getting Microsoft 365 licenses..."
echo

LICENSE_DATA=$(az rest \
    --method get \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/subscribedSkus" \
    --headers "Content-Type=application/json")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Validate response
# ------------------------------------------------------------
if [[ -z "$LICENSE_DATA" ]]; then
    echo "ERROR: No license data returned."
    exit 1
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Display License Inventory
# ------------------------------------------------------------

printf "%-45s %12s %12s %12s\n" \
    "LICENSE" "PURCHASED" "ASSIGNED" "AVAILABLE"

echo "--------------------------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "$LICENSE_DATA" | jq -r '
.value[] |
[
    (.skuPartNumber // "Unknown"),
    (.prepaidUnits.enabled // 0),
    (.consumedUnits // 0),
    ((.prepaidUnits.enabled // 0) - (.consumedUnits // 0))
] |
@tsv
' | while IFS=$'\t' read -r SKU PURCHASED ASSIGNED AVAILABLE
do

    printf "%-45s %12s %12s %12s\n" \
        "$SKU" \
        "$PURCHASED" \
        "$ASSIGNED" \
        "$AVAILABLE"

done

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " LICENSE INVENTORY COMPLETED"
echo "============================================================"
echo
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
02 — Assigned vs Available Licenses.

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 02 - ASSIGNED VS AVAILABLE LICENSES
# ==============================================================
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#   - az login
#
# APIs:
#   Microsoft Graph:
#   /v1.0/subscribedSkus
#   /v1.0/users?$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,assignedLicenses
# ==============================================================

set -euo pipefail

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | M365 ASSIGNED VS AVAILABLE LICENSES"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Check dependencies
# ------------------------------------------------------------

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Azure CLI is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: jq is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! az account show >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Please run 'az login' first."
    exit 1
fi

TENANT_ID=$(az account show --query tenantId -o tsv)

echo "Tenant ID : $TENANT_ID"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get subscribed SKUs
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Getting Microsoft 365 license inventory..."

LICENSE_DATA=$(az rest \
    --method get \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/subscribedSkus")

if [[ -z "$LICENSE_DATA" ]]; then
    echo "ERROR: Unable to retrieve license information."
    exit 1
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get users and assigned licenses
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Getting users and assigned licenses..."

USERS=$(az rest \
    --method get \
    --url 'https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/users?$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,accountEnabled,assignedLicenses&$top=999')

if [[ -z "$USERS" ]]; then
    echo "ERROR: Unable to retrieve users."
    exit 1
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Create temporary files
# ------------------------------------------------------------

LICENSE_FILE=$(mktemp)
USER_FILE=$(mktemp)

trap 'rm -f "$LICENSE_FILE" "$USER_FILE"' EXIT

echo "$LICENSE_DATA" > "$LICENSE_FILE"
echo "$USERS" > "$USER_FILE"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# License Summary
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " LICENSE SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

printf "%-42s %10s %10s %10s\n" \
    "LICENSE" "PURCHASED" "ASSIGNED" "AVAILABLE"

echo "-------------------------------------------------------------------------------"

jq -r '
.value[] |
[
    .skuId,
    (.skuPartNumber // "Unknown"),
    (.prepaidUnits.enabled // 0)
] |
@tsv
' "$LICENSE_FILE" |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r SKU_ID SKU_NAME PURCHASED
do

    ASSIGNED=$(jq --arg sku "$SKU_ID" '
        [
            .value[] |
            .assignedLicenses[]? |
            select(.skuId == $sku)
        ] |
        length
    ' "$USER_FILE")

    AVAILABLE=$((PURCHASED - ASSIGNED))

    printf "%-42s %10s %10s %10s\n" \
        "$SKU_NAME" \
        "$PURCHASED" \
        "$ASSIGNED" \
        "$AVAILABLE"

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Detailed User License Assignment
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " USER LICENSE ASSIGNMENTS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

printf "%-35s %-42s %-10s\n" \
    "USER" "LICENSE" "STATUS"

echo "--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------"

jq -r '
.value[] |
select((.assignedLicenses | length) > 0) |
[
    .displayName,
    .userPrincipalName,
    .accountEnabled,
    (
        .assignedLicenses |
        map(.skuId) |
        join(",")
    )
] |
@tsv
' "$USER_FILE" |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r DISPLAY_NAME UPN ACCOUNT_ENABLED SKU_IDS
do

    IFS=',' read -ra SKU_ARRAY <<< "$SKU_IDS"

    for SKU_ID in "${SKU_ARRAY[@]}"
    do

        SKU_NAME=$(jq -r --arg sku "$SKU_ID" '
            .value[] |
            select(.skuId == $sku) |
            .skuPartNumber
        ' "$LICENSE_FILE")

        if [[ "$ACCOUNT_ENABLED" == "true" ]]; then
            STATUS="Active"
        else
            STATUS="Disabled"
        fi

        printf "%-35s %-42s %-10s\n" \
            "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
            "$SKU_NAME" \
            "$STATUS"

    done

done

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ASSIGNED VS AVAILABLE CHECK COMPLETED"
echo "============================================================"
echo

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
03 — Unassigned / Unused Licenses

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 03 - UNUSED / UNASSIGNED LICENSES
# ==============================================================
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#   - az login
#
# API:
#   Microsoft Graph
#   /v1.0/subscribedSkus
#   /v1.0/users
# ==============================================================

set -euo pipefail

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | M365 UNUSED / UNASSIGNED LICENSES"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Check dependencies
# ------------------------------------------------------------

command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
    echo "ERROR: Azure CLI is not installed."
    exit 1
}

command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
    echo "ERROR: jq is not installed."
    exit 1
}

az account show >/dev/null 2>&1 || {
    echo "ERROR: Please run 'az login' first."
    exit 1
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Tenant
# ------------------------------------------------------------

TENANT_ID=$(az account show --query tenantId -o tsv)

echo "Tenant ID : $TENANT_ID"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Temporary files
# ------------------------------------------------------------

LICENSE_FILE=$(mktemp)
USER_FILE=$(mktemp)

trap 'rm -f "$LICENSE_FILE" "$USER_FILE"' EXIT

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get subscribed licenses
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Getting subscribed Microsoft 365 licenses..."

az rest \
    --method get \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/subscribedSkus" \
    > "$LICENSE_FILE"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get users
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Getting users and assigned licenses..."

az rest \
    --method get \
    --url 'https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/users?$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,assignedLicenses&$top=999' \
    > "$USER_FILE"

echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Header
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " UNUSED LICENSE REPORT"
echo "============================================================"
echo

printf "%-42s %12s %12s %12s %12s\n" \
    "LICENSE" \
    "PURCHASED" \
    "ASSIGNED" \
    "UNUSED" \
    "UTILIZATION"

echo "------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------"

TOTAL_PURCHASED=0
TOTAL_ASSIGNED=0
TOTAL_UNUSED=0

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Process every SKU
# ------------------------------------------------------------

jq -r '
.value[] |
[
    .skuId,
    (.skuPartNumber // "Unknown"),
    (.prepaidUnits.enabled // 0)
] |
@tsv
' "$LICENSE_FILE" |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r SKU_ID SKU_NAME PURCHASED
do

    ASSIGNED=$(jq --arg sku "$SKU_ID" '
        [
            .value[] |
            .assignedLicenses[]? |
            select(.skuId == $sku)
        ] |
        length
    ' "$USER_FILE")

    UNUSED=$((PURCHASED - ASSIGNED))

    if [[ "$PURCHASED" -gt 0 ]]; then
        UTILIZATION=$(awk \
            -v a="$ASSIGNED" \
            -v p="$PURCHASED" \
            'BEGIN { printf "%.1f%%", (a/p)*100 }')
    else
        UTILIZATION="0.0%"
    fi

    printf "%-42s %12s %12s %12s %12s\n" \
        "$SKU_NAME" \
        "$PURCHASED" \
        "$ASSIGNED" \
        "$UNUSED" \
        "$UTILIZATION"

done

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " LICENSE OPTIMIZATION FINDINGS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Show licenses with unused quantities
# ------------------------------------------------------------

FOUND_UNUSED=false

jq -r '
.value[] |
[
    .skuId,
    (.skuPartNumber // "Unknown"),
    (.prepaidUnits.enabled // 0)
] |
@tsv
' "$LICENSE_FILE" |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r SKU_ID SKU_NAME PURCHASED
do

    ASSIGNED=$(jq --arg sku "$SKU_ID" '
        [
            .value[] |
            .assignedLicenses[]? |
            select(.skuId == $sku)
        ] |
        length
    ' "$USER_FILE")

    UNUSED=$((PURCHASED - ASSIGNED))

    if [[ "$UNUSED" -gt 0 ]]; then

        FOUND_UNUSED=true

        echo "⚠ $SKU_NAME"
        echo "  Purchased : $PURCHASED"
        echo "  Assigned  : $ASSIGNED"
        echo "  Unused     : $UNUSED"
        echo

    fi

done

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS RECOMMENDATION"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Review unused licenses before the next renewal."
echo "Unused licenses represent potential license-cost optimization."
echo

echo "============================================================"
echo " UNUSED LICENSE CHECK COMPLETED"
echo "============================================================"
echo

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

04 — User License Mapping.

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 04 - USER LICENSE MAPPING
# ==============================================================
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#   - az login
#
# API:
#   Microsoft Graph
#   /v1.0/users
#   /v1.0/subscribedSkus
#
# Purpose:
#   Map every M365 user to their assigned licenses.
# ==============================================================

set -euo pipefail

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | M365 USER LICENSE MAPPING"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Dependencies
# ------------------------------------------------------------

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Azure CLI is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: jq is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! az account show >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Please run 'az login' first."
    exit 1
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Tenant
# ------------------------------------------------------------

TENANT_ID=$(az account show --query tenantId -o tsv)

echo "Tenant ID : $TENANT_ID"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Temporary files
# ------------------------------------------------------------

USERS_FILE=$(mktemp)
SKUS_FILE=$(mktemp)

trap 'rm -f "$USERS_FILE" "$SKUS_FILE"' EXIT

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get subscribed SKUs
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Getting Microsoft 365 SKU information..."

az rest \
    --method get \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/subscribedSkus" \
    > "$SKUS_FILE"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get users
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Getting Microsoft 365 users..."

az rest \
    --method get \
    --url 'https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/users?$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,accountEnabled,assignedLicenses&$top=999' \
    > "$USERS_FILE"

echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Header
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " USER LICENSE ASSIGNMENTS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

printf "%-30s %-38s %-32s %-12s\n" \
    "DISPLAY NAME" \
    "USER" \
    "LICENSE" \
    "ACCOUNT"

echo "----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Process users
# ------------------------------------------------------------

jq -c '
.value[]
' "$USERS_FILE" |
while read -r USER
do

    DISPLAY_NAME=$(echo "$USER" | jq -r '.displayName // "Unknown"')

    UPN=$(echo "$USER" | jq -r '.userPrincipalName // "Unknown"')

    ACCOUNT_ENABLED=$(echo "$USER" | jq -r '.accountEnabled // false')

    if [[ "$ACCOUNT_ENABLED" == "true" ]]; then
        ACCOUNT_STATUS="Active"
    else
        ACCOUNT_STATUS="Disabled"
    fi

    LICENSE_COUNT=$(echo "$USER" | jq '.assignedLicenses | length')

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Users without licenses
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ "$LICENSE_COUNT" -eq 0 ]]; then

        printf "%-30s %-38s %-32s %-12s\n" \
            "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
            "$UPN" \
            "NO LICENSE" \
            "$ACCOUNT_STATUS"

        continue
    fi

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Process assigned licenses
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    echo "$USER" |
    jq -r '
        .assignedLicenses[]?.skuId
    ' |
    while read -r SKU_ID
    do

        SKU_NAME=$(jq -r \
            --arg sku "$SKU_ID" \
            '
            .value[]
            | select(.skuId == $sku)
            | .skuPartNumber
            ' "$SKUS_FILE")

        if [[ -z "$SKU_NAME" || "$SKU_NAME" == "null" ]]; then
            SKU_NAME="$SKU_ID"
        fi

        printf "%-30s %-38s %-32s %-12s\n" \
            "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
            "$UPN" \
            "$SKU_NAME" \
            "$ACCOUNT_STATUS"

    done

done

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " USER LICENSE STATISTICS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

TOTAL_USERS=$(jq '.value | length' "$USERS_FILE")

LICENSED_USERS=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select((.assignedLicenses | length) > 0)
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

UNLICENSED_USERS=$((TOTAL_USERS - LICENSED_USERS))

DISABLED_USERS=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select(.accountEnabled == false)
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

DISABLED_LICENSED_USERS=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select(
        .accountEnabled == false
        and
        (.assignedLicenses | length) > 0
    )
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

echo "Total Users                    : $TOTAL_USERS"
echo "Licensed Users                 : $LICENSED_USERS"
echo "Users Without License          : $UNLICENSED_USERS"
echo "Disabled Users                 : $DISABLED_USERS"
echo "Disabled Users With License    : $DISABLED_LICENSED_USERS"

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS FINDINGS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

if [[ "$DISABLED_LICENSED_USERS" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🔴 $DISABLED_LICENSED_USERS disabled users still have licenses."
    echo "   Review these users for potential license recovery."
else
    echo "🟢 No disabled users with assigned licenses detected."
fi

if [[ "$UNLICENSED_USERS" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🟡 $UNLICENSED_USERS users currently have no license."
fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " USER LICENSE MAPPING COMPLETED"
echo "============================================================"
echo
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

05 — Inactive Users Still Consuming Licenses.

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 05 - INACTIVE USERS WITH LICENSES
# ==============================================================
# Purpose:
#   Identify licensed users who have not successfully signed in
#   for the configured number of days.
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#   - az login
#   - Microsoft Graph permissions:
#       User.Read.All
#       AuditLog.Read.All
#
# API:
#   Microsoft Graph v1.0/users
#
# Important:
#   signInActivity requires Entra ID P1/P2 licensing.
# ==============================================================

set -euo pipefail

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# CONFIGURATION
# ------------------------------------------------------------

INACTIVE_DAYS="${1:-90}"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Validate threshold
# ------------------------------------------------------------

if ! [[ "$INACTIVE_DAYS" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    echo "ERROR: Inactive days must be a number."
    echo
    echo "Usage:"
    echo "  ./05-m365-inactive-users.sh 90"
    echo "  ./05-m365-inactive-users.sh 60"
    echo "  ./05-m365-inactive-users.sh 30"
    exit 1
fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | M365 INACTIVE LICENSED USERS"
echo "============================================================"
echo
echo "Inactive threshold : $INACTIVE_DAYS days"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Dependencies
# ------------------------------------------------------------

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Azure CLI is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: jq is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! az account show >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Please run 'az login' first."
    exit 1
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Tenant
# ------------------------------------------------------------

TENANT_ID=$(az account show --query tenantId -o tsv)

echo "Tenant ID : $TENANT_ID"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Temporary files
# ------------------------------------------------------------

USERS_FILE=$(mktemp)
SKUS_FILE=$(mktemp)

trap 'rm -f "$USERS_FILE" "$SKUS_FILE"' EXIT

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get SKU information
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Getting Microsoft 365 licenses..."

az rest \
    --method get \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/subscribedSkus" \
    > "$SKUS_FILE"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get users + sign-in activity
#
# Microsoft limits pages containing signInActivity to 500 users.
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Getting users and sign-in activity..."

az rest \
    --method get \
    --url 'https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/users?$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,accountEnabled,assignedLicenses,signInActivity&$top=500' \
    > "$USERS_FILE"

echo
echo "Processing data..."
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Calculate cutoff date
# ------------------------------------------------------------

CUTOFF_DATE=$(date -u -d "$INACTIVE_DAYS days ago" +"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Report header
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " INACTIVE LICENSED USERS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

printf "%-28s %-38s %-30s %-15s\n" \
    "USER" \
    "UPN" \
    "LAST SUCCESSFUL SIGN-IN" \
    "STATUS"

echo "----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Counters
# ------------------------------------------------------------

INACTIVE_COUNT=0
DISABLED_LICENSED_COUNT=0
NEVER_SIGNED_IN_COUNT=0

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Process users
# ------------------------------------------------------------

jq -c '.value[]' "$USERS_FILE" |
while read -r USER
do

    DISPLAY_NAME=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.displayName // "Unknown"')

    UPN=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.userPrincipalName // "Unknown"')

    ACCOUNT_ENABLED=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.accountEnabled // false')

    LICENSE_COUNT=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq '.assignedLicenses | length')

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Skip users without licenses
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ "$LICENSE_COUNT" -eq 0 ]]; then
        continue
    fi

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Last successful sign-in
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    LAST_SIGNIN=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime // empty')

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Disabled account
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ "$ACCOUNT_ENABLED" == "false" ]]; then

        printf "%-28s %-38s %-30s %-15s\n" \
            "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
            "$UPN" \
            "${LAST_SIGNIN:-Never}" \
            "DISABLED"

        continue
    fi

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Never successfully signed in
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ -z "$LAST_SIGNIN" || "$LAST_SIGNIN" == "null" ]]; then

        printf "%-28s %-38s %-30s %-15s\n" \
            "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
            "$UPN" \
            "Never" \
            "NEVER USED"

        continue
    fi

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Compare dates
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    LAST_SIGNIN_EPOCH=$(date -u -d "$LAST_SIGNIN" +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)
    CUTOFF_EPOCH=$(date -u -d "$CUTOFF_DATE" +%s)

    if [[ "$LAST_SIGNIN_EPOCH" -lt "$CUTOFF_EPOCH" ]]; then

        printf "%-28s %-38s %-30s %-15s\n" \
            "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
            "$UPN" \
            "$LAST_SIGNIN" \
            "INACTIVE"

    fi

done

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS FINDINGS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Inactive threshold : $INACTIVE_DAYS days"
echo "Cutoff date        : $CUTOFF_DATE"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Generate summary using jq
# ------------------------------------------------------------

TOTAL_LICENSED=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select((.assignedLicenses | length) > 0)
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

DISABLED_LICENSED=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select(
        (.assignedLicenses | length) > 0
        and
        (.accountEnabled == false)
    )
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

NEVER_USED=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select(
        (.assignedLicenses | length) > 0
        and
        (.accountEnabled == true)
        and
        (
            .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == null
            or
            .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == ""
        )
    )
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

INACTIVE=$(jq --arg cutoff "$CUTOFF_DATE" '
[
    .value[]
    | select((.assignedLicenses | length) > 0)
    | select(.accountEnabled == true)
    | select(
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime != null
        and
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime != ""
        and
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime < $cutoff
    )
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

echo "Total licensed users          : $TOTAL_LICENSED"
echo "Disabled licensed users       : $DISABLED_LICENSED"
echo "Never successfully signed in  : $NEVER_USED"
echo "Inactive > $INACTIVE_DAYS days        : $INACTIVE"

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " RECOMMENDATION"
echo "============================================================"
echo

if [[ "$DISABLED_LICENSED" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🔴 Review disabled users with assigned licenses."
fi

if [[ "$NEVER_USED" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🔴 Review licensed users who have never successfully signed in."
fi

if [[ "$INACTIVE" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🟠 Review users inactive for more than $INACTIVE_DAYS days."
fi

if [[ "$DISABLED_LICENSED" -eq 0 &&
      "$NEVER_USED" -eq 0 &&
      "$INACTIVE" -eq 0 ]]; then

    echo "🟢 No obvious inactive licensed users detected."
fi

echo
echo "IMPORTANT:"
echo "Inactive does not automatically mean the license should be removed."
echo "Review business requirements before reclaiming a license."
echo

echo "============================================================"
echo " INACTIVE USER CHECK COMPLETED"
echo "============================================================"
echo

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

06 — License Utilization.

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 06 - LICENSE UTILIZATION
# ==============================================================
#
# Purpose:
#   Analyze Microsoft 365 license utilization based on
#   last successful user sign-in.
#
# Classification:
#
#   <= 30 days      ACTIVE
#   31 - 90 days    LOW USAGE
#   91 - 180 days   INACTIVE
#   > 180 days      STALE
#   Never signed    NEVER USED
#   Disabled        DISABLED
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#   - az login
#
# Graph API:
#   /v1.0/users
#
# Required permissions:
#   User.Read.All
#   AuditLog.Read.All
#
# ==============================================================

set -euo pipefail

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | M365 LICENSE UTILIZATION"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Check dependencies
# ------------------------------------------------------------

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Azure CLI is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: jq is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! az account show >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Please run 'az login' first."
    exit 1
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Tenant
# ------------------------------------------------------------

TENANT_ID=$(az account show --query tenantId -o tsv)

echo "Tenant ID : $TENANT_ID"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Temporary files
# ------------------------------------------------------------

USERS_FILE=$(mktemp)
SKUS_FILE=$(mktemp)

trap 'rm -f "$USERS_FILE" "$SKUS_FILE"' EXIT

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get SKU information
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Getting Microsoft 365 SKU information..."

az rest \
    --method get \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/subscribedSkus" \
    > "$SKUS_FILE"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get users
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Getting users and sign-in activity..."

az rest \
    --method get \
    --url 'https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/users?$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,accountEnabled,assignedLicenses,signInActivity&$top=500' \
    > "$USERS_FILE"

echo
echo "Analyzing license utilization..."
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Current time
# ------------------------------------------------------------

NOW_EPOCH=$(date -u +%s)

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Report
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " USER LICENSE UTILIZATION"
echo "============================================================"
echo

printf "%-28s %-35s %-32s %-15s\n" \
    "USER" \
    "UPN" \
    "LICENSE" \
    "UTILIZATION"

echo "----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Counters
# ------------------------------------------------------------

ACTIVE=0
LOW_USAGE=0
INACTIVE=0
STALE=0
NEVER_USED=0
DISABLED=0

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Process users
# ------------------------------------------------------------

jq -c '.value[]' "$USERS_FILE" |
while read -r USER
do

    DISPLAY_NAME=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.displayName // "Unknown"')

    UPN=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.userPrincipalName // "Unknown"')

    ACCOUNT_ENABLED=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.accountEnabled // false')

    LICENSE_COUNT=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq '.assignedLicenses | length')

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Ignore unlicensed users
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ "$LICENSE_COUNT" -eq 0 ]]; then
        continue
    fi

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Get license names
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    LICENSE_NAMES=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.assignedLicenses[]?.skuId' |
        while read -r SKU_ID
        do

            NAME=$(jq -r \
                --arg sku "$SKU_ID" \
                '
                .value[]
                | select(.skuId == $sku)
                | .skuPartNumber
                ' "$SKUS_FILE")

            [[ -z "$NAME" || "$NAME" == "null" ]] &&
                NAME="$SKU_ID"

            echo "$NAME"

        done |
        paste -sd "," -
    )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Disabled account
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ "$ACCOUNT_ENABLED" == "false" ]]; then

        STATUS="DISABLED"

        printf "%-28s %-35s %-32s %-15s\n" \
            "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
            "$UPN" \
            "$LICENSE_NAMES" \
            "$STATUS"

        continue
    fi

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Last successful sign-in
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    LAST_SIGNIN=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime // empty')

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Never used
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ -z "$LAST_SIGNIN" || "$LAST_SIGNIN" == "null" ]]; then

        STATUS="NEVER USED"

        printf "%-28s %-35s %-32s %-15s\n" \
            "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
            "$UPN" \
            "$LICENSE_NAMES" \
            "$STATUS"

        continue
    fi

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Convert last sign-in to epoch
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    LAST_EPOCH=$(date -u -d "$LAST_SIGNIN" +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)

    DAYS_UNUSED=$(( (NOW_EPOCH - LAST_EPOCH) / 86400 ))

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Classification
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ "$DAYS_UNUSED" -le 30 ]]; then

        STATUS="ACTIVE"

    elif [[ "$DAYS_UNUSED" -le 90 ]]; then

        STATUS="LOW USAGE"

    elif [[ "$DAYS_UNUSED" -le 180 ]]; then

        STATUS="INACTIVE"

    else

        STATUS="STALE"

    fi

    printf "%-28s %-35s %-32s %-15s\n" \
        "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
        "$UPN" \
        "$LICENSE_NAMES" \
        "$STATUS"

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Summary calculations
# ------------------------------------------------------------

ACTIVE_COUNT=$(jq --argjson now "$NOW_EPOCH" '
[
    .value[]
    | select((.assignedLicenses | length) > 0)
    | select(.accountEnabled == true)
    | select(.signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime != null)
    | .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime as $d
    | (($now - ($d | fromdateiso8601)) / 86400) as $days
    | select($days <= 30)
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

LOW_USAGE_COUNT=$(jq --argjson now "$NOW_EPOCH" '
[
    .value[]
    | select((.assignedLicenses | length) > 0)
    | select(.accountEnabled == true)
    | select(.signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime != null)
    | .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime as $d
    | (($now - ($d | fromdateiso8601)) / 86400) as $days
    | select($days > 30 and $days <= 90)
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

INACTIVE_COUNT=$(jq --argjson now "$NOW_EPOCH" '
[
    .value[]
    | select((.assignedLicenses | length) > 0)
    | select(.accountEnabled == true)
    | select(.signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime != null)
    | .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime as $d
    | (($now - ($d | fromdateiso8601)) / 86400) as $days
    | select($days > 90 and $days <= 180)
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

STALE_COUNT=$(jq --argjson now "$NOW_EPOCH" '
[
    .value[]
    | select((.assignedLicenses | length) > 0)
    | select(.accountEnabled == true)
    | select(.signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime != null)
    | .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime as $d
    | (($now - ($d | fromdateiso8601)) / 86400) as $days
    | select($days > 180)
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

NEVER_COUNT=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select((.assignedLicenses | length) > 0)
    | select(.accountEnabled == true)
    | select(
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == null
        or
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == ""
    )
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

DISABLED_COUNT=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select((.assignedLicenses | length) > 0)
    | select(.accountEnabled == false)
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

TOTAL_LICENSED=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select((.assignedLicenses | length) > 0)
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Summary
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " LICENSE UTILIZATION SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Total Licensed Users       : $TOTAL_LICENSED"
echo
echo "🟢 Active <= 30 days       : $ACTIVE_COUNT"
echo "🟡 Low Usage 31-90 days    : $LOW_USAGE_COUNT"
echo "🟠 Inactive 91-180 days    : $INACTIVE_COUNT"
echo "🔴 Stale > 180 days        : $STALE_COUNT"
echo "⚪ Never Used               : $NEVER_COUNT"
echo "🔴 Disabled                : $DISABLED_COUNT"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Calculate utilization percentage
# ------------------------------------------------------------

if [[ "$TOTAL_LICENSED" -gt 0 ]]; then

    ACTIVE_PERCENT=$(awk \
        -v a="$ACTIVE_COUNT" \
        -v t="$TOTAL_LICENSED" \
        'BEGIN { printf "%.1f", (a/t)*100 }')

else

    ACTIVE_PERCENT="0.0"

fi

echo
echo "Active License Utilization : ${ACTIVE_PERCENT}%"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Recommendations
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS RECOMMENDATIONS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

if [[ "$STALE_COUNT" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🔴 Review $STALE_COUNT stale licensed users."
fi

if [[ "$INACTIVE_COUNT" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🟠 Review $INACTIVE_COUNT inactive licensed users."
fi

if [[ "$NEVER_COUNT" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "⚪ Review $NEVER_COUNT licensed users who never signed in."
fi

if [[ "$DISABLED_COUNT" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🔴 Review $DISABLED_COUNT disabled users with licenses."
fi

if [[ "$LOW_USAGE_COUNT" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🟡 Review $LOW_USAGE_COUNT low-usage users."
fi

if [[ "$STALE_COUNT" -eq 0 &&
      "$INACTIVE_COUNT" -eq 0 &&
      "$NEVER_COUNT" -eq 0 &&
      "$DISABLED_COUNT" -eq 0 ]]; then

    echo "🟢 No obvious license-utilization issues detected."

fi

echo
echo "NOTE:"
echo "Usage classification is based on successful Entra sign-in."
echo "It does not prove whether a user actively uses every M365"
echo "workload such as Exchange, Teams, SharePoint or OneDrive."
echo
echo "Review business requirements before removing licenses."
echo

echo "============================================================"
echo " LICENSE UTILIZATION CHECK COMPLETED"
echo "============================================================"
echo
-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

07 — Privileged / Admin Users.

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 07 - PRIVILEGED / ADMIN USERS
# ==============================================================
#
# Purpose:
#   Identify users assigned to Microsoft Entra directory roles.
#
# APIs:
#   Microsoft Graph:
#   /v1.0/directoryRoles
#   /v1.0/directoryRoles/{id}/members
#   /v1.0/users/{id}
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#   - az login
#
# Recommended permissions:
#   RoleManagement.Read.Directory
#   Directory.Read.All
#
# ==============================================================

set -euo pipefail

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | M365 PRIVILEGED / ADMIN USERS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Dependencies
# ------------------------------------------------------------

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Azure CLI is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: jq is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! az account show >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Please run 'az login' first."
    exit 1
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Tenant
# ------------------------------------------------------------

TENANT_ID=$(az account show --query tenantId -o tsv)

echo "Tenant ID : $TENANT_ID"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Temporary files
# ------------------------------------------------------------

ROLES_FILE=$(mktemp)
trap 'rm -f "$ROLES_FILE"' EXIT

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get active directory roles
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Getting Microsoft Entra directory roles..."

az rest \
    --method get \
    --url 'https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/directoryRoles?$select=id,displayName,description' \
    > "$ROLES_FILE"

echo
echo "Processing privileged roles..."
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Header
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " PRIVILEGED USERS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

printf "%-28s %-38s %-38s %-12s\n" \
    "ROLE" \
    "USER" \
    "UPN" \
    "ACCOUNT"

echo "----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Counters
# ------------------------------------------------------------

TOTAL_ADMIN_ASSIGNMENTS=0

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Process roles
# ------------------------------------------------------------

jq -c '.value[]' "$ROLES_FILE" |
while read -r ROLE
do

    ROLE_ID=$(echo "$ROLE" |
        jq -r '.id')

    ROLE_NAME=$(echo "$ROLE" |
        jq -r '.displayName // "Unknown Role"')

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Get role members
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    MEMBERS=$(az rest \
        --method get \
        --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/directoryRoles/$ROLE_ID/members?\$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,accountEnabled,userType" \
        2>/dev/null || echo '{"value":[]}')

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Process members
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    echo "$MEMBERS" |
    jq -c '.value[]?' |
    while read -r MEMBER
    do

        MEMBER_TYPE=$(echo "$MEMBER" |
            jq -r 'if .userPrincipalName then "User" else "Other" end')

        if [[ "$MEMBER_TYPE" != "User" ]]; then
            continue
        fi

        DISPLAY_NAME=$(echo "$MEMBER" |
            jq -r '.displayName // "Unknown"')

        UPN=$(echo "$MEMBER" |
            jq -r '.userPrincipalName // "Unknown"')

        ACCOUNT_ENABLED=$(echo "$MEMBER" |
            jq -r '.accountEnabled // false')

        USER_TYPE=$(echo "$MEMBER" |
            jq -r '.userType // "Unknown"')

        if [[ "$ACCOUNT_ENABLED" == "true" ]]; then
            STATUS="Active"
        else
            STATUS="Disabled"
        fi

        printf "%-28s %-38s %-38s %-12s\n" \
            "$ROLE_NAME" \
            "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
            "$UPN" \
            "$STATUS"

    done

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Summary
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " PRIVILEGED ACCESS SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get all role assignments into a temporary JSON file
# ------------------------------------------------------------

ALL_ASSIGNMENTS=$(mktemp)
trap 'rm -f "$ROLES_FILE" "$ALL_ASSIGNMENTS"' EXIT

echo '{"value":[]}' > "$ALL_ASSIGNMENTS"

jq -c '.value[]' "$ROLES_FILE" |
while read -r ROLE
do

    ROLE_ID=$(echo "$ROLE" |
        jq -r '.id')

    ROLE_NAME=$(echo "$ROLE" |
        jq -r '.displayName')

    MEMBERS=$(az rest \
        --method get \
        --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/directoryRoles/$ROLE_ID/members?\$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,accountEnabled" \
        2>/dev/null || echo '{"value":[]}')

    echo "$MEMBERS" |
    jq --arg role "$ROLE_NAME" '
        .value[]
        | select(.userPrincipalName != null)
        | {
            id: .id,
            displayName: .displayName,
            userPrincipalName: .userPrincipalName,
            accountEnabled: .accountEnabled,
            role: $role
        }
    ' >> /tmp/zenaiops_admin_records.json 2>/dev/null || true

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Simpler summary directly from role data
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Active Directory Roles found : $(jq '.value | length' "$ROLES_FILE")"

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS SECURITY FINDINGS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Review the following conditions:"
echo
echo "🔴 Global Administrator accounts"
echo "🔴 Privileged Role Administrator accounts"
echo "🟠 Users with multiple privileged roles"
echo "🟠 Disabled users retaining privileged roles"
echo "🟡 Excessive administrator assignments"
echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " PRIVILEGED USER CHECK COMPLETED"
echo "============================================================"
echo

-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

08 — MFA Coverage.

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 08 - MFA COVERAGE
# ==============================================================
#
# Purpose:
#   Identify users with / without registered MFA methods.
#
# Graph APIs:
#   /v1.0/users
#   /v1.0/users/{id}/authentication/methods
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#   - az login
#
# Recommended Graph permissions:
#   User.Read.All
#   UserAuthenticationMethod.Read.All
#
# ==============================================================

set -euo pipefail

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | M365 MFA COVERAGE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Dependencies
# ------------------------------------------------------------

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Azure CLI is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: jq is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! az account show >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Please run 'az login' first."
    exit 1
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Tenant
# ------------------------------------------------------------

TENANT_ID=$(az account show --query tenantId -o tsv)

echo "Tenant ID : $TENANT_ID"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Temporary files
# ------------------------------------------------------------

USERS_FILE=$(mktemp)

trap 'rm -f "$USERS_FILE"' EXIT

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get users
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Getting Microsoft 365 users..."

az rest \
    --method get \
    --url 'https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/users?$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,accountEnabled,userType&$top=999' \
    > "$USERS_FILE"

TOTAL_USERS=$(jq '.value | length' "$USERS_FILE")

echo "Users found : $TOTAL_USERS"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Report Header
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " MFA USER STATUS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

printf "%-28s %-38s %-12s %-30s\n" \
    "USER" \
    "UPN" \
    "MFA" \
    "METHODS"

echo "----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Counters
# ------------------------------------------------------------

MFA_ENABLED=0
MFA_MISSING=0
DISABLED_USERS=0
ERROR_USERS=0

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Process users
# ------------------------------------------------------------

jq -c '.value[]' "$USERS_FILE" |
while read -r USER
do

    USER_ID=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.id')

    DISPLAY_NAME=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.displayName // "Unknown"')

    UPN=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.userPrincipalName // "Unknown"')

    ACCOUNT_ENABLED=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.accountEnabled // false')

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Skip disabled accounts
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ "$ACCOUNT_ENABLED" == "false" ]]; then

        printf "%-28s %-38s %-12s %-30s\n" \
            "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
            "$UPN" \
            "DISABLED" \
            "-"

        continue

    fi

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Get authentication methods
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    METHODS=$(az rest \
        --method get \
        --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/users/$USER_ID/authentication/methods" \
        2>/dev/null || echo '{"value":[]}')

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Detect MFA-capable methods
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    #
    # We intentionally exclude password authentication.
    #
    # Common MFA-capable methods:
    #   microsoftAuthenticatorAuthenticationMethod
    #   phoneAuthenticationMethod
    #   fido2AuthenticationMethod
    #   windowsHelloForBusinessAuthenticationMethod
    #   softwareOathAuthenticationMethod
    #   temporaryAccessPassAuthenticationMethod
    #
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    MFA_METHODS=$(echo "$METHODS" |
        jq -r '
        [
            .value[]?.["@odata.type"]
            | select(
                . == "#microsoft.graph.microsoftAuthenticatorAuthenticationMethod"
                or
                . == "#microsoft.graph.phoneAuthenticationMethod"
                or
                . == "#microsoft.graph.fido2AuthenticationMethod"
                or
                . == "#microsoft.graph.windowsHelloForBusinessAuthenticationMethod"
                or
                . == "#microsoft.graph.softwareOathAuthenticationMethod"
                or
                . == "#microsoft.graph.temporaryAccessPassAuthenticationMethod"
            )
        ]
        | unique
        | map(
            sub("^#microsoft.graph."; "")
            | sub("AuthenticationMethod$"; "")
        )
        | join(", ")
        ' 2>/dev/null || echo "")

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Determine status
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ -n "$MFA_METHODS" ]]; then

        STATUS="ENABLED"

        printf "%-28s %-38s %-12s %-30s\n" \
            "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
            "$UPN" \
            "$STATUS" \
            "$MFA_METHODS"

    else

        STATUS="MISSING"

        printf "%-28s %-38s %-12s %-30s\n" \
            "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
            "$UPN" \
            "$STATUS" \
            "No MFA method"

    fi

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Summary
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " MFA COVERAGE SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "NOTE:"
echo "The summary below is calculated from active users."
echo

ACTIVE_USERS=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select(.accountEnabled == true)
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

echo "Total users              : $TOTAL_USERS"
echo "Active users             : $ACTIVE_USERS"
echo "Disabled users           : $((TOTAL_USERS - ACTIVE_USERS))"

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " MFA ANALYSIS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "The detailed section above identifies each user's"
echo "registered MFA-capable authentication methods."
echo
echo "For production ZenAiOps, the next version should aggregate"
echo "these results into:"
echo
echo "  MFA Enabled"
echo "  MFA Missing"
echo "  MFA Coverage %"
echo "  Admins without MFA"
echo "  Guest users without MFA"
echo "  Privileged users without MFA"
echo

echo "============================================================"
echo " MFA COVERAGE CHECK COMPLETED"
echo "============================================================"
echo

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
09 — Guest Users & External Access.

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 09 - GUEST USERS
# ==============================================================
#
# Purpose:
#   Identify Microsoft Entra B2B guest users and provide a
#   security/governance view of external identities.
#
# Graph API:
#   /v1.0/users
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#   - az login
#
# Recommended permissions:
#   User.Read.All
#   AuditLog.Read.All
#   UserAuthenticationMethod.Read.All
#
# ==============================================================

set -euo pipefail

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | M365 GUEST USERS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Dependencies
# ------------------------------------------------------------

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Azure CLI is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: jq is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! az account show >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Please run 'az login' first."
    exit 1
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Tenant
# ------------------------------------------------------------

TENANT_ID=$(az account show --query tenantId -o tsv)

echo "Tenant ID : $TENANT_ID"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Temporary files
# ------------------------------------------------------------

GUEST_FILE=$(mktemp)

trap 'rm -f "$GUEST_FILE"' EXIT

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get guest users
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Getting Microsoft Entra guest users..."

az rest \
    --method get \
    --url 'https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/users?$filter=userType%20eq%20%27Guest%27&$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,mail,userType,accountEnabled,createdDateTime,signInActivity,assignedLicenses&$top=500' \
    > "$GUEST_FILE"

GUEST_COUNT=$(jq '.value | length' "$GUEST_FILE")

echo "Guest users found : $GUEST_COUNT"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Header
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " GUEST USER INVENTORY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

printf "%-27s %-38s %-12s %-22s %-12s\n" \
    "GUEST" \
    "EMAIL / UPN" \
    "STATUS" \
    "LAST SIGN-IN" \
    "LICENSED"

echo "----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Process guests
# ------------------------------------------------------------

jq -c '.value[]' "$GUEST_FILE" |
while read -r GUEST
do

    DISPLAY_NAME=$(echo "$GUEST" |
        jq -r '.displayName // "Unknown"')

    UPN=$(echo "$GUEST" |
        jq -r '.userPrincipalName // "Unknown"')

    ACCOUNT_ENABLED=$(echo "$GUEST" |
        jq -r '.accountEnabled // false')

    CREATED=$(echo "$GUEST" |
        jq -r '.createdDateTime // "Unknown"')

    LAST_SIGNIN=$(echo "$GUEST" |
        jq -r '.signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime // empty')

    LICENSE_COUNT=$(echo "$GUEST" |
        jq '.assignedLicenses | length')

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Account status
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ "$ACCOUNT_ENABLED" == "true" ]]; then
        STATUS="Active"
    else
        STATUS="Disabled"
    fi

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Last sign-in
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ -z "$LAST_SIGNIN" ]]; then
        LAST_SIGNIN_DISPLAY="Never"
    else
        LAST_SIGNIN_DISPLAY="$LAST_SIGNIN"
    fi

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # License status
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ "$LICENSE_COUNT" -gt 0 ]]; then
        LICENSED="YES"
    else
        LICENSED="NO"
    fi

    printf "%-27s %-38s %-12s %-22s %-12s\n" \
        "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
        "$UPN" \
        "$STATUS" \
        "$LAST_SIGNIN_DISPLAY" \
        "$LICENSED"

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Summary
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " GUEST USER SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

ACTIVE_GUESTS=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select(.accountEnabled == true)
]
| length
' "$GUEST_FILE")

DISABLED_GUESTS=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select(.accountEnabled == false)
]
| length
' "$GUEST_FILE")

NEVER_SIGNED_IN=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select(
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == null
        or
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == ""
    )
]
| length
' "$GUEST_FILE")

LICENSED_GUESTS=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select((.assignedLicenses | length) > 0)
]
| length
' "$GUEST_FILE")

echo "Total Guest Users            : $GUEST_COUNT"
echo "Active Guest Users           : $ACTIVE_GUESTS"
echo "Disabled Guest Users         : $DISABLED_GUESTS"
echo "Never Successfully Signed In : $NEVER_SIGNED_IN"
echo "Licensed Guest Users         : $LICENSED_GUESTS"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Findings
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS GUEST SECURITY FINDINGS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

if [[ "$DISABLED_GUESTS" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🟠 $DISABLED_GUESTS disabled guest accounts found."
fi

if [[ "$NEVER_SIGNED_IN" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🟡 $NEVER_SIGNED_IN guest accounts have never successfully signed in."
fi

if [[ "$LICENSED_GUESTS" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🟠 $LICENSED_GUESTS guest accounts have assigned licenses."
fi

if [[ "$DISABLED_GUESTS" -eq 0 &&
      "$NEVER_SIGNED_IN" -eq 0 &&
      "$LICENSED_GUESTS" -eq 0 ]]; then

    echo "🟢 No obvious guest-account issues detected."

fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " RECOMMENDED REVIEW"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Review:"
echo "  1. Guests who no longer require access"
echo "  2. Guests who have never signed in"
echo "  3. Disabled guests"
echo "  4. Licensed guest accounts"
echo "  5. Long-inactive guest accounts"
echo "  6. Guest access to sensitive groups/sites/Teams"
echo

echo "NOTE:"
echo "Guest status alone does not indicate whether access is risky."
echo "The next version can correlate guests with group, Teams,"
echo "SharePoint and application access."
echo

echo "============================================================"
echo " GUEST USER CHECK COMPLETED"
echo "============================================================"
echo

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

10 — Stale / Disabled Users.

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 10 - STALE / DISABLED USERS
# ==============================================================
#
# Purpose:
#   Identify stale, inactive, disabled and never-used users
#   in Microsoft 365 / Microsoft Entra ID.
#
# Usage:
#   ./10-M365-Stale-Users.sh
#   ./10-M365-Stale-Users.sh 90
#   ./10-M365-Stale-Users.sh 180
#
# Default:
#   90 days
#
# APIs:
#   Microsoft Graph v1.0/users
#
# Recommended permissions:
#   User.Read.All
#   AuditLog.Read.All
#
# ==============================================================

set -euo pipefail

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Configuration
# ------------------------------------------------------------

STALE_DAYS="${1:-90}"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Validate input
# ------------------------------------------------------------

if ! [[ "$STALE_DAYS" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    echo "ERROR: Stale days must be a number."
    echo
    echo "Usage:"
    echo "  $0 90"
    echo "  $0 180"
    exit 1
fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | M365 STALE / DISABLED USERS"
echo "============================================================"
echo
echo "Stale threshold : $STALE_DAYS days"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Dependencies
# ------------------------------------------------------------

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Azure CLI is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: jq is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! az account show >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Please run 'az login' first."
    exit 1
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Tenant
# ------------------------------------------------------------

TENANT_ID=$(az account show --query tenantId -o tsv)

echo "Tenant ID : $TENANT_ID"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Temporary file
# ------------------------------------------------------------

USERS_FILE=$(mktemp)

trap 'rm -f "$USERS_FILE"' EXIT

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get users
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Getting Microsoft 365 users..."

az rest \
    --method get \
    --url 'https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/users?$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,mail,userType,accountEnabled,createdDateTime,assignedLicenses,signInActivity&$top=500' \
    > "$USERS_FILE"

USER_COUNT=$(jq '.value | length' "$USERS_FILE")

echo "Users found : $USER_COUNT"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Calculate cutoff
# ------------------------------------------------------------

NOW_EPOCH=$(date -u +%s)

CUTOFF_EPOCH=$(date \
    -u \
    -d "$STALE_DAYS days ago" \
    +%s)

CUTOFF_DATE=$(date \
    -u \
    -d "$STALE_DAYS days ago" \
    +"%Y-%m-%d")

echo "Stale cutoff date : $CUTOFF_DATE"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Header
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " USER ACCOUNT HYGIENE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

printf "%-27s %-38s %-12s %-22s %-12s\n" \
    "USER" \
    "UPN" \
    "ACCOUNT" \
    "LAST SUCCESSFUL SIGN-IN" \
    "LICENSE"

echo "----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Process users
# ------------------------------------------------------------

jq -c '.value[]' "$USERS_FILE" |
while read -r USER
do

    DISPLAY_NAME=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.displayName // "Unknown"')

    UPN=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.userPrincipalName // "Unknown"')

    ACCOUNT_ENABLED=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.accountEnabled // false')

    USER_TYPE=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.userType // "Member"')

    LAST_SIGNIN=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime // empty')

    LICENSE_COUNT=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq '.assignedLicenses | length')

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # License
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ "$LICENSE_COUNT" -gt 0 ]]; then
        LICENSE="YES"
    else
        LICENSE="NO"
    fi

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Disabled user
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ "$ACCOUNT_ENABLED" == "false" ]]; then

        printf "%-27s %-38s %-12s %-22s %-12s\n" \
            "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
            "$UPN" \
            "DISABLED" \
            "${LAST_SIGNIN:-Never}" \
            "$LICENSE"

        continue
    fi

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Never used
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ -z "$LAST_SIGNIN" || "$LAST_SIGNIN" == "null" ]]; then

        printf "%-27s %-38s %-12s %-22s %-12s\n" \
            "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
            "$UPN" \
            "NEVER USED" \
            "Never" \
            "$LICENSE"

        continue
    fi

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Convert sign-in time
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    LAST_EPOCH=$(date \
        -u \
        -d "$LAST_SIGNIN" \
        +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)

    DAYS_SINCE=$(( \
        (NOW_EPOCH - LAST_EPOCH) / 86400 \
    ))

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Stale account
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ "$DAYS_SINCE" -gt "$STALE_DAYS" ]]; then

        printf "%-27s %-38s %-12s %-22s %-12s\n" \
            "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
            "$UPN" \
            "STALE" \
            "$LAST_SIGNIN" \
            "$LICENSE"

    fi

done

# ============================================================
# SUMMARY
# ============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " USER HYGIENE SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

TOTAL_USERS=$(jq '.value | length' "$USERS_FILE")

ACTIVE_USERS=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select(.accountEnabled == true)
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

DISABLED_USERS=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select(.accountEnabled == false)
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

NEVER_USED_USERS=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select(
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == null
        or
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == ""
    )
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

STALE_USERS=$(jq --argjson cutoff "$CUTOFF_EPOCH" '
[
    .value[]
    | select(.accountEnabled == true)
    | select(
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime != null
        and
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime != ""
    )
    | select(
        (.signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime | fromdateiso8601)
        < $cutoff
    )
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

DISABLED_WITH_LICENSE=$(jq '
[
    .value[]
    | select(.accountEnabled == false)
    | select((.assignedLicenses | length) > 0)
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

STALE_WITH_LICENSE=$(jq --argjson cutoff "$CUTOFF_EPOCH" '
[
    .value[]
    | select(.accountEnabled == true)
    | select((.assignedLicenses | length) > 0)
    | select(
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime != null
        and
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime != ""
    )
    | select(
        (.signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime | fromdateiso8601)
        < $cutoff
    )
]
| length
' "$USERS_FILE")

echo "Total Users                    : $TOTAL_USERS"
echo "Active Users                   : $ACTIVE_USERS"
echo "Disabled Users                 : $DISABLED_USERS"
echo "Never Used Users               : $NEVER_USED_USERS"
echo "Stale Users > $STALE_DAYS days        : $STALE_USERS"
echo "Disabled Users With License    : $DISABLED_WITH_LICENSE"
echo "Stale Users With License       : $STALE_WITH_LICENSE"

# ============================================================
# SECURITY FINDINGS
# ============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS SECURITY FINDINGS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

if [[ "$DISABLED_USERS" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🔴 Disabled accounts found : $DISABLED_USERS"
fi

if [[ "$DISABLED_WITH_LICENSE" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🔴 Disabled users still consuming licenses : $DISABLED_WITH_LICENSE"
fi

if [[ "$STALE_USERS" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🟠 Stale active accounts found : $STALE_USERS"
fi

if [[ "$STALE_WITH_LICENSE" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🟠 Stale licensed accounts found : $STALE_WITH_LICENSE"
fi

if [[ "$NEVER_USED_USERS" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🟡 Never-used accounts found : $NEVER_USED_USERS"
fi

if [[ "$DISABLED_USERS" -eq 0 &&
      "$STALE_USERS" -eq 0 &&
      "$NEVER_USED_USERS" -eq 0 ]]; then

    echo "🟢 No obvious stale-account issues detected."

fi

# ============================================================
# RECOMMENDATIONS
# ============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS RECOMMENDATIONS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Review the following:"
echo
echo "1. Disabled accounts"
echo "2. Stale active accounts"
echo "3. Never-used accounts"
echo "4. Disabled accounts with licenses"
echo "5. Stale accounts with licenses"
echo "6. Guest accounts separately"
echo
echo "Do not automatically delete or disable accounts based only"
echo "on inactivity. Validate business requirements first."
echo

echo "============================================================"
echo " STALE USER CHECK COMPLETED"
echo "============================================================"
echo

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
11 — Risky Users & Risk Detections.

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 11 - RISKY USERS & RISK DETECTIONS
# ==============================================================
#
# Purpose:
#   Identify Microsoft Entra ID Protection risky users and
#   recent risk detections.
#
# APIs:
#   GET /v1.0/identityProtection/riskyUsers
#   GET /v1.0/identityProtection/riskDetections
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#   - az login
#
# Required Graph permissions:
#   IdentityRiskyUser.Read.All
#   IdentityRiskEvent.Read.All
#
# Licensing:
#   Risk detection API -> Entra ID P1/P2
#   Risky users API    -> Entra ID P2
#
# ==============================================================

set -euo pipefail

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | M365 RISKY USERS & RISK DETECTIONS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Dependencies
# ------------------------------------------------------------

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Azure CLI is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: jq is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! az account show >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Please run 'az login' first."
    exit 1
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Tenant
# ------------------------------------------------------------

TENANT_ID=$(az account show --query tenantId -o tsv)

echo "Tenant ID : $TENANT_ID"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Temporary files
# ------------------------------------------------------------

RISKY_USERS_FILE=$(mktemp)
RISK_DETECTIONS_FILE=$(mktemp)

trap 'rm -f "$RISKY_USERS_FILE" "$RISK_DETECTIONS_FILE"' EXIT

# ============================================================
# 1. RISKY USERS
# ============================================================

echo "Getting risky users..."

RISKY_USERS_RESPONSE=$(az rest \
    --method get \
    --url 'https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/identityProtection/riskyUsers?$top=500' \
    2>&1) || true

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Check API response
# ------------------------------------------------------------

if echo "$RISKY_USERS_RESPONSE" | jq -e '.error' >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo
    echo "WARNING: Unable to retrieve risky users."
    echo
    echo "$RISKY_USERS_RESPONSE" |
        jq -r '.error.message // "Microsoft Graph returned an error."' 2>/dev/null ||
        echo "$RISKY_USERS_RESPONSE"

    echo
    echo "Possible causes:"
    echo "  - Entra ID P2 license not available"
    echo "  - Missing IdentityRiskyUser.Read.All permission"
    echo "  - Insufficient Entra role"
    echo

    echo '{"value":[]}' > "$RISKY_USERS_FILE"

else

    echo "$RISKY_USERS_RESPONSE" > "$RISKY_USERS_FILE"

fi

# ============================================================
# 2. RISK DETECTIONS
# ============================================================

echo "Getting risk detections..."

RISK_DETECTIONS_RESPONSE=$(az rest \
    --method get \
    --url 'https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/identityProtection/riskDetections?$top=500' \
    2>&1) || true

if echo "$RISK_DETECTIONS_RESPONSE" | jq -e '.error' >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo
    echo "WARNING: Unable to retrieve risk detections."
    echo

    echo "$RISK_DETECTIONS_RESPONSE" |
        jq -r '.error.message // "Microsoft Graph returned an error."' 2>/dev/null ||
        echo "$RISK_DETECTIONS_RESPONSE"

    echo

    echo "Possible causes:"
    echo "  - Entra ID P1/P2 license not available"
    echo "  - Missing IdentityRiskEvent.Read.All permission"
    echo "  - Insufficient Entra role"
    echo

    echo '{"value":[]}' > "$RISK_DETECTIONS_FILE"

else

    echo "$RISK_DETECTIONS_RESPONSE" > "$RISK_DETECTIONS_FILE"

fi

# ============================================================
# RISKY USER REPORT
# ============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " RISKY USERS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

printf "%-28s %-38s %-12s %-18s %-30s\n" \
    "USER" \
    "UPN" \
    "RISK LEVEL" \
    "RISK STATE" \
    "RISK DETAIL"

echo "----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------"

jq -r '
.value[]? |
[
    (.userDisplayName // "Unknown"),
    (.userPrincipalName // "Unknown"),
    (.riskLevel // "unknown"),
    (.riskState // "unknown"),
