    (.riskDetail // "unknown")
] |
@tsv
' "$RISKY_USERS_FILE" |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r USER UPN LEVEL STATE DETAIL
do

    printf "%-28s %-38s %-12s %-18s %-30s\n" \
        "$USER" \
        "$UPN" \
        "$LEVEL" \
        "$STATE" \
        "$DETAIL"

done

# ============================================================
# RISK DETECTION REPORT
# ============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " RISK DETECTIONS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

printf "%-28s %-20s %-12s %-28s %-20s\n" \
    "USER" \
    "RISK EVENT" \
    "LEVEL" \
    "ACTIVITY" \
    "DATE"

echo "----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------"

jq -r '
.value[]? |
[
    (.userDisplayName // "Unknown"),
    (.riskEventType // "Unknown"),
    (.riskLevel // "unknown"),
    (.activity // "Unknown"),
    (.activityDateTime // "Unknown")
] |
@tsv
' "$RISK_DETECTIONS_FILE" |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r USER EVENT LEVEL ACTIVITY DATE
do

    printf "%-28s %-20s %-12s %-28s %-20s\n" \
        "$USER" \
        "$EVENT" \
        "$LEVEL" \
        "$ACTIVITY" \
        "$DATE"

done

# ============================================================
# SUMMARY
# ============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " RISK SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

TOTAL_RISKY_USERS=$(jq '
.value
| length
' "$RISKY_USERS_FILE")

HIGH_RISK_USERS=$(jq '
[
    .value[]?
    | select(.riskLevel == "high")
]
| length
' "$RISKY_USERS_FILE")

MEDIUM_RISK_USERS=$(jq '
[
    .value[]?
    | select(.riskLevel == "medium")
]
| length
' "$RISKY_USERS_FILE")

LOW_RISK_USERS=$(jq '
[
    .value[]?
    | select(.riskLevel == "low")
]
| length
' "$RISKY_USERS_FILE")

AT_RISK_USERS=$(jq '
[
    .value[]?
    | select(.riskState == "atRisk")
]
| length
' "$RISKY_USERS_FILE")

TOTAL_DETECTIONS=$(jq '
.value
| length
' "$RISK_DETECTIONS_FILE")

echo "Total Risky Users       : $TOTAL_RISKY_USERS"
echo "High Risk Users         : $HIGH_RISK_USERS"
echo "Medium Risk Users       : $MEDIUM_RISK_USERS"
echo "Low Risk Users          : $LOW_RISK_USERS"
echo "Users Currently At Risk: $AT_RISK_USERS"
echo "Risk Detections         : $TOTAL_DETECTIONS"

# ============================================================
# FINDINGS
# ============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS SECURITY FINDINGS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

if [[ "$HIGH_RISK_USERS" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🔴 HIGH RISK USERS : $HIGH_RISK_USERS"
fi

if [[ "$MEDIUM_RISK_USERS" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🟠 MEDIUM RISK USERS : $MEDIUM_RISK_USERS"
fi

if [[ "$LOW_RISK_USERS" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "🟡 LOW RISK USERS : $LOW_RISK_USERS"
fi

if [[ "$TOTAL_RISKY_USERS" -eq 0 ]]; then
    echo "🟢 No risky users returned by Microsoft Entra ID Protection."
fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " RECOMMENDED ACTION"
echo "============================================================"
echo

if [[ "$HIGH_RISK_USERS" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "Review high-risk identities immediately."
fi

if [[ "$AT_RISK_USERS" -gt 0 ]]; then
    echo "Review users currently marked 'atRisk'."
fi

echo
echo "Do not automatically disable or remediate users solely"
echo "from this report. Validate the Microsoft risk signal and"
echo "customer security process first."
echo

echo "============================================================"
echo " RISK CHECK COMPLETED"
echo "============================================================"
echo

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
12 — Mailbox External Forwarding.

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 12 - MAILBOX EXTERNAL FORWARDING
# ==============================================================
#
# Purpose:
#   Identify Microsoft 365 users with mailbox forwarding
#   configured to an external address.
#
# Graph API:
#   /v1.0/users/{id}/mailboxSettings
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#   - az login
#
# Recommended Graph permission:
#   User.Read.All
#   MailboxSettings.Read
#
# IMPORTANT:
#   This check focuses on mailbox forwarding.
#   Inbox rules that redirect/forward mail are a separate check.
#
# ==============================================================

set -euo pipefail

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | M365 MAILBOX EXTERNAL FORWARDING"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Dependencies
# ------------------------------------------------------------

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Azure CLI is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: jq is not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! az account show >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: Please run 'az login' first."
    exit 1
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Tenant
# ------------------------------------------------------------

TENANT_ID=$(az account show --query tenantId -o tsv)

echo "Tenant ID : $TENANT_ID"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Temporary file
# ------------------------------------------------------------

USERS_FILE=$(mktemp)

trap 'rm -f "$USERS_FILE"' EXIT

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Get users with mailboxes
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Getting Microsoft 365 users..."

az rest \
    --method get \
    --url 'https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/users?$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,mail,accountEnabled&$top=500' \
    > "$USERS_FILE"

USER_COUNT=$(jq '.value | length' "$USERS_FILE")

echo "Users found : $USER_COUNT"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Header
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " MAILBOX FORWARDING CHECK"
echo "============================================================"
echo

printf "%-27s %-38s %-12s %-40s\n" \
    "USER" \
    "UPN" \
    "STATUS" \
    "FORWARDING"

echo "----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Counters
# ------------------------------------------------------------

FORWARDING_FOUND=0
FORWARDING_EXTERNAL=0
NO_FORWARDING=0
CHECK_ERRORS=0

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Process users
# ------------------------------------------------------------

jq -c '.value[]' "$USERS_FILE" |
while read -r USER
do

    USER_ID=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.id')

    DISPLAY_NAME=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.displayName // "Unknown"')

    UPN=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.userPrincipalName // "Unknown"')

    ACCOUNT_ENABLED=$(echo "$USER" |
        jq -r '.accountEnabled // false')

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Skip disabled users
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ "$ACCOUNT_ENABLED" == "false" ]]; then
        continue
    fi

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Get mailbox settings
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    MAILBOX_RESPONSE=$(az rest \
        --method get \
        --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/users/$USER_ID/mailboxSettings" \
        2>/dev/null || echo '{"error":{"message":"Unable to read mailbox settings"}}')

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Check for API error
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if echo "$MAILBOX_RESPONSE" | jq -e '.error' >/dev/null 2>&1; then

        ((CHECK_ERRORS+=1)) || true

        continue

    fi

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Extract forwarding address
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    FORWARDING_ADDRESS=$(echo "$MAILBOX_RESPONSE" |
        jq -r '.forwardingSmtpAddress // empty')

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # No forwarding
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ -z "$FORWARDING_ADDRESS" ]]; then

        continue

    fi

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Determine external forwarding
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    USER_DOMAIN="${UPN#*@}"
    FORWARD_DOMAIN="${FORWARDING_ADDRESS#*@}"

    if [[ "${FORWARD_DOMAIN,,}" != "${USER_DOMAIN,,}" ]]; then

        STATUS="EXTERNAL"

        echo
        printf "%-27s %-38s %-12s %-40s\n" \
            "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
            "$UPN" \
            "$STATUS" \
            "$FORWARDING_ADDRESS"

    else

        STATUS="INTERNAL"

        printf "%-27s %-38s %-12s %-40s\n" \
            "$DISPLAY_NAME" \
            "$UPN" \
            "$STATUS" \
            "$FORWARDING_ADDRESS"

    fi

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Summary
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " MAILBOX FORWARDING SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Users checked : $USER_COUNT"
echo "API errors    : $CHECK_ERRORS"

echo
echo "IMPORTANT:"
echo "The first version reports mailbox-level forwarding."
echo "It does not yet inspect Inbox rules."
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Findings
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS SECURITY FINDINGS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Review any external mailbox forwarding configuration."
echo
echo "Potential concerns:"
echo "  🔴 Unauthorized external forwarding"
echo "  🟠 Forwarding to personal email"
echo "  🟠 Forwarding to unknown domains"
echo "  🟡 Business-approved external forwarding"
echo

echo "Do not automatically disable forwarding."
echo "Validate the business requirement first."
echo

echo "============================================================"
echo " MAILBOX FORWARDING CHECK COMPLETED"
echo "============================================================"
echo
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

13 — Microsoft 365 Email Security Baseline

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS 13 - MICROSOFT 365 EMAIL SECURITY BASELINE
# ==============================================================
#
# Checks:
#   01. SPF
#   02. DMARC
#   03. DKIM
#   04. Anti-Spam
#   05. Anti-Phishing
#   06. DMARC enforcement
#
# Requirements:
#   - bash
#   - jq
#   - dig OR nslookup
#   - pwsh
#   - ExchangeOnlineManagement PowerShell module
#
# Usage:
#   ./13-m365-email-security.sh
#
# ==============================================================

set -u

PASS=0
WARN=0
FAIL=0

TMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)

cleanup() {
    rm -rf "$TMP_DIR"
}
trap cleanup EXIT


# ==============================================================
# FUNCTIONS
# ==============================================================

pass() {
    echo "   ✅ $1"
    PASS=$((PASS + 1))
}

warn() {
    echo "   ⚠️  $1"
    WARN=$((WARN + 1))
}

fail() {
    echo "   ❌ $1"
    FAIL=$((FAIL + 1))
}


# ==============================================================
# REQUIREMENTS
# ==============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 13 - EMAIL SECURITY BASELINE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: jq is required."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v pwsh >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR: PowerShell 7 (pwsh) is required."
    echo
    echo "Install PowerShell and ExchangeOnlineManagement module."
    exit 1
fi


# ==============================================================
# GET TENANT DOMAIN
# ==============================================================

echo "Detecting Microsoft 365 domain..."

DOMAIN=$(az rest \
    --method get \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/domains" \
    2>/dev/null |
    jq -r '
        .value[]
        | select(.isDefault == true)
        | .id
    ' | head -1)

if [[ -z "$DOMAIN" || "$DOMAIN" == "null" ]]; then
    echo "ERROR: Unable to determine default Microsoft 365 domain."
    exit 1
fi

echo "Domain : $DOMAIN"
echo


# ==============================================================
# 01 - SPF
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "01. SPF"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

SPF_RECORD=""

if command -v dig >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    SPF_RECORD=$(dig +short TXT "$DOMAIN" 2>/dev/null |
        grep -i "v=spf1" |
        tr -d '"')

elif command -v nslookup >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    SPF_RECORD=$(nslookup -type=TXT "$DOMAIN" 2>/dev/null |
        grep -i "v=spf1" |
        sed 's/.*text = //' |
        tr -d '"')
fi

if [[ -n "$SPF_RECORD" ]]; then

    echo "   SPF Record:"
    echo "   $SPF_RECORD"

    if echo "$SPF_RECORD" | grep -qi "v=spf1"; then
        pass "SPF record detected"
    else
        fail "Invalid SPF record"
    fi

else
    fail "SPF record not found"
fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 02 - DMARC
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "02. DMARC"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

DMARC_RECORD=""

if command -v dig >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    DMARC_RECORD=$(dig +short TXT "_dmarc.$DOMAIN" 2>/dev/null |
        grep -i "v=DMARC1" |
        tr -d '"')

elif command -v nslookup >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    DMARC_RECORD=$(nslookup -type=TXT "_dmarc.$DOMAIN" 2>/dev/null |
        grep -i "v=DMARC1" |
        sed 's/.*text = //' |
        tr -d '"')
fi

if [[ -n "$DMARC_RECORD" ]]; then

    echo "   DMARC Record:"
    echo "   $DMARC_RECORD"

    DMARC_POLICY=$(echo "$DMARC_RECORD" |
        grep -oE 'p=[^; ]+' |
        head -1)

    case "$DMARC_POLICY" in

        p=reject)
            pass "DMARC enabled - REJECT"
            ;;

        p=quarantine)
            warn "DMARC enabled - QUARANTINE"
            ;;

        p=none)
            warn "DMARC monitoring only - p=none"
            ;;

        *)
            warn "DMARC record found but policy could not be determined"
            ;;
    esac

else
    fail "DMARC record not found"
fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 03 - DKIM
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "03. DKIM"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

DKIM_RESULT="$TMP_DIR/dkim.txt"

pwsh -NoProfile -Command '

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

try {

    Import-Module ExchangeOnlineManagement -ErrorAction Stop

    Connect-ExchangeOnline `
        -ShowBanner:$false `
        -CommandName Get-DkimSigningConfig

    Get-DkimSigningConfig |
        Select-Object Identity,Enabled,Status,Domain,Selector1CNAME,Selector2CNAME |
        ConvertTo-Json -Depth 5

    Disconnect-ExchangeOnline -Confirm:$false

}
catch {

    Write-Error $_.Exception.Message
    exit 1
}

' > "$DKIM_RESULT" 2>/dev/null


if [[ -s "$DKIM_RESULT" ]]; then

    echo "   DKIM Configuration:"

    jq -r '
        if type == "array" then .[] else . end
        | "   Domain: \(.Domain // .Identity)"
        + " | Enabled: \(.Enabled)"
        + " | Status: \(.Status // "N/A")"
    ' "$DKIM_RESULT" 2>/dev/null

    DKIM_ENABLED=$(jq -r '
        [
            if type == "array" then .[] else . end
            | select(.Enabled == true)
        ] | length
    ' "$DKIM_RESULT" 2>/dev/null)

    if [[ "$DKIM_ENABLED" -gt 0 ]]; then
        pass "DKIM signing enabled"
    else
        fail "DKIM signing not enabled"
    fi

else

    warn "Unable to retrieve DKIM configuration"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 04 - ANTI-SPAM
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "04. ANTI-SPAM"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

ANTISPAM_RESULT="$TMP_DIR/antispam.txt"

pwsh -NoProfile -Command '

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

try {

    Import-Module ExchangeOnlineManagement -ErrorAction Stop

    Connect-ExchangeOnline `
        -ShowBanner:$false `
        -CommandName Get-HostedContentFilterPolicy,Get-HostedContentFilterRule

    $policies = Get-HostedContentFilterPolicy |
        Select-Object Name,Identity,IsDefault

    $rules = Get-HostedContentFilterRule |
        Select-Object Name,State,Priority

    [PSCustomObject]@{
        Policies = $policies
        Rules    = $rules
    } | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10

    Disconnect-ExchangeOnline -Confirm:$false

}
catch {

    Write-Error $_.Exception.Message
    exit 1

}

' > "$ANTISPAM_RESULT" 2>/dev/null


if [[ -s "$ANTISPAM_RESULT" ]]; then

    echo "   Anti-Spam Policies:"

    jq -r '
        .Policies[]
        | "   • \(.Name) | Default: \(.IsDefault)"
    ' "$ANTISPAM_RESULT" 2>/dev/null

    RULE_COUNT=$(jq '.Rules | length' "$ANTISPAM_RESULT" 2>/dev/null)

    if [[ "$RULE_COUNT" -gt 0 ]]; then
        pass "Anti-Spam policy/rules detected"
    else
        warn "No Anti-Spam rules detected"
    fi

else

    warn "Unable to retrieve Anti-Spam configuration"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 05 - ANTI-PHISHING
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "05. ANTI-PHISHING"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

ANTIPHISH_RESULT="$TMP_DIR/antiphish.txt"

pwsh -NoProfile -Command '

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

try {

    Import-Module ExchangeOnlineManagement -ErrorAction Stop

    Connect-ExchangeOnline `
        -ShowBanner:$false `
        -CommandName Get-AntiPhishPolicy

    Get-AntiPhishPolicy |
        Select-Object `
            Name,
            Enabled,
            IsDefault,
            EnableMailboxIntelligence,
            EnableMailboxIntelligenceProtection,
            EnableSpoofIntelligence,
            EnableOrganizationDomainsProtection,
            HonorDmarcPolicy,
            DmarcQuarantineAction,
            DmarcRejectAction |
        ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10

    Disconnect-ExchangeOnline -Confirm:$false

}
catch {

    Write-Error $_.Exception.Message
    exit 1

}

' > "$ANTIPHISH_RESULT" 2>/dev/null


if [[ -s "$ANTIPHISH_RESULT" ]]; then

    echo "   Anti-Phishing Policies:"

    jq -r '
        if type == "array" then .[] else . end
        | "   • \(.Name)"
        + " | Enabled: \(.Enabled)"
        + " | Default: \(.IsDefault)"
    ' "$ANTIPHISH_RESULT" 2>/dev/null

    PHISH_COUNT=$(jq '
        [
            if type == "array" then .[] else . end
            | select(.Enabled == true)
        ] | length
    ' "$ANTIPHISH_RESULT" 2>/dev/null)

    if [[ "$PHISH_COUNT" -gt 0 ]]; then
        pass "Anti-Phishing protection enabled"
    else
        warn "No enabled Anti-Phishing policy detected"
    fi

else

    warn "Unable to retrieve Anti-Phishing configuration"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 06 - DMARC ENFORCEMENT
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "06. DMARC ENFORCEMENT"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

if [[ -s "$ANTIPHISH_RESULT" ]]; then

    DMARC_HONOR=$(jq -r '
        [
            if type == "array" then .[] else . end
            | select(.HonorDmarcPolicy == true)
        ] | length
    ' "$ANTIPHISH_RESULT" 2>/dev/null)

    if [[ "$DMARC_HONOR" -gt 0 ]]; then
        pass "Microsoft 365 is configured to honor DMARC policy"
    else
        warn "DMARC policy enforcement configuration requires review"
    fi

else
    warn "Unable to determine DMARC enforcement configuration"
fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# FINAL SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | EMAIL SECURITY SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Tenant Domain       : $DOMAIN"
echo

echo "Security Checks"
echo "----------------------------"
echo "PASS                : $PASS"
echo "WARNING             : $WARN"
echo "FAIL                : $FAIL"
echo

TOTAL=$((PASS + WARN + FAIL))

if [[ "$TOTAL" -gt 0 ]]; then

    SCORE=$((PASS * 100 / TOTAL))

else

    SCORE=0

fi

echo "Security Score      : $SCORE / 100"
echo

if [[ "$FAIL" -eq 0 && "$WARN" -eq 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status      : ✅ HEALTHY"

elif [[ "$FAIL" -eq 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status      : ⚠️ REVIEW REQUIRED"

else

    echo "Overall Status      : ❌ ACTION REQUIRED"

fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZenAiOps | Microsoft 365 Managed Services"
echo " Email Security • Identity • Compliance • Monitoring"
echo "============================================================"
echo


-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

14 — External Mail Forwarding & Suspicious Inbox Rules

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS 14 - EXTERNAL MAIL FORWARDING & INBOX RULES
# ==============================================================
#
# Checks:
#   01. Mailbox external forwarding
#   02. Inbox rules
#   03. External redirect/forward rules
#   04. Suspicious forwarding destinations
#
# Requirements:
#   - bash
#   - jq
#   - pwsh
#   - ExchangeOnlineManagement module
#
# Usage:
#   ./14-m365-forwarding-rules.sh
#
# ==============================================================

set -u

TMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)

cleanup() {
    rm -rf "$TMP_DIR"
}

trap cleanup EXIT


# ==============================================================
# COUNTERS
# ==============================================================

TOTAL_MAILBOXES=0
EXTERNAL_FORWARDING=0
TOTAL_RULES=0
EXTERNAL_RULES=0
SUSPICIOUS_RULES=0


# ==============================================================
# HEADER
# ==============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 14 - MAIL FORWARDING & INBOX RULES"
echo "============================================================"
echo


# ==============================================================
# REQUIREMENTS
# ==============================================================

if ! command -v pwsh >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ PowerShell (pwsh) not installed."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ jq not installed."
    exit 1
fi


# ==============================================================
# 01 - MAILBOX FORWARDING
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "01. MAILBOX EXTERNAL FORWARDING"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

FORWARDING_JSON="$TMP_DIR/forwarding.json"

pwsh -NoProfile -Command '

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

try {

    Import-Module ExchangeOnlineManagement -ErrorAction Stop

    Connect-ExchangeOnline `
        -ShowBanner:$false `
        -CommandName Get-EXOMailbox

    $results = Get-EXOMailbox -ResultSize Unlimited |
        Select-Object `
            DisplayName,
            UserPrincipalName,
            PrimarySmtpAddress,
            ForwardingAddress,
            ForwardingSmtpAddress,
            DeliverToMailboxAndForward

    $results | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 5

    Disconnect-ExchangeOnline -Confirm:$false

}
catch {

    Write-Error $_.Exception.Message
    exit 1
}

' > "$FORWARDING_JSON" 2>/dev/null


if [[ -s "$FORWARDING_JSON" ]]; then

    echo

    # Normalize JSON to array
    jq -c '
        if type == "array" then .
        elif . == null then []
        else [.] end
    ' "$FORWARDING_JSON" > "$TMP_DIR/forwarding-normalized.json"


    TOTAL_MAILBOXES=$(jq 'length' \
        "$TMP_DIR/forwarding-normalized.json")


    echo "Total Mailboxes : $TOTAL_MAILBOXES"
    echo


    # ----------------------------------------------------------
    # Find external SMTP forwarding
    # ----------------------------------------------------------

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(
            (.ForwardingSmtpAddress != null and
             .ForwardingSmtpAddress != "")
        )
        | [
            .DisplayName,
            .UserPrincipalName,
            .ForwardingSmtpAddress,
            .DeliverToMailboxAndForward
          ]
        | @tsv
    ' "$TMP_DIR/forwarding-normalized.json" |
    while IFS=$'\t' read -r NAME UPN FORWARD DELIVER; do

        EXTERNAL_FORWARDING=$((EXTERNAL_FORWARDING + 1))

        echo "⚠️  EXTERNAL FORWARDING"
        echo "   User       : $NAME"
        echo "   UPN        : $UPN"
        echo "   Forward To : $FORWARD"
        echo "   Keep Copy  : $DELIVER"
        echo

    done

    # Count separately because while-loop counters run in subshell
    EXTERNAL_FORWARDING=$(jq '
        [
            .[]
            | select(
                .ForwardingSmtpAddress != null and
                .ForwardingSmtpAddress != ""
              )
        ] | length
    ' "$TMP_DIR/forwarding-normalized.json")


    if [[ "$EXTERNAL_FORWARDING" -eq 0 ]]; then
        echo "   ✅ No mailbox-level external SMTP forwarding detected"
    fi

else

    echo "   ❌ Unable to retrieve mailbox forwarding configuration"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 02 - INBOX RULES
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "02. INBOX RULES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

RULES_JSON="$TMP_DIR/rules.json"

pwsh -NoProfile -Command '

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

try {

    Import-Module ExchangeOnlineManagement -ErrorAction Stop

    Connect-ExchangeOnline `
        -ShowBanner:$false `
        -CommandName Get-EXOMailbox,Get-InboxRule

    $mailboxes = Get-EXOMailbox -ResultSize Unlimited |
        Where-Object {
            $_.PrimarySmtpAddress -ne $null
        }

    $allRules = @()

    foreach ($mailbox in $mailboxes) {

        try {

            $rules = Get-InboxRule `
                -Mailbox $mailbox.PrimarySmtpAddress `
                -ResultSize Unlimited `
                -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

            foreach ($rule in $rules) {

                $allRules += [PSCustomObject]@{

                    DisplayName       = $mailbox.DisplayName
                    UserPrincipalName = $mailbox.UserPrincipalName
                    Mailbox          = $mailbox.PrimarySmtpAddress

                    RuleName         = $rule.Name
                    Enabled          = $rule.Enabled
                    Priority         = $rule.Priority

                    ForwardTo        = @($rule.ForwardTo)
                    ForwardAsAttach  = @($rule.ForwardAsAttachmentTo)
                    RedirectTo       = @($rule.RedirectTo)

                    DeleteMessage    = $rule.DeleteMessage
                    MoveToFolder     = $rule.MoveToFolder
                    StopProcessing   = $rule.StopProcessingRules

                }

            }

        }
        catch {
            # Continue with next mailbox
        }
    }

    $allRules | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10

    Disconnect-ExchangeOnline -Confirm:$false

}
catch {

    Write-Error $_.Exception.Message
    exit 1
}

' > "$RULES_JSON" 2>/dev/null


if [[ -s "$RULES_JSON" ]]; then

    jq -c '
        if type == "array" then .
        elif . == null then []
        else [.] end
    ' "$RULES_JSON" > "$TMP_DIR/rules-normalized.json"


    TOTAL_RULES=$(jq 'length' \
        "$TMP_DIR/rules-normalized.json")


    echo
    echo "Total Inbox Rules : $TOTAL_RULES"
    echo


    # ==========================================================
    # EXTERNAL FORWARD / REDIRECT RULES
    # ==========================================================

    echo "External Forward / Redirect Rules"
    echo "--------------------------------"

    jq -r '
        .[]
        |
        select(
            (
                (.ForwardTo // []) +
                (.ForwardAsAttach // []) +
                (.RedirectTo // [])
            ) | length > 0
        )
        |
        [
            .DisplayName,
            .UserPrincipalName,
            .RuleName,
            .Enabled,
            (
                (
                    (.ForwardTo // []) +
                    (.ForwardAsAttach // []) +
                    (.RedirectTo // [])
                ) | join("; ")
            )
        ]
        | @tsv
    ' "$TMP_DIR/rules-normalized.json" |
    while IFS=$'\t' read -r NAME UPN RULE ENABLED DEST; do

        echo "⚠️  FORWARD / REDIRECT RULE"
        echo "   User        : $NAME"
        echo "   UPN         : $UPN"
        echo "   Rule        : $RULE"
        echo "   Enabled     : $ENABLED"
        echo "   Destination : $DEST"
        echo

    done


    # ==========================================================
    # COUNT RULES WITH FORWARD / REDIRECT
    # ==========================================================

    EXTERNAL_RULES=$(jq '
        [
            .[]
            | select(
                (
                    (.ForwardTo // []) +
                    (.ForwardAsAttach // []) +
                    (.RedirectTo // [])
                ) | length > 0
            )
        ] | length
    ' "$TMP_DIR/rules-normalized.json")


    # ==========================================================
    # SUSPICIOUS RULES
    # ==========================================================

    echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
    echo "03. SUSPICIOUS INBOX RULES"
    echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

    jq -r '
        .[]
        |
        select(
            (.Enabled == true)
            and
            (
                (.DeleteMessage == true)
                or
                (.ForwardTo // [] | length > 0)
                or
                (.ForwardAsAttach // [] | length > 0)
                or
                (.RedirectTo // [] | length > 0)
            )
        )
        |
        [
            .DisplayName,
            .UserPrincipalName,
            .RuleName,
            .DeleteMessage,
            (
                (
                    (.ForwardTo // []) +
                    (.ForwardAsAttach // []) +
                    (.RedirectTo // [])
                ) | join("; ")
            )
        ]
        | @tsv
    ' "$TMP_DIR/rules-normalized.json" |
    while IFS=$'\t' read -r NAME UPN RULE DELETE DEST; do

        SUSPICIOUS_RULES=$((SUSPICIOUS_RULES + 1))

        echo "🚨 REVIEW REQUIRED"
        echo "   User        : $NAME"
        echo "   UPN         : $UPN"
        echo "   Rule        : $RULE"
        echo "   Delete Mail : $DELETE"
        echo "   Destination : $DEST"
        echo

    done


    SUSPICIOUS_RULES=$(jq '
        [
            .[]
            | select(
                (.Enabled == true)
                and
                (
                    (.DeleteMessage == true)
                    or
                    ((.ForwardTo // []) | length > 0)
                    or
                    ((.ForwardAsAttach // []) | length > 0)
                    or
                    ((.RedirectTo // []) | length > 0)
                )
            )
        ] | length
    ' "$TMP_DIR/rules-normalized.json")


else

    echo "⚠️  Unable to retrieve Inbox Rules"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 04 - SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | MAIL FORWARDING SECURITY SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Total Mailboxes             : $TOTAL_MAILBOXES"
echo "External Mailbox Forwarding : $EXTERNAL_FORWARDING"
echo "Total Inbox Rules           : $TOTAL_RULES"
echo "Forward/Redirect Rules      : $EXTERNAL_RULES"
echo "Suspicious Rules            : $SUSPICIOUS_RULES"
echo


# ==============================================================
# STATUS
# ==============================================================

if [[ "$EXTERNAL_FORWARDING" -eq 0 &&
      "$EXTERNAL_RULES" -eq 0 &&
      "$SUSPICIOUS_RULES" -eq 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status              : ✅ HEALTHY"

elif [[ "$SUSPICIOUS_RULES" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status              : 🚨 ACTION REQUIRED"

else

    echo "Overall Status              : ⚠️ REVIEW REQUIRED"

fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZenAiOps | Microsoft 365 Managed Services"
echo " Identity • Email Security • Compliance • Monitoring"
echo "============================================================"
echo

15 — Microsoft 365 Audit Logging & Investigation Readiness

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS 15 - MICROSOFT 365 AUDIT LOGGING
# ==============================================================
#
# Checks:
#   01. Unified Audit Log ingestion
#   02. Organization mailbox audit configuration
#   03. AuditDisabled status
#   04. Recent audit events
#   05. Admin audit configuration
#
# Requirements:
#   - bash
#   - jq
#   - pwsh
#   - ExchangeOnlineManagement
#
# Usage:
#   ./15-m365-audit-logging.sh
#
# ==============================================================

set -u

TMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)

cleanup() {
    rm -rf "$TMP_DIR"
}

trap cleanup EXIT

PASS=0
WARN=0
FAIL=0

pass() {
    echo "   ✅ $1"
    PASS=$((PASS + 1))
}

warn() {
    echo "   ⚠️  $1"
    WARN=$((WARN + 1))
}

fail() {
    echo "   ❌ $1"
    FAIL=$((FAIL + 1))
}


# ==============================================================
# HEADER
# ==============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 15 - MICROSOFT 365 AUDIT LOGGING"
echo "============================================================"
echo


# ==============================================================
# REQUIREMENTS
# ==============================================================

if ! command -v pwsh >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ PowerShell (pwsh) is required."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ jq is required."
    exit 1
fi


# ==============================================================
# RUN POWERSHELL AUDIT CHECK
# ==============================================================

AUDIT_JSON="$TMP_DIR/audit.json"

echo "Checking Microsoft 365 audit configuration..."
echo


pwsh -NoProfile -Command '

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

try {

    Import-Module ExchangeOnlineManagement -ErrorAction Stop

    Connect-ExchangeOnline `
        -ShowBanner:$false `
        -CommandName `
            Get-AdminAuditLogConfig,
            Get-OrganizationConfig,
            Get-EXOMailbox,
            Search-UnifiedAuditLog

    # ----------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Unified Audit Log
    # ----------------------------------------------------------

    $auditConfig = Get-AdminAuditLogConfig

    # ----------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Organization Audit
    # ----------------------------------------------------------

    $orgConfig = Get-OrganizationConfig

    # ----------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Mailbox Audit
    # ----------------------------------------------------------

    $mailboxes = Get-EXOMailbox `
        -ResultSize Unlimited `
        -Properties AuditEnabled

    $mailboxTotal = @($mailboxes).Count

    $mailboxAuditEnabled = @(
        $mailboxes |
        Where-Object {
            $_.AuditEnabled -eq $true
        }
    ).Count

    # ----------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Recent Unified Audit Events
    # ----------------------------------------------------------

    $startDate = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().AddDays(-1)
    $endDate   = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime()

    $auditEvents = @()

    try {

        $auditEvents = @(
            Search-UnifiedAuditLog `
                -StartDate $startDate `
                -EndDate $endDate `
                -ResultSize 100
        )

    }
    catch {

        $auditEvents = @()

    }

    # ----------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. Admin Audit
    # ----------------------------------------------------------

    $adminAuditEnabled = $auditConfig.AdminAuditLogEnabled

    # ----------------------------------------------------------
    # OUTPUT
    # ----------------------------------------------------------

    [PSCustomObject]@{

        UnifiedAuditLogIngestionEnabled =
            $auditConfig.UnifiedAuditLogIngestionEnabled

        AdminAuditLogEnabled =
            $adminAuditEnabled

        AuditDisabled =
            $orgConfig.AuditDisabled

        MailboxTotal =
            $mailboxTotal

        MailboxAuditEnabled =
            $mailboxAuditEnabled

        RecentAuditEvents =
            @($auditEvents).Count

        CheckedFrom =
            $startDate

        CheckedTo =
            $endDate

    } | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10

    Disconnect-ExchangeOnline -Confirm:$false

}
catch {

    Write-Error $_.Exception.Message
    exit 1
}

' > "$AUDIT_JSON" 2>/dev/null


# ==============================================================
# VALIDATE RESULT
# ==============================================================

if [[ ! -s "$AUDIT_JSON" ]]; then

    echo "❌ Unable to retrieve Microsoft 365 audit configuration."
    echo
    echo "Possible causes:"
    echo "  • Exchange Online connection failed"
    echo "  • Missing Audit Logs permissions"
    echo "  • ExchangeOnlineManagement module issue"
    echo

    exit 1

fi


# ==============================================================
# READ VALUES
# ==============================================================

UNIFIED_AUDIT=$(jq -r \
    '.UnifiedAuditLogIngestionEnabled' \
    "$AUDIT_JSON")

ADMIN_AUDIT=$(jq -r \
    '.AdminAuditLogEnabled' \
    "$AUDIT_JSON")

AUDIT_DISABLED=$(jq -r \
    '.AuditDisabled' \
    "$AUDIT_JSON")

MAILBOX_TOTAL=$(jq -r \
    '.MailboxTotal' \
    "$AUDIT_JSON")

MAILBOX_AUDIT=$(jq -r \
    '.MailboxAuditEnabled' \
    "$AUDIT_JSON")

RECENT_EVENTS=$(jq -r \
    '.RecentAuditEvents' \
    "$AUDIT_JSON")


# ==============================================================
# 01 - UNIFIED AUDIT
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "01. UNIFIED AUDIT LOG"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "   UnifiedAuditLogIngestionEnabled : $UNIFIED_AUDIT"

if [[ "$UNIFIED_AUDIT" == "True" ||
      "$UNIFIED_AUDIT" == "true" ]]; then

    pass "Unified Audit Log ingestion is enabled"

else

    fail "Unified Audit Log ingestion is disabled"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 02 - ORGANIZATION AUDIT
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "02. ORGANIZATION AUDIT"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "   AuditDisabled : $AUDIT_DISABLED"

if [[ "$AUDIT_DISABLED" == "False" ||
      "$AUDIT_DISABLED" == "false" ]]; then

    pass "Organization mailbox auditing is enabled"

else

    fail "Organization mailbox auditing is disabled"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 03 - ADMIN AUDIT
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "03. ADMIN AUDIT LOG"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "   AdminAuditLogEnabled : $ADMIN_AUDIT"

if [[ "$ADMIN_AUDIT" == "True" ||
      "$ADMIN_AUDIT" == "true" ]]; then

    pass "Administrator audit logging enabled"

else

    warn "Administrator audit logging requires review"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 04 - MAILBOX AUDIT
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "04. MAILBOX AUDIT COVERAGE"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "   Total Mailboxes       : $MAILBOX_TOTAL"
echo "   Audit Enabled         : $MAILBOX_AUDIT"

if [[ "$MAILBOX_TOTAL" -gt 0 ]]; then

    MAILBOX_PERCENT=$((MAILBOX_AUDIT * 100 / MAILBOX_TOTAL))

else

    MAILBOX_PERCENT=0

fi

echo "   Coverage              : ${MAILBOX_PERCENT}%"
echo


if [[ "$MAILBOX_PERCENT" -ge 95 ]]; then

    pass "Mailbox audit coverage is high"

elif [[ "$MAILBOX_PERCENT" -ge 80 ]]; then

    warn "Some mailboxes require audit coverage review"

else

    fail "Low mailbox audit coverage"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 05 - RECENT AUDIT EVENTS
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "05. RECENT AUDIT ACTIVITY"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "   Search Window         : Last 24 hours"
echo "   Audit Events Found    : $RECENT_EVENTS"
echo

if [[ "$UNIFIED_AUDIT" == "True" ||
      "$UNIFIED_AUDIT" == "true" ]]; then

    if [[ "$RECENT_EVENTS" -gt 0 ]]; then

        pass "Audit events are being ingested"

    else

        warn "No audit events returned in the last 24 hours"

    fi

else

    fail "Audit ingestion disabled; event search unavailable"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | AUDIT READINESS SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Unified Audit Log      : $UNIFIED_AUDIT"
echo "Admin Audit            : $ADMIN_AUDIT"
echo "Organization Audit     : $AUDIT_DISABLED"
echo "Mailbox Audit Coverage : ${MAILBOX_PERCENT}%"
echo "Recent Audit Events    : $RECENT_EVENTS"
echo

echo "PASS                   : $PASS"
echo "WARNING                : $WARN"
echo "FAIL                   : $FAIL"
echo


# ==============================================================
# OVERALL STATUS
# ==============================================================

if [[ "$FAIL" -eq 0 && "$WARN" -eq 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status         : ✅ HEALTHY"

elif [[ "$FAIL" -eq 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status         : ⚠️ REVIEW REQUIRED"

else

    echo "Overall Status         : 🚨 ACTION REQUIRED"

fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZenAiOps | Microsoft 365 Managed Services"
echo " Audit • Security • Compliance • Investigation"
echo "============================================================"
echo
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

16 — Conditional Access Security Baseline

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS 16 - CONDITIONAL ACCESS SECURITY BASELINE
# ==============================================================
#
# Checks:
#   01. Conditional Access policy inventory
#   02. MFA protection
#   03. Legacy authentication blocking
#   04. Admin protection
#   05. Guest protection
#   06. Risk-based policies
#   07. Device compliance
#   08. Report-only policies
#   09. Disabled policies
#   10. Authentication strength
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#   - Microsoft Graph permission:
#       Policy.Read.All
#
# Usage:
#   ./16-m365-conditional-access.sh
#
# ==============================================================

set -u

TMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)

cleanup() {
    rm -rf "$TMP_DIR"
}

trap cleanup EXIT


# ==============================================================
# COUNTERS
# ==============================================================

PASS=0
WARN=0
FAIL=0

MFA_POLICY=0
LEGACY_BLOCK=0
ADMIN_POLICY=0
GUEST_POLICY=0
RISK_POLICY=0
DEVICE_POLICY=0
AUTH_STRENGTH=0

REPORT_ONLY=0
DISABLED=0
TOTAL=0


# ==============================================================
# STATUS FUNCTIONS
# ==============================================================

pass() {
    echo "   ✅ $1"
    PASS=$((PASS + 1))
}

warn() {
    echo "   ⚠️  $1"
    WARN=$((WARN + 1))
}

fail() {
    echo "   ❌ $1"
    FAIL=$((FAIL + 1))
}


# ==============================================================
# HEADER
# ==============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 16 - CONDITIONAL ACCESS SECURITY"
echo "============================================================"
echo


# ==============================================================
# CHECK REQUIREMENTS
# ==============================================================

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ Azure CLI is required."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ jq is required."
    exit 1
fi


# ==============================================================
# GET CONDITIONAL ACCESS POLICIES
# ==============================================================

echo "Getting Conditional Access policies..."
echo


POLICY_FILE="$TMP_DIR/policies.json"

az rest \
    --method GET \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/identity/conditionalAccess/policies" \
    > "$POLICY_FILE" 2> "$TMP_DIR/error.txt"


if [[ $? -ne 0 ]]; then

    echo "❌ Unable to retrieve Conditional Access policies."
    echo
    cat "$TMP_DIR/error.txt"
    echo
    echo "Required Microsoft Graph application permission:"
    echo "   Policy.Read.All"
    echo
    exit 1

fi


# ==============================================================
# CHECK GRAPH ERROR
# ==============================================================

if jq -e '.error' "$POLICY_FILE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo "❌ Microsoft Graph returned an error:"
    jq -r '.error.message' "$POLICY_FILE"
    echo
    exit 1

fi


# ==============================================================
# NORMALIZE POLICIES
# ==============================================================

jq '.value // []' "$POLICY_FILE" \
    > "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json"


TOTAL=$(jq 'length' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json")


echo "Total Conditional Access Policies : $TOTAL"
echo


# ==============================================================
# 01 - POLICY INVENTORY
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "01. CONDITIONAL ACCESS INVENTORY"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

jq -r '
    .[]
    | "   • \(.displayName)"
      + " | State: \(.state)"
' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json"

echo


# ==============================================================
# POLICY STATE COUNTS
# ==============================================================

REPORT_ONLY=$(jq '
    [
        .[]
        | select(.state == "enabledForReportingButNotEnforced")
    ] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json")

DISABLED=$(jq '
    [
        .[]
        | select(.state == "disabled")
    ] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json")


echo "Enabled Policies       : $(jq '[.[] | select(.state=="enabled")] | length' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json")"
echo "Report-only Policies   : $REPORT_ONLY"
echo "Disabled Policies      : $DISABLED"
echo


# ==============================================================
# 02 - MFA PROTECTION
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "02. MFA PROTECTION"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


MFA_POLICY=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        .state == "enabled"
        and
        (
            (
                .grantControls.builtInControls // []
            ) | index("mfa")
        ) != null
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json")


if [[ "$MFA_POLICY" -gt 0 ]]; then

    pass "Enabled Conditional Access MFA policy detected"

    echo
    echo "   MFA Policies:"

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(
            .state == "enabled"
            and
            (
                (.grantControls.builtInControls // [])
                | index("mfa")
            ) != null
        )
        | "   • \(.displayName)"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json"

else

    fail "No enabled Conditional Access MFA policy detected"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 03 - LEGACY AUTHENTICATION
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "03. LEGACY AUTHENTICATION BLOCK"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


LEGACY_BLOCK=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        .state == "enabled"
        and
        (
            .conditions.clientAppTypes // []
        )
        | (
            index("exchangeActiveSync")
            // index("other")
        )
    )
    | select(
        (
            (.grantControls.builtInControls // [])
            | index("block")
        ) != null
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)


# More direct detection:
LEGACY_BLOCK=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(.state == "enabled")
    | select(
        (
            (.conditions.clientAppTypes // [])
            | index("exchangeActiveSync")
        ) != null
        or
        (
            (.conditions.clientAppTypes // [])
            | index("other")
        ) != null
    )
    | select(
        (
            (.grantControls.builtInControls // [])
            | index("block")
        ) != null
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json")


if [[ "$LEGACY_BLOCK" -gt 0 ]]; then

    pass "Conditional Access blocking legacy authentication detected"

else

    warn "No clear enabled legacy-authentication blocking policy detected"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 04 - ADMIN PROTECTION
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "04. ADMIN ACCOUNT PROTECTION"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


ADMIN_POLICY=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(.state == "enabled")
    | select(
        (
            (.conditions.users.includeRoles // [])
            | length
        ) > 0
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json")


if [[ "$ADMIN_POLICY" -gt 0 ]]; then

    pass "Conditional Access policy targeting directory roles detected"

    echo
    echo "   Admin-targeted policies:"

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(.state == "enabled")
        | select(
            (.conditions.users.includeRoles // []) | length > 0
        )
        | "   • \(.displayName)"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json"

else

    warn "No enabled Conditional Access policy explicitly targeting directory roles"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 05 - GUEST / EXTERNAL USER PROTECTION
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "05. GUEST / EXTERNAL USER PROTECTION"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


GUEST_POLICY=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(.state == "enabled")
    | select(
        (.conditions.users.includeGuestsOrExternalUsers != null)
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json")


if [[ "$GUEST_POLICY" -gt 0 ]]; then

    pass "Guest/external-user Conditional Access policy detected"

    echo
    echo "   Guest/External policies:"

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(.state == "enabled")
        | select(
            .conditions.users.includeGuestsOrExternalUsers != null
        )
        | "   • \(.displayName)"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json"

else

    warn "No enabled Guest/External-user Conditional Access policy detected"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 06 - RISK-BASED PROTECTION
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "06. RISK-BASED ACCESS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


RISK_POLICY=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(.state == "enabled")
    | select(
        (
            (.conditions.userRiskLevels // [])
            | length
        ) > 0
        or
        (
            (.conditions.signInRiskLevels // [])
            | length
        ) > 0
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json")


if [[ "$RISK_POLICY" -gt 0 ]]; then

    pass "Risk-based Conditional Access policy detected"

    echo
    echo "   Risk policies:"

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(.state == "enabled")
        | select(
            (
                (.conditions.userRiskLevels // []) | length > 0
            )
            or
            (
                (.conditions.signInRiskLevels // []) | length > 0
            )
        )
        | "   • \(.displayName)"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json"

else

    warn "No enabled user-risk/sign-in-risk Conditional Access policy detected"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 07 - DEVICE COMPLIANCE
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "07. DEVICE COMPLIANCE"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


DEVICE_POLICY=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(.state == "enabled")
    | select(
        (
            (.grantControls.builtInControls // [])
            | index("compliantDevice")
        ) != null
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json")


if [[ "$DEVICE_POLICY" -gt 0 ]]; then

    pass "Compliant-device Conditional Access policy detected"

    echo
    echo "   Device policies:"

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(.state == "enabled")
        | select(
            (.grantControls.builtInControls // [])
            | index("compliantDevice")
        )
        | "   • \(.displayName)"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json"

else

    warn "No enabled compliant-device access policy detected"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 08 - REPORT ONLY
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "08. REPORT-ONLY POLICIES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


if [[ "$REPORT_ONLY" -gt 0 ]]; then

    warn "$REPORT_ONLY Conditional Access policy/policies are report-only"

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(.state == "enabledForReportingButNotEnforced")
        | "   • \(.displayName)"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json"

else

    pass "No Conditional Access policies are currently report-only"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 09 - DISABLED POLICIES
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "09. DISABLED POLICIES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


if [[ "$DISABLED" -gt 0 ]]; then

    warn "$DISABLED Conditional Access policy/policies are disabled"

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(.state == "disabled")
        | "   • \(.displayName)"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json"

else

    pass "No disabled Conditional Access policies"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 10 - AUTHENTICATION STRENGTH
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "10. AUTHENTICATION STRENGTH"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


AUTH_STRENGTH=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(.state == "enabled")
    | select(
        .grantControls.authenticationStrength != null
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json")


if [[ "$AUTH_STRENGTH" -gt 0 ]]; then

    pass "Authentication Strength used in Conditional Access"

    echo
    echo "   Authentication Strength policies:"

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(.state == "enabled")
        | select(
            .grantControls.authenticationStrength != null
        )
        | "   • \(.displayName)"
          + " | Strength: \(.grantControls.authenticationStrength.displayName // "Configured")"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/policies-array.json"

else

    warn "No enabled Conditional Access Authentication Strength policy detected"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# FINAL SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | CONDITIONAL ACCESS SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Total CA Policies          : $TOTAL"
echo "Report-only Policies       : $REPORT_ONLY"
echo "Disabled Policies          : $DISABLED"
echo
echo "MFA Protection             : $MFA_POLICY"
echo "Legacy Auth Blocking       : $LEGACY_BLOCK"
echo "Admin Protection           : $ADMIN_POLICY"
echo "Guest Protection           : $GUEST_POLICY"
echo "Risk-based Policies        : $RISK_POLICY"
echo "Device Compliance          : $DEVICE_POLICY"
echo "Authentication Strength    : $AUTH_STRENGTH"
echo

echo "PASS                       : $PASS"
echo "WARNING                    : $WARN"
echo "FAIL                       : $FAIL"
echo


# ==============================================================
# OVERALL STATUS
# ==============================================================

if [[ "$FAIL" -eq 0 && "$WARN" -eq 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status             : ✅ HEALTHY"

elif [[ "$FAIL" -eq 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status             : ⚠️ REVIEW REQUIRED"

else

    echo "Overall Status             : 🚨 ACTION REQUIRED"

fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZenAiOps | Microsoft 365 Managed Services"
echo " Identity • Security • Compliance • Monitoring"
echo "============================================================"
echo

17: Microsoft 365 Defender / Security Alerts

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS 17 - MICROSOFT 365 DEFENDER SECURITY ALERTS
# ==============================================================
#
# Checks:
#   01. Active security alerts
#   02. Critical / High / Medium / Low alerts
#   03. Alert status
#   04. Detection source
#   05. Affected users / assets
#   06. Unassigned alerts
#   07. Recent alerts
#   08. Microsoft Defender incidents
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#   - Microsoft Graph:
#
#       SecurityAlert.Read.All
#       SecurityIncident.Read.All
#
# API:
#   /v1.0/security/alerts_v2
#   /v1.0/security/incidents
#
# ==============================================================

set -u

TMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)

cleanup() {
    rm -rf "$TMP_DIR"
}

trap cleanup EXIT


# ==============================================================
# COUNTERS
# ==============================================================

TOTAL_ALERTS=0
HIGH_ALERTS=0
MEDIUM_ALERTS=0
LOW_ALERTS=0
CRITICAL_ALERTS=0

ACTIVE_ALERTS=0
UNASSIGNED_ALERTS=0
RECENT_ALERTS=0

TOTAL_INCIDENTS=0
ACTIVE_INCIDENTS=0
HIGH_INCIDENTS=0
MEDIUM_INCIDENTS=0


# ==============================================================
# HEADER
# ==============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 17 - MICROSOFT 365 DEFENDER ALERTS"
echo "============================================================"
echo


# ==============================================================
# REQUIREMENTS
# ==============================================================

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ Azure CLI is required."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ jq is required."
    exit 1
fi


# ==============================================================
# 01 - GET SECURITY ALERTS
# ==============================================================

echo "Getting Microsoft 365 Defender alerts..."
echo

ALERTS_FILE="$TMP_DIR/alerts.json"

az rest \
    --method GET \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/security/alerts_v2?\$top=100" \
    > "$ALERTS_FILE" \
    2> "$TMP_DIR/alerts-error.txt"


if [[ $? -ne 0 ]]; then

    echo "❌ Unable to retrieve Defender alerts."
    echo
    cat "$TMP_DIR/alerts-error.txt"
    echo
    echo "Required permission:"
    echo "   SecurityAlert.Read.All"
    echo

    exit 1
fi


# ==============================================================
# GRAPH ERROR
# ==============================================================

if jq -e '.error' "$ALERTS_FILE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo "❌ Microsoft Graph returned an error:"
    jq -r '.error.message' "$ALERTS_FILE"
    echo

    exit 1
fi


# ==============================================================
# NORMALIZE
# ==============================================================

jq '.value // []' "$ALERTS_FILE" \
    > "$TMP_DIR/alerts-array.json"


TOTAL_ALERTS=$(jq 'length' "$TMP_DIR/alerts-array.json")


echo "Total Alerts Returned : $TOTAL_ALERTS"
echo


# ==============================================================
# 02 - ALERT SEVERITY
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "02. ALERT SEVERITY"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

CRITICAL_ALERTS=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        (.severity // "" | ascii_downcase) == "critical"
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/alerts-array.json")


HIGH_ALERTS=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        (.severity // "" | ascii_downcase) == "high"
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/alerts-array.json")


MEDIUM_ALERTS=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        (.severity // "" | ascii_downcase) == "medium"
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/alerts-array.json")


LOW_ALERTS=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        (.severity // "" | ascii_downcase) == "low"
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/alerts-array.json")


echo "Critical : $CRITICAL_ALERTS"
echo "High     : $HIGH_ALERTS"
echo "Medium   : $MEDIUM_ALERTS"
echo "Low      : $LOW_ALERTS"
echo


# ==============================================================
# 03 - ACTIVE ALERTS
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "03. ACTIVE ALERTS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


ACTIVE_ALERTS=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        (.status // "" | ascii_downcase)
        | IN(
            "new",
            "inprogress",
            "active",
            "investigating"
        )
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/alerts-array.json")


echo "Active Alerts : $ACTIVE_ALERTS"
echo


if [[ "$CRITICAL_ALERTS" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "🚨 CRITICAL ALERTS"
    echo

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(
            (.severity // "" | ascii_downcase) == "critical"
        )
        | "   • \(.title // "Untitled")"
          + " | Status: \(.status // "N/A")"
          + " | Source: \(.productName // "N/A")"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/alerts-array.json"

    echo

fi


if [[ "$HIGH_ALERTS" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "🔴 HIGH ALERTS"
    echo

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(
            (.severity // "" | ascii_downcase) == "high"
        )
        | "   • \(.title // "Untitled")"
          + " | Status: \(.status // "N/A")"
          + " | Source: \(.productName // "N/A")"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/alerts-array.json"

    echo

fi


# ==============================================================
# 04 - UNASSIGNED ALERTS
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "04. UNASSIGNED ALERTS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


UNASSIGNED_ALERTS=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        .assignedTo == null
        or .assignedTo == ""
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/alerts-array.json")


echo "Unassigned Alerts : $UNASSIGNED_ALERTS"
echo


if [[ "$UNASSIGNED_ALERTS" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "⚠️  Alerts requiring ownership:"
    echo

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(
            .assignedTo == null
            or .assignedTo == ""
        )
        | "   • \(.title // "Untitled")"
          + " | Severity: \(.severity // "N/A")"
          + " | Status: \(.status // "N/A")"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/alerts-array.json"

else

    echo "   ✅ All returned alerts have an owner"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 05 - DETECTION SOURCES
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "05. DETECTION SOURCES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


jq -r '
    .[]
    | (.productName // "Unknown")
' "$TMP_DIR/alerts-array.json" |
sort |
uniq -c |
sort -nr |
while read -r COUNT SOURCE; do

    echo "   $SOURCE : $COUNT"

done

echo


# ==============================================================
# 06 - RECENT ALERTS
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "06. RECENT ALERTS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

NOW=$(date -u +%s)
TWENTY_FOUR_HOURS_AGO=$((NOW - 86400))


while IFS= read -r CREATED; do

    if [[ -z "$CREATED" || "$CREATED" == "null" ]]; then
        continue
    fi

    CREATED_TS=$(date -d "$CREATED" +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)

    if [[ "$CREATED_TS" -ge "$TWENTY_FOUR_HOURS_AGO" ]]; then
        RECENT_ALERTS=$((RECENT_ALERTS + 1))
    fi

done < <(
    jq -r '.[].createdDateTime // empty' \
        "$TMP_DIR/alerts-array.json"
)


echo "Alerts in last 24 hours : $RECENT_ALERTS"
echo


# ==============================================================
# 07 - ALERT DETAILS
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "07. ALERT DETAILS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

jq -r '
    .[]
    |
    [
        .severity // "N/A",
        .status // "N/A",
        .productName // "N/A",
        .title // "Untitled",
        .createdDateTime // "N/A",
        .assignedTo // "Unassigned",
        .incidentId // "No Incident"
    ]
    | @tsv
' "$TMP_DIR/alerts-array.json" |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r SEVERITY STATUS PRODUCT TITLE CREATED ASSIGNED INCIDENT; do

    echo
    echo "   --------------------------------------------------------"
    echo "   Alert       : $TITLE"
    echo "   Severity    : $SEVERITY"
    echo "   Status      : $STATUS"
    echo "   Product     : $PRODUCT"
    echo "   Created     : $CREATED"
    echo "   Assigned To : $ASSIGNED"
    echo "   Incident ID : $INCIDENT"

done

echo


# ==============================================================
# 08 - GET INCIDENTS
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "08. MICROSOFT DEFENDER INCIDENTS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

INCIDENT_FILE="$TMP_DIR/incidents.json"

az rest \
    --method GET \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/security/incidents?\$top=100" \
    > "$INCIDENT_FILE" \
    2> "$TMP_DIR/incidents-error.txt"


if [[ $? -eq 0 ]] &&
   ! jq -e '.error' "$INCIDENT_FILE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    jq '.value // []' "$INCIDENT_FILE" \
        > "$TMP_DIR/incidents-array.json"

    TOTAL_INCIDENTS=$(jq 'length' \
        "$TMP_DIR/incidents-array.json")


    ACTIVE_INCIDENTS=$(jq '
        [
            .[]
            | select(
                (.status // "" | ascii_downcase)
                | IN(
                    "active",
                    "new",
                    "inprogress",
                    "investigating"
                )
            )
        ] | length
    ' "$TMP_DIR/incidents-array.json")


    HIGH_INCIDENTS=$(jq '
        [
            .[]
            | select(
                (.severity // "" | ascii_downcase) == "high"
            )
        ] | length
    ' "$TMP_DIR/incidents-array.json")


    MEDIUM_INCIDENTS=$(jq '
        [
            .[]
            | select(
                (.severity // "" | ascii_downcase) == "medium"
            )
        ] | length
    ' "$TMP_DIR/incidents-array.json")


    echo "Total Incidents  : $TOTAL_INCIDENTS"
    echo "Active Incidents : $ACTIVE_INCIDENTS"
    echo "High Incidents   : $HIGH_INCIDENTS"
    echo "Medium Incidents : $MEDIUM_INCIDENTS"
    echo


    if [[ "$ACTIVE_INCIDENTS" -gt 0 ]]; then

        echo "🚨 ACTIVE INCIDENTS"
        echo

        jq -r '
            .[]
            | select(
                (.status // "" | ascii_downcase)
                | IN(
                    "active",
                    "new",
                    "inprogress",
                    "investigating"
                )
            )
            | "   • \(.displayName // .title // "Unnamed")"
              + " | Severity: \(.severity // "N/A")"
              + " | Status: \(.status // "N/A")"
        ' "$TMP_DIR/incidents-array.json"

        echo

    fi

else

    echo "⚠️  Unable to retrieve Defender incidents."
    echo
    echo "Required permission:"
    echo "   SecurityIncident.Read.All"
    echo

fi


# ==============================================================
# FINAL SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | MICROSOFT 365 DEFENDER SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Security Alerts"
echo "----------------------------"
echo "Total Alerts        : $TOTAL_ALERTS"
echo "Critical            : $CRITICAL_ALERTS"
echo "High                : $HIGH_ALERTS"
echo "Medium              : $MEDIUM_ALERTS"
echo "Low                 : $LOW_ALERTS"
echo "Active              : $ACTIVE_ALERTS"
echo "Unassigned          : $UNASSIGNED_ALERTS"
echo "Last 24 Hours       : $RECENT_ALERTS"
echo

echo "Defender Incidents"
echo "----------------------------"
echo "Total               : $TOTAL_INCIDENTS"
echo "Active              : $ACTIVE_INCIDENTS"
echo "High                : $HIGH_INCIDENTS"
echo "Medium              : $MEDIUM_INCIDENTS"
echo


# ==============================================================
# OVERALL STATUS
# ==============================================================

if [[ "$CRITICAL_ALERTS" -gt 0 ||
      "$HIGH_INCIDENTS" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status      : 🚨 CRITICAL"

elif [[ "$HIGH_ALERTS" -gt 0 ||
        "$ACTIVE_INCIDENTS" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status      : 🔴 ACTION REQUIRED"

elif [[ "$MEDIUM_ALERTS" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status      : ⚠️ REVIEW REQUIRED"

else

    echo "Overall Status      : ✅ HEALTHY"

fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZenAiOps | Microsoft 365 Managed Services"
echo " Defender • Security • Incident Monitoring"
echo "============================================================"
echo
