Next → 18: Microsoft Secure Score

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS 18 - MICROSOFT SECURE SCORE
# ==============================================================
#
# Checks:
#   01. Current Secure Score
#   02. Maximum Score
#   03. Percentage
#   04. Historical Score
#   05. Control Scores
#   06. Improvement Actions
#   07. Points available
#   08. Top improvement opportunities
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#
# Microsoft Graph Permission:
#   SecurityEvents.Read.All
#
# ==============================================================

set -u

TMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)

cleanup() {
    rm -rf "$TMP_DIR"
}

trap cleanup EXIT


# ==============================================================
# HEADER
# ==============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 18 - MICROSOFT SECURE SCORE"
echo "============================================================"
echo


# ==============================================================
# REQUIREMENTS
# ==============================================================

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ Azure CLI is required."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ jq is required."
    exit 1
fi


# ==============================================================
# 01 - GET SECURE SCORE
# ==============================================================

echo "Getting Microsoft Secure Score..."
echo

SCORE_FILE="$TMP_DIR/secure-score.json"

az rest \
    --method GET \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/security/secureScores?\$top=90" \
    > "$SCORE_FILE" \
    2> "$TMP_DIR/score-error.txt"


if [[ $? -ne 0 ]]; then

    echo "❌ Unable to retrieve Secure Score."
    echo
    cat "$TMP_DIR/score-error.txt"
    echo
    echo "Required Microsoft Graph permission:"
    echo "   SecurityEvents.Read.All"
    echo

    exit 1

fi


if jq -e '.error' "$SCORE_FILE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo "❌ Microsoft Graph error:"
    jq -r '.error.message' "$SCORE_FILE"
    echo

    exit 1

fi


# ==============================================================
# NORMALIZE
# ==============================================================

jq '.value // []' "$SCORE_FILE" \
    > "$TMP_DIR/scores-array.json"


SCORE_COUNT=$(jq 'length' "$TMP_DIR/scores-array.json")


if [[ "$SCORE_COUNT" -eq 0 ]]; then

    echo "⚠️ No Secure Score data returned."
    exit 0

fi


# ==============================================================
# LATEST SCORE
# ==============================================================

LATEST=$(jq '
    sort_by(.createdDateTime)
    | last
' "$TMP_DIR/scores-array.json")


CURRENT_SCORE=$(echo "$LATEST" |
    jq -r '.currentScore // 0')

MAX_SCORE=$(echo "$LATEST" |
    jq -r '.maxScore // 0')

ACTIVE_USERS=$(echo "$LATEST" |
    jq -r '.activeUserCount // 0')

LICENSED_USERS=$(echo "$LATEST" |
    jq -r '.licensedUserCount // 0')

CREATED=$(echo "$LATEST" |
    jq -r '.createdDateTime // "N/A"')


# ==============================================================
# SCORE %
# ==============================================================

if [[ "$MAX_SCORE" != "0" ]]; then

    SCORE_PERCENT=$(awk \
        -v score="$CURRENT_SCORE" \
        -v max="$MAX_SCORE" \
        'BEGIN {printf "%.2f", (score/max)*100}')

else

    SCORE_PERCENT="0.00"

fi


# ==============================================================
# 02 - CURRENT SCORE
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "02. CURRENT SECURE SCORE"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "   Current Score     : $CURRENT_SCORE"
echo "   Maximum Score     : $MAX_SCORE"
echo "   Score Percentage  : ${SCORE_PERCENT}%"
echo "   Active Users      : $ACTIVE_USERS"
echo "   Licensed Users    : $LICENSED_USERS"
echo "   Score Date        : $CREATED"
echo


# ==============================================================
# 03 - SCORE STATUS
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "03. SCORE STATUS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

SCORE_INT=$(printf "%.0f" "$SCORE_PERCENT")


if [[ "$SCORE_INT" -ge 80 ]]; then

    echo "   🟢 Strong Secure Score"

elif [[ "$SCORE_INT" -ge 60 ]]; then

    echo "   🟡 Moderate Secure Score"

elif [[ "$SCORE_INT" -ge 40 ]]; then

    echo "   🟠 Improvement Required"

else

    echo "   🔴 Significant Improvement Required"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 04 - SCORE HISTORY
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "04. SECURE SCORE HISTORY"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo

echo "   Date                         Score       Max       %"
echo "   --------------------------------------------------------"

jq -r '
    sort_by(.createdDateTime)
    | reverse
    | .[:10][]
    |
    [
        (.createdDateTime // "N/A"),
        (.currentScore // 0),
        (.maxScore // 0)
    ]
    | @tsv
' "$TMP_DIR/scores-array.json" |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r DATE SCORE MAX; do

    if [[ "$MAX" != "0" ]]; then

        PERCENT=$(awk \
            -v s="$SCORE" \
            -v m="$MAX" \
            'BEGIN {printf "%.1f", (s/m)*100}')

    else

        PERCENT="0"

    fi

    printf "   %-28s %-10s %-10s %s%%\n" \
        "$DATE" "$SCORE" "$MAX" "$PERCENT"

done

echo


# ==============================================================
# 05 - GET CONTROL PROFILES
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "05. SECURE SCORE IMPROVEMENT ACTIONS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

CONTROL_FILE="$TMP_DIR/control-profiles.json"

az rest \
    --method GET \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/security/secureScoreControlProfiles?\$top=999" \
    > "$CONTROL_FILE" \
    2> "$TMP_DIR/control-error.txt"


if [[ $? -ne 0 ]]; then

    echo "⚠️ Unable to retrieve improvement actions."
    echo

else

    if jq -e '.error' "$CONTROL_FILE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then

        echo "⚠️ Microsoft Graph returned an error:"
        jq -r '.error.message' "$CONTROL_FILE"
        echo

    else

        jq '.value // []' "$CONTROL_FILE" \
            > "$TMP_DIR/controls-array.json"

        CONTROL_COUNT=$(jq 'length' \
            "$TMP_DIR/controls-array.json")

        echo "   Improvement Actions Returned : $CONTROL_COUNT"
        echo

    fi

fi


# ==============================================================
# 06 - TOP IMPROVEMENT OPPORTUNITIES
# ==============================================================

if [[ -s "$TMP_DIR/controls-array.json" ]]; then

    echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
    echo "06. TOP IMPROVEMENT OPPORTUNITIES"
    echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

    echo

    jq -r '
        .[]
        |
        {
            title: (
                .title
                // .controlName
                // "Unnamed Control"
            ),

            category: (
                .controlCategory
                // "Unknown"
            ),

            score: (
                .score
                // 0
            ),

            maxScore: (
                .maxScore
                // 0
            ),

            implementationCost: (
                .implementationCost
                // "Unknown"
            ),

            userImpact: (
                .userImpact
                // "Unknown"
            )
        }
        |
        [
            .title,
            .category,
            .score,
            .maxScore,
            .implementationCost,
            .userImpact
        ]
        | @tsv
    ' "$TMP_DIR/controls-array.json" |
    head -20 |
    while IFS=$'\t' read -r TITLE CATEGORY SCORE MAX COST IMPACT; do

        echo "   • $TITLE"
        echo "     Category          : $CATEGORY"
        echo "     Score             : $SCORE / $MAX"
        echo "     Implementation   : $COST"
        echo "     User Impact       : $IMPACT"
        echo

    done

fi


# ==============================================================
# 07 - CONTROL CATEGORY SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

if [[ -s "$TMP_DIR/controls-array.json" ]]; then

    echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
    echo "07. CONTROL CATEGORIES"
    echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | .controlCategory // "Unknown"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/controls-array.json" |
    sort |
    uniq -c |
    sort -nr |
    while read -r COUNT CATEGORY; do

        echo "   $CATEGORY : $COUNT"

    done

    echo

fi


# ==============================================================
# 08 - SCORE TREND
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "08. SCORE TREND"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

OLDEST=$(jq '
    sort_by(.createdDateTime)
    | first
' "$TMP_DIR/scores-array.json")

OLDEST_SCORE=$(echo "$OLDEST" |
    jq -r '.currentScore // 0')

SCORE_CHANGE=$(awk \
    -v latest="$CURRENT_SCORE" \
    -v oldest="$OLDEST_SCORE" \
    'BEGIN {printf "%.2f", latest-oldest}')


if awk "BEGIN {exit !($SCORE_CHANGE > 0)}"; then

    echo "   Trend : 📈 Improving"
    echo "   Change: +$SCORE_CHANGE"

elif awk "BEGIN {exit !($SCORE_CHANGE < 0)}"; then

    echo "   Trend : 📉 Declining"
    echo "   Change: $SCORE_CHANGE"

else

    echo "   Trend : ➡️ Stable"
    echo "   Change: 0"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# FINAL SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | MICROSOFT SECURE SCORE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Current Score        : $CURRENT_SCORE / $MAX_SCORE"
echo "Score Percentage     : ${SCORE_PERCENT}%"
echo "Score Change         : $SCORE_CHANGE"
echo "Active Users         : $ACTIVE_USERS"
echo "Licensed Users       : $LICENSED_USERS"
echo "Historical Records   : $SCORE_COUNT"
echo

if [[ -s "$TMP_DIR/controls-array.json" ]]; then

    CONTROL_COUNT=$(jq 'length' \
        "$TMP_DIR/controls-array.json")

    echo "Improvement Actions  : $CONTROL_COUNT"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS STATUS
# ==============================================================

if [[ "$SCORE_INT" -ge 80 ]]; then

    echo "ZenAiOps Status      : 🟢 GOOD"

elif [[ "$SCORE_INT" -ge 60 ]]; then

    echo "ZenAiOps Status      : 🟡 REVIEW"

elif [[ "$SCORE_INT" -ge 40 ]]; then

    echo "ZenAiOps Status      : 🟠 ACTION REQUIRED"

else

    echo "ZenAiOps Status      : 🔴 HIGH PRIORITY"

fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZenAiOps | Microsoft 365 Managed Services"
echo " Security Posture • Recommendations • Continuous Monitoring"
echo "============================================================"
echo

19 — Microsoft 365 Service Health & Incidents

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS 19 - MICROSOFT 365 SERVICE HEALTH
# ==============================================================
#
# Checks:
#   01. Microsoft 365 service health
#   02. Exchange Online
#   03. Microsoft Teams
#   04. SharePoint Online
#   05. OneDrive
#   06. Identity / Entra
#   07. Active service incidents
#   08. Service advisories / issues
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#
# Microsoft Graph permission:
#   ServiceHealth.Read.All
#
# ==============================================================

set -u

TMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)

cleanup() {
    rm -rf "$TMP_DIR"
}

trap cleanup EXIT


# ==============================================================
# COUNTERS
# ==============================================================

TOTAL_SERVICES=0
HEALTHY_SERVICES=0
DEGRADED_SERVICES=0
INTERRUPTED_SERVICES=0
INVESTIGATING_SERVICES=0

TOTAL_ISSUES=0
ACTIVE_ISSUES=0
RESOLVED_ISSUES=0


# ==============================================================
# HEADER
# ==============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 19 - MICROSOFT 365 SERVICE HEALTH"
echo "============================================================"
echo


# ==============================================================
# REQUIREMENTS
# ==============================================================

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ Azure CLI is required."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ jq is required."
    exit 1
fi


# ==============================================================
# 01 - SERVICE HEALTH OVERVIEW
# ==============================================================

echo "Getting Microsoft 365 service health..."
echo

HEALTH_FILE="$TMP_DIR/health.json"

az rest \
    --method GET \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/admin/serviceAnnouncement/healthOverviews" \
    > "$HEALTH_FILE" \
    2> "$TMP_DIR/health-error.txt"


if [[ $? -ne 0 ]]; then

    echo "❌ Unable to retrieve Microsoft 365 service health."
    echo
    cat "$TMP_DIR/health-error.txt"
    echo
    echo "Required Graph permission:"
    echo "   ServiceHealth.Read.All"
    echo

    exit 1

fi


# ==============================================================
# GRAPH ERROR
# ==============================================================

if jq -e '.error' "$HEALTH_FILE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo "❌ Microsoft Graph error:"
    jq -r '.error.message' "$HEALTH_FILE"
    echo

    exit 1

fi


# ==============================================================
# NORMALIZE
# ==============================================================

jq '.value // []' "$HEALTH_FILE" \
    > "$TMP_DIR/health-array.json"

TOTAL_SERVICES=$(jq 'length' "$TMP_DIR/health-array.json")


echo "Services Returned : $TOTAL_SERVICES"
echo


# ==============================================================
# 02 - SERVICE STATUS
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "02. MICROSOFT 365 SERVICE STATUS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo

jq -r '
    .[]
    |
    [
        .service // "Unknown",
        .status // "unknown"
    ]
    | @tsv
' "$TMP_DIR/health-array.json" |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r SERVICE STATUS; do

    case "$STATUS" in

        serviceOperational)

            echo "   🟢 $SERVICE"
            echo "      Status : Operational"
            ;;

        serviceDegradation)

            echo "   🟠 $SERVICE"
            echo "      Status : Service Degradation"
            ;;

        serviceInterruption)

            echo "   🔴 $SERVICE"
            echo "      Status : Service Interruption"
            ;;

        investigating)

            echo "   🟡 $SERVICE"
            echo "      Status : Investigating"
            ;;

        restoringService)

            echo "   🟡 $SERVICE"
            echo "      Status : Restoring Service"
            ;;

        verifyingService)

            echo "   🟡 $SERVICE"
            echo "      Status : Verifying Service"
            ;;

        serviceRestored|resolved|mitigated)

            echo "   🔵 $SERVICE"
            echo "      Status : Restored / Resolved"
            ;;

        *)

            echo "   ⚪ $SERVICE"
            echo "      Status : $STATUS"
            ;;

    esac

done

echo


# ==============================================================
# COUNT STATUS
# ==============================================================

HEALTHY_SERVICES=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(.status == "serviceOperational")
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/health-array.json")


DEGRADED_SERVICES=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(.status == "serviceDegradation")
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/health-array.json")


INTERRUPTED_SERVICES=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(.status == "serviceInterruption")
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/health-array.json")


INVESTIGATING_SERVICES=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        .status == "investigating"
        or
        .status == "restoringService"
        or
        .status == "verifyingService"
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/health-array.json")


# ==============================================================
# 03 - SERVICE ISSUES
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "03. MICROSOFT 365 SERVICE ISSUES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

ISSUES_FILE="$TMP_DIR/issues.json"

az rest \
    --method GET \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/admin/serviceAnnouncement/issues?\$top=100" \
    > "$ISSUES_FILE" \
    2> "$TMP_DIR/issues-error.txt"


if [[ $? -ne 0 ]]; then

    echo "⚠️ Unable to retrieve service issues."
    echo

else

    if jq -e '.error' "$ISSUES_FILE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then

        echo "⚠️ Microsoft Graph returned an error:"
        jq -r '.error.message' "$ISSUES_FILE"
        echo

    else

        jq '.value // []' "$ISSUES_FILE" \
            > "$TMP_DIR/issues-array.json"

        TOTAL_ISSUES=$(jq 'length' \
            "$TMP_DIR/issues-array.json")


        echo "Issues Returned : $TOTAL_ISSUES"
        echo


        # ------------------------------------------------------
        # ACTIVE ISSUES
        # ------------------------------------------------------

        ACTIVE_ISSUES=$(jq '
        [
            .[]
            | select(
                (.status // "" | ascii_downcase)
                | IN(
                    "investigating",
                    "serviceinterruption",
                    "servicedegradation",
                    "restoringservice",
                    "verifyingservice",
                    "confirmed",
                    "reported"
                )
            )
        ] | length
        ' "$TMP_DIR/issues-array.json")


        RESOLVED_ISSUES=$(jq '
        [
            .[]
            | select(
                (.status // "" | ascii_downcase)
                | IN(
                    "resolved",
                    "servicerestored",
                    "mitigated",
                    "resolvedexternal"
                )
            )
        ] | length
        ' "$TMP_DIR/issues-array.json")


        echo "Active Issues   : $ACTIVE_ISSUES"
        echo "Resolved Issues : $RESOLVED_ISSUES"
        echo


        # ------------------------------------------------------
        # DISPLAY ACTIVE ISSUES
        # ------------------------------------------------------

        if [[ "$ACTIVE_ISSUES" -gt 0 ]]; then

            echo "🚨 ACTIVE MICROSOFT ISSUES"
            echo

            jq -r '
                .[]
                | select(
                    (.status // "" | ascii_downcase)
                    | IN(
                        "investigating",
                        "serviceinterruption",
                        "servicedegradation",
                        "restoringservice",
                        "verifyingservice",
                        "confirmed",
                        "reported"
                    )
                )
                |
                "   --------------------------------------------------------\n"
                + "   ID       : \(.id // "N/A")\n"
                + "   Service  : \(.service // "N/A")\n"
                + "   Title    : \(.title // .feature // "N/A")\n"
                + "   Status   : \(.status // "N/A")\n"
                + "   Start    : \(.startDateTime // "N/A")\n"
                + "   End      : \(.endDateTime // "N/A")\n"
            ' "$TMP_DIR/issues-array.json"

        else

            echo "   ✅ No active Microsoft 365 service issues detected."

        fi

    fi

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 04 - CUSTOMER IMPACT VIEW
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "04. CUSTOMER IMPACT VIEW"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo

if [[ "$INTERRUPTED_SERVICES" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "   🔴 Service interruption detected"

elif [[ "$DEGRADED_SERVICES" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "   🟠 Service degradation detected"

elif [[ "$INVESTIGATING_SERVICES" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "   🟡 Microsoft is investigating a service issue"

else

    echo "   🟢 No active service degradation detected"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 05 - COMMON SERVICES
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "05. KEY CUSTOMER SERVICES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo

for SERVICE_NAME in \
    "Exchange Online" \
    "Microsoft Teams" \
    "SharePoint Online" \
    "OneDrive" \
    "Identity Service" \
    "Microsoft 365 suite"

do

    RESULT=$(jq -r \
        --arg service "$SERVICE_NAME" \
        '.[] | select(.service == $service) | .status' \
        "$TMP_DIR/health-array.json" |
        head -1)

    if [[ -z "$RESULT" ]]; then

        echo "   ⚪ $SERVICE_NAME : Not returned"

    else

        case "$RESULT" in

            serviceOperational)
                echo "   🟢 $SERVICE_NAME : Operational"
                ;;

            serviceDegradation)
                echo "   🟠 $SERVICE_NAME : Degraded"
                ;;

            serviceInterruption)
                echo "   🔴 $SERVICE_NAME : Interrupted"
                ;;

            investigating)
                echo "   🟡 $SERVICE_NAME : Investigating"
                ;;

            restoringService)
                echo "   🟡 $SERVICE_NAME : Restoring"
                ;;

            *)
                echo "   ⚪ $SERVICE_NAME : $RESULT"
                ;;

        esac

    fi

done

echo


# ==============================================================
# FINAL SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | MICROSOFT 365 SERVICE HEALTH"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Services Monitored       : $TOTAL_SERVICES"
echo "Operational              : $HEALTHY_SERVICES"
echo "Degraded                 : $DEGRADED_SERVICES"
echo "Interrupted              : $INTERRUPTED_SERVICES"
echo "Investigating/Restoring  : $INVESTIGATING_SERVICES"
echo
echo "Service Issues           : $TOTAL_ISSUES"
echo "Active Issues            : $ACTIVE_ISSUES"
echo "Resolved Issues          : $RESOLVED_ISSUES"
echo


# ==============================================================
# OVERALL STATUS
# ==============================================================

if [[ "$INTERRUPTED_SERVICES" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status           : 🔴 SERVICE INTERRUPTION"

elif [[ "$DEGRADED_SERVICES" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status           : 🟠 SERVICE DEGRADATION"

elif [[ "$ACTIVE_ISSUES" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status           : 🟡 MICROSOFT ISSUE ACTIVE"

else

    echo "Overall Status           : 🟢 ALL SERVICES OPERATIONAL"

fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZenAiOps | Microsoft 365 Managed Services"
echo " Service Health • Incident Awareness • Customer Monitoring"
echo "============================================================"
echo

19 — Microsoft 365 Service Health & Incidents

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS 19 - MICROSOFT 365 SERVICE HEALTH
# ==============================================================
#
# Checks:
#   01. Microsoft 365 service health
#   02. Exchange Online
#   03. Microsoft Teams
#   04. SharePoint Online
#   05. OneDrive
#   06. Identity / Entra
#   07. Active service incidents
#   08. Service advisories / issues
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#
# Microsoft Graph permission:
#   ServiceHealth.Read.All
#
# ==============================================================

set -u

TMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)

cleanup() {
    rm -rf "$TMP_DIR"
}

trap cleanup EXIT


# ==============================================================
# COUNTERS
# ==============================================================

TOTAL_SERVICES=0
HEALTHY_SERVICES=0
DEGRADED_SERVICES=0
INTERRUPTED_SERVICES=0
INVESTIGATING_SERVICES=0

TOTAL_ISSUES=0
ACTIVE_ISSUES=0
RESOLVED_ISSUES=0


# ==============================================================
# HEADER
# ==============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 19 - MICROSOFT 365 SERVICE HEALTH"
echo "============================================================"
echo


# ==============================================================
# REQUIREMENTS
# ==============================================================

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ Azure CLI is required."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ jq is required."
    exit 1
fi


# ==============================================================
# 01 - SERVICE HEALTH OVERVIEW
# ==============================================================

echo "Getting Microsoft 365 service health..."
echo

HEALTH_FILE="$TMP_DIR/health.json"

az rest \
    --method GET \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/admin/serviceAnnouncement/healthOverviews" \
    > "$HEALTH_FILE" \
    2> "$TMP_DIR/health-error.txt"


if [[ $? -ne 0 ]]; then

    echo "❌ Unable to retrieve Microsoft 365 service health."
    echo
    cat "$TMP_DIR/health-error.txt"
    echo
    echo "Required Graph permission:"
    echo "   ServiceHealth.Read.All"
    echo

    exit 1

fi


# ==============================================================
# GRAPH ERROR
# ==============================================================

if jq -e '.error' "$HEALTH_FILE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo "❌ Microsoft Graph error:"
    jq -r '.error.message' "$HEALTH_FILE"
    echo

    exit 1

fi


# ==============================================================
# NORMALIZE
# ==============================================================

jq '.value // []' "$HEALTH_FILE" \
    > "$TMP_DIR/health-array.json"

TOTAL_SERVICES=$(jq 'length' "$TMP_DIR/health-array.json")


echo "Services Returned : $TOTAL_SERVICES"
echo


# ==============================================================
# 02 - SERVICE STATUS
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "02. MICROSOFT 365 SERVICE STATUS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo

jq -r '
    .[]
    |
    [
        .service // "Unknown",
        .status // "unknown"
    ]
    | @tsv
' "$TMP_DIR/health-array.json" |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r SERVICE STATUS; do

    case "$STATUS" in

        serviceOperational)

            echo "   🟢 $SERVICE"
            echo "      Status : Operational"
            ;;

        serviceDegradation)

            echo "   🟠 $SERVICE"
            echo "      Status : Service Degradation"
            ;;

        serviceInterruption)

            echo "   🔴 $SERVICE"
            echo "      Status : Service Interruption"
            ;;

        investigating)

            echo "   🟡 $SERVICE"
            echo "      Status : Investigating"
            ;;

        restoringService)

            echo "   🟡 $SERVICE"
            echo "      Status : Restoring Service"
            ;;

        verifyingService)

            echo "   🟡 $SERVICE"
            echo "      Status : Verifying Service"
            ;;

        serviceRestored|resolved|mitigated)

            echo "   🔵 $SERVICE"
            echo "      Status : Restored / Resolved"
            ;;

        *)

            echo "   ⚪ $SERVICE"
            echo "      Status : $STATUS"
            ;;

    esac

done

echo


# ==============================================================
# COUNT STATUS
# ==============================================================

HEALTHY_SERVICES=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(.status == "serviceOperational")
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/health-array.json")


DEGRADED_SERVICES=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(.status == "serviceDegradation")
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/health-array.json")


INTERRUPTED_SERVICES=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(.status == "serviceInterruption")
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/health-array.json")


INVESTIGATING_SERVICES=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        .status == "investigating"
        or
        .status == "restoringService"
        or
        .status == "verifyingService"
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/health-array.json")


# ==============================================================
# 03 - SERVICE ISSUES
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "03. MICROSOFT 365 SERVICE ISSUES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

ISSUES_FILE="$TMP_DIR/issues.json"

az rest \
    --method GET \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/admin/serviceAnnouncement/issues?\$top=100" \
    > "$ISSUES_FILE" \
    2> "$TMP_DIR/issues-error.txt"


if [[ $? -ne 0 ]]; then

    echo "⚠️ Unable to retrieve service issues."
    echo

else

    if jq -e '.error' "$ISSUES_FILE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then

        echo "⚠️ Microsoft Graph returned an error:"
        jq -r '.error.message' "$ISSUES_FILE"
        echo

    else

        jq '.value // []' "$ISSUES_FILE" \
            > "$TMP_DIR/issues-array.json"

        TOTAL_ISSUES=$(jq 'length' \
            "$TMP_DIR/issues-array.json")


        echo "Issues Returned : $TOTAL_ISSUES"
        echo


        # ------------------------------------------------------
        # ACTIVE ISSUES
        # ------------------------------------------------------

        ACTIVE_ISSUES=$(jq '
        [
            .[]
            | select(
                (.status // "" | ascii_downcase)
                | IN(
                    "investigating",
                    "serviceinterruption",
                    "servicedegradation",
                    "restoringservice",
                    "verifyingservice",
                    "confirmed",
                    "reported"
                )
            )
        ] | length
        ' "$TMP_DIR/issues-array.json")


        RESOLVED_ISSUES=$(jq '
        [
            .[]
            | select(
                (.status // "" | ascii_downcase)
                | IN(
                    "resolved",
                    "servicerestored",
                    "mitigated",
                    "resolvedexternal"
                )
            )
        ] | length
        ' "$TMP_DIR/issues-array.json")


        echo "Active Issues   : $ACTIVE_ISSUES"
        echo "Resolved Issues : $RESOLVED_ISSUES"
        echo


        # ------------------------------------------------------
        # DISPLAY ACTIVE ISSUES
        # ------------------------------------------------------

        if [[ "$ACTIVE_ISSUES" -gt 0 ]]; then

            echo "🚨 ACTIVE MICROSOFT ISSUES"
            echo

            jq -r '
                .[]
                | select(
                    (.status // "" | ascii_downcase)
                    | IN(
                        "investigating",
                        "serviceinterruption",
                        "servicedegradation",
                        "restoringservice",
                        "verifyingservice",
                        "confirmed",
                        "reported"
                    )
                )
                |
                "   --------------------------------------------------------\n"
                + "   ID       : \(.id // "N/A")\n"
                + "   Service  : \(.service // "N/A")\n"
                + "   Title    : \(.title // .feature // "N/A")\n"
                + "   Status   : \(.status // "N/A")\n"
                + "   Start    : \(.startDateTime // "N/A")\n"
                + "   End      : \(.endDateTime // "N/A")\n"
            ' "$TMP_DIR/issues-array.json"

        else

            echo "   ✅ No active Microsoft 365 service issues detected."

        fi

    fi

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 04 - CUSTOMER IMPACT VIEW
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "04. CUSTOMER IMPACT VIEW"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo

if [[ "$INTERRUPTED_SERVICES" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "   🔴 Service interruption detected"

elif [[ "$DEGRADED_SERVICES" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "   🟠 Service degradation detected"

elif [[ "$INVESTIGATING_SERVICES" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "   🟡 Microsoft is investigating a service issue"

else

    echo "   🟢 No active service degradation detected"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 05 - COMMON SERVICES
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "05. KEY CUSTOMER SERVICES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo

for SERVICE_NAME in \
    "Exchange Online" \
    "Microsoft Teams" \
    "SharePoint Online" \
    "OneDrive" \
    "Identity Service" \
    "Microsoft 365 suite"

do

    RESULT=$(jq -r \
        --arg service "$SERVICE_NAME" \
        '.[] | select(.service == $service) | .status' \
        "$TMP_DIR/health-array.json" |
        head -1)

    if [[ -z "$RESULT" ]]; then

        echo "   ⚪ $SERVICE_NAME : Not returned"

    else

        case "$RESULT" in

            serviceOperational)
                echo "   🟢 $SERVICE_NAME : Operational"
                ;;

            serviceDegradation)
                echo "   🟠 $SERVICE_NAME : Degraded"
                ;;

            serviceInterruption)
                echo "   🔴 $SERVICE_NAME : Interrupted"
                ;;

            investigating)
                echo "   🟡 $SERVICE_NAME : Investigating"
                ;;

            restoringService)
                echo "   🟡 $SERVICE_NAME : Restoring"
                ;;

            *)
                echo "   ⚪ $SERVICE_NAME : $RESULT"
                ;;

        esac

    fi

done

echo


# ==============================================================
# FINAL SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | MICROSOFT 365 SERVICE HEALTH"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Services Monitored       : $TOTAL_SERVICES"
echo "Operational              : $HEALTHY_SERVICES"
echo "Degraded                 : $DEGRADED_SERVICES"
echo "Interrupted              : $INTERRUPTED_SERVICES"
echo "Investigating/Restoring  : $INVESTIGATING_SERVICES"
echo
echo "Service Issues           : $TOTAL_ISSUES"
echo "Active Issues            : $ACTIVE_ISSUES"
echo "Resolved Issues          : $RESOLVED_ISSUES"
echo


# ==============================================================

# ==============================================================

if [[ "$INTERRUPTED_SERVICES" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status           : 🔴 SERVICE INTERRUPTION"

elif [[ "$DEGRADED_SERVICES" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status           : 🟠 SERVICE DEGRADATION"

elif [[ "$ACTIVE_ISSUES" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status           : 🟡 MICROSOFT ISSUE ACTIVE"

20 — Microsoft 365 License Optimization & Cost Waste

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS 20 - MICROSOFT 365 LICENSE OPTIMIZATION
# ==============================================================
#
# Checks:
#   01. License inventory
#   02. Purchased vs assigned
#   03. Available licenses
#   04. Disabled users with licenses
#   05. Never-used licensed users
#   06. Inactive licensed users
#   07. Potential license waste
#   08. Estimated monthly cost opportunity
#
# Usage:
#   ./20-m365-license-optimization.sh
#
# Optional:
#   ./20-m365-license-optimization.sh 90
#
# Default inactivity threshold = 90 days
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#
# Graph permissions:
#   User.Read.All
#   Organization.Read.All
#
# ==============================================================

set -u

INACTIVE_DAYS="${1:-90}"

TMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)

cleanup() {
    rm -rf "$TMP_DIR"
}

trap cleanup EXIT


# ==============================================================
# COUNTERS
# ==============================================================

TOTAL_USERS=0
LICENSED_USERS=0
DISABLED_LICENSED=0
NEVER_USED_LICENSED=0
INACTIVE_LICENSED=0

TOTAL_LICENSES=0
TOTAL_ASSIGNED=0
TOTAL_AVAILABLE=0

POTENTIAL_WASTE=0


# ==============================================================
# HEADER
# ==============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 20 - MICROSOFT 365 LICENSE OPTIMIZATION"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Inactive threshold : $INACTIVE_DAYS days"
echo


# ==============================================================
# REQUIREMENTS
# ==============================================================

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ Azure CLI is required."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ jq is required."
    exit 1
fi


# ==============================================================
# 01 - LICENSE INVENTORY
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "01. LICENSE INVENTORY"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

SKUS_FILE="$TMP_DIR/skus.json"

az rest \
    --method GET \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/subscribedSkus" \
    > "$SKUS_FILE" \
    2> "$TMP_DIR/skus-error.txt"


if [[ $? -ne 0 ]]; then

    echo "❌ Unable to retrieve Microsoft 365 licenses."
    echo
    cat "$TMP_DIR/skus-error.txt"
    exit 1

fi


if jq -e '.error' "$SKUS_FILE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo "❌ Microsoft Graph error:"
    jq -r '.error.message' "$SKUS_FILE"
    exit 1

fi


jq '.value // []' "$SKUS_FILE" \
    > "$TMP_DIR/skus-array.json"


echo

jq -r '
    .[]
    |
    [
        .skuPartNumber,
        (.prepaidUnits.enabled // 0),
        (.consumedUnits // 0),
        (
            (.prepaidUnits.enabled // 0)
            -
            (.consumedUnits // 0)
        )
    ]
    | @tsv
' "$TMP_DIR/skus-array.json" |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r SKU PURCHASED ASSIGNED AVAILABLE; do

    echo "   $SKU"
    echo "      Purchased : $PURCHASED"
    echo "      Assigned  : $ASSIGNED"
    echo "      Available : $AVAILABLE"
    echo

done


# ==============================================================
# TOTAL LICENSES
# ==============================================================

TOTAL_LICENSES=$(jq '
    [
        .[]
        | (.prepaidUnits.enabled // 0)
    ]
    | add // 0
' "$TMP_DIR/skus-array.json")


TOTAL_ASSIGNED=$(jq '
    [
        .[]
        | (.consumedUnits // 0)
    ]
    | add // 0
' "$TMP_DIR/skus-array.json")


TOTAL_AVAILABLE=$(jq '
    [
        .[]
        |
        (
            (.prepaidUnits.enabled // 0)
            -
            (.consumedUnits // 0)
        )
    ]
    | add // 0
' "$TMP_DIR/skus-array.json")


echo "Total Purchased : $TOTAL_LICENSES"
echo "Total Assigned  : $TOTAL_ASSIGNED"
echo "Total Available  : $TOTAL_AVAILABLE"
echo


# ==============================================================
# 02 - USER INVENTORY
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "02. USER LICENSE ANALYSIS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

USERS_FILE="$TMP_DIR/users.json"

az rest \
    --method GET \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/users?\$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,accountEnabled,assignedLicenses,signInActivity&\$top=500" \
    > "$USERS_FILE" \
    2> "$TMP_DIR/users-error.txt"


if [[ $? -ne 0 ]]; then

    echo "❌ Unable to retrieve users."
    cat "$TMP_DIR/users-error.txt"
    exit 1

fi


if jq -e '.error' "$USERS_FILE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo "❌ Microsoft Graph error:"
    jq -r '.error.message' "$USERS_FILE"
    exit 1

fi


jq '.value // []' "$USERS_FILE" \
    > "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json"


TOTAL_USERS=$(jq 'length' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json")


LICENSED_USERS=$(jq '
    [
        .[]
        | select(
            (.assignedLicenses // []) | length > 0
        )
    ] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json")


echo "Total Users    : $TOTAL_USERS"
echo "Licensed Users : $LICENSED_USERS"
echo


# ==============================================================
# DATE
# ==============================================================

CUTOFF_DATE=$(date -u -d "$INACTIVE_DAYS days ago" \
    +"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")


# ==============================================================
# 03 - DISABLED LICENSED USERS
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "03. DISABLED USERS WITH LICENSES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


DISABLED_LICENSED=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        .accountEnabled == false
        and
        ((.assignedLicenses // []) | length > 0)
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json")


echo "Count : $DISABLED_LICENSED"
echo


if [[ "$DISABLED_LICENSED" -gt 0 ]]; then

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(
            .accountEnabled == false
            and
            ((.assignedLicenses // []) | length > 0)
        )
        |
        "   🔴 \(.displayName)"
        + " | \(.userPrincipalName)"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json"

else

    echo "   ✅ No disabled users with licenses"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 04 - NEVER USED LICENSED USERS
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "04. NEVER-USED LICENSED USERS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


NEVER_USED_LICENSED=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        ((.assignedLicenses // []) | length > 0)
        and
        (
            .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == null
            or
            .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == ""
        )
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json")


echo "Count : $NEVER_USED_LICENSED"
echo


if [[ "$NEVER_USED_LICENSED" -gt 0 ]]; then

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(
            ((.assignedLicenses // []) | length > 0)
            and
            (
                .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == null
                or
                .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == ""
            )
        )
        |
        "   ⚠️  \(.displayName)"
        + " | \(.userPrincipalName)"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json"

else

    echo "   ✅ No never-used licensed users detected"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 05 - INACTIVE LICENSED USERS
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "05. INACTIVE LICENSED USERS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

INACTIVE_LICENSED=$(jq \
    --arg cutoff "$CUTOFF_DATE" '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        ((.assignedLicenses // []) | length > 0)
        and
        (.accountEnabled == true)
        and
        (.signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime != null)
        and
        (.signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime < $cutoff)
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json")


echo "Cutoff Date : $CUTOFF_DATE"
echo "Count       : $INACTIVE_LICENSED"
echo


if [[ "$INACTIVE_LICENSED" -gt 0 ]]; then

    jq -r \
        --arg cutoff "$CUTOFF_DATE" '
        .[]
        | select(
            ((.assignedLicenses // []) | length > 0)
            and
            (.accountEnabled == true)
            and
            (.signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime != null)
            and
            (.signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime < $cutoff)
        )
        |
        "   ⚠️  \(.displayName)"
        + " | \(.userPrincipalName)"
        + " | Last Sign-in: \(.signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime)"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json"

else

    echo "   ✅ No inactive licensed users detected"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 06 - POTENTIAL LICENSE WASTE
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "06. POTENTIAL LICENSE WASTE"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

# We deliberately don't automatically count every inactive user
# as removable.
#
# Candidate = disabled licensed user
#          OR never-used licensed user
#
# Active users inactive > threshold are reported separately.

POTENTIAL_WASTE=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        ((.assignedLicenses // []) | length > 0)
        and
        (
            .accountEnabled == false
            or
            (
                .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == null
                or
                .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == ""
            )
        )
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json")


echo "Potential license review candidates : $POTENTIAL_WASTE"
echo


if [[ "$POTENTIAL_WASTE" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "Candidates:"
    echo

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(
            ((.assignedLicenses // []) | length > 0)
            and
            (
                .accountEnabled == false
                or
                (
                    .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == null
                    or
                    .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == ""
                )
            )
        )
        |
        "   • \(.displayName)"
        + " | \(.userPrincipalName)"
        + " | Status: "
        + (
            if .accountEnabled == false
            then "DISABLED"
            else "NEVER USED"
            end
        )
    ' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json"

else

    echo "   ✅ No obvious license-waste candidates"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 07 - LICENSE UTILIZATION
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "07. LICENSE UTILIZATION"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


if [[ "$TOTAL_LICENSES" -gt 0 ]]; then

    UTILIZATION=$(awk \
        -v assigned="$TOTAL_ASSIGNED" \
        -v total="$TOTAL_LICENSES" \
        'BEGIN {printf "%.2f", (assigned/total)*100}')

else

    UTILIZATION="0.00"

fi


echo "Purchased Licenses : $TOTAL_LICENSES"
echo "Assigned Licenses  : $TOTAL_ASSIGNED"
echo "Available Licenses : $TOTAL_AVAILABLE"
echo "Utilization        : ${UTILIZATION}%"
echo


# ==============================================================
# 08 - COST OPPORTUNITY
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "08. ESTIMATED COST OPPORTUNITY"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo
echo "Enter estimated monthly license price if you want"
echo "ZenAiOps to calculate potential monthly savings."
echo
echo "Example:"
echo "  Business Premium = 2500"
echo "  Business Standard = 1500"
echo
echo "For this report, we use a simple blended price."
echo

read -r -p "Blended monthly license price (INR): " LICENSE_PRICE


if [[ "$LICENSE_PRICE" =~ ^[0-9]+([.][0-9]+)?$ ]]; then

    ESTIMATED_SAVINGS=$(awk \
        -v count="$POTENTIAL_WASTE" \
        -v price="$LICENSE_PRICE" \
        'BEGIN {printf "%.2f", count*price}')

    ANNUAL_SAVINGS=$(awk \
        -v monthly="$ESTIMATED_SAVINGS" \
        'BEGIN {printf "%.2f", monthly*12}')

    echo
    echo "Potential Candidates : $POTENTIAL_WASTE"
    echo "Estimated Monthly    : ₹$ESTIMATED_SAVINGS"
    echo "Estimated Annual     : ₹$ANNUAL_SAVINGS"

else

    ESTIMATED_SAVINGS="N/A"
    ANNUAL_SAVINGS="N/A"

    echo
    echo "⚠️ Cost calculation skipped."

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# FINAL SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | LICENSE OPTIMIZATION SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Total Users              : $TOTAL_USERS"
echo "Licensed Users           : $LICENSED_USERS"
echo
echo "Purchased Licenses       : $TOTAL_LICENSES"
echo "Assigned Licenses        : $TOTAL_ASSIGNED"
echo "Available Licenses       : $TOTAL_AVAILABLE"
echo "License Utilization      : ${UTILIZATION}%"
echo
echo "Disabled + Licensed      : $DISABLED_LICENSED"
echo "Never Used + Licensed    : $NEVER_USED_LICENSED"
echo "Inactive + Licensed      : $INACTIVE_LICENSED"
echo
echo "Potential Review         : $POTENTIAL_WASTE"
echo "Monthly Opportunity      : ₹$ESTIMATED_SAVINGS"
echo "Annual Opportunity       : ₹$ANNUAL_SAVINGS"
echo


# ==============================================================
# STATUS
# ==============================================================

if [[ "$POTENTIAL_WASTE" -gt 10 ]]; then

    echo "Optimization Status      : 🔴 HIGH OPPORTUNITY"

elif [[ "$POTENTIAL_WASTE" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "Optimization Status      : 🟡 REVIEW REQUIRED"

else

    echo "Optimization Status      : 🟢 NO OBVIOUS WASTE"

fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZenAiOps | Microsoft 365 Managed Services"
echo " License • Cost • Identity • Optimization"
echo "============================================================"
echo

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

21 — Microsoft 365 User Lifecycle — Joiner / Mover / Leaver

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS 21 - MICROSOFT 365 USER LIFECYCLE
# ==============================================================
#
# Checks:
#   01. New users / Joiners
#   02. Disabled users / Leavers
#   03. Disabled users with licenses
#   04. Disabled users with admin roles
#   05. Guests
#   06. Never-used accounts
#   07. Stale accounts
#   08. Lifecycle summary
#
# Usage:
#   ./21-m365-user-lifecycle.sh
#
# Optional:
#   ./21-m365-user-lifecycle.sh 30 90
#
#   30 = Joiner window in days
#   90 = Stale threshold in days
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#
# Graph permissions:
#   User.Read.All
#   Directory.Read.All
#   RoleManagement.Read.Directory
#
# ==============================================================

set -u

JOINER_DAYS="${1:-30}"
STALE_DAYS="${2:-90}"

TMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)

cleanup() {
    rm -rf "$TMP_DIR"
}

trap cleanup EXIT


# ==============================================================
# COUNTERS
# ==============================================================

TOTAL_USERS=0
JOINERS=0
LEAVERS=0
DISABLED_LICENSED=0
DISABLED_ADMINS=0
GUESTS=0
NEVER_USED=0
STALE_USERS=0


# ==============================================================
# HEADER
# ==============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 21 - MICROSOFT 365 USER LIFECYCLE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Joiner window : $JOINER_DAYS days"
echo "Stale window  : $STALE_DAYS days"
echo


# ==============================================================
# REQUIREMENTS
# ==============================================================

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ Azure CLI is required."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ jq is required."
    exit 1
fi


# ==============================================================
# DATES
# ==============================================================

JOINER_CUTOFF=$(date -u \
    -d "$JOINER_DAYS days ago" \
    +"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")

STALE_CUTOFF=$(date -u \
    -d "$STALE_DAYS days ago" \
    +"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")


# ==============================================================
# 01 - GET USERS
# ==============================================================

echo "Getting Microsoft 365 users..."
echo

USERS_FILE="$TMP_DIR/users.json"

az rest \
    --method GET \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/users?\$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,mail,userType,accountEnabled,createdDateTime,assignedLicenses,jobTitle,department,signInActivity&\$top=500" \
    > "$USERS_FILE" \
    2> "$TMP_DIR/users-error.txt"


if [[ $? -ne 0 ]]; then

    echo "❌ Unable to retrieve users."
    echo
    cat "$TMP_DIR/users-error.txt"
    exit 1

fi


if jq -e '.error' "$USERS_FILE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo "❌ Microsoft Graph error:"
    jq -r '.error.message' "$USERS_FILE"
    exit 1

fi


jq '.value // []' "$USERS_FILE" \
    > "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json"


TOTAL_USERS=$(jq 'length' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json")


echo "Total Users : $TOTAL_USERS"
echo


# ==============================================================
# 02 - JOINERS
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "02. JOINERS - RECENTLY CREATED USERS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

JOINERS=$(jq \
    --arg cutoff "$JOINER_CUTOFF" '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        .createdDateTime != null
        and
        .createdDateTime >= $cutoff
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json")


echo "Users created in last $JOINER_DAYS days : $JOINERS"
echo


if [[ "$JOINERS" -gt 0 ]]; then

    jq -r \
        --arg cutoff "$JOINER_CUTOFF" '
        .[]
        | select(
            .createdDateTime != null
            and
            .createdDateTime >= $cutoff
        )
        |
        "   🆕 \(.displayName)"
        + " | \(.userPrincipalName)"
        + " | Created: \(.createdDateTime)"
        + " | Enabled: \(.accountEnabled)"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json"

else

    echo "   No new users detected."

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 03 - LEAVERS
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "03. LEAVERS - DISABLED USERS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

LEAVERS=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(.accountEnabled == false)
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json")


echo "Disabled Users : $LEAVERS"
echo


if [[ "$LEAVERS" -gt 0 ]]; then

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(.accountEnabled == false)
        |
        "   🚪 \(.displayName)"
        + " | \(.userPrincipalName)"
        + " | Licensed: "
        + (
            if ((.assignedLicenses // []) | length) > 0
            then "YES"
            else "NO"
            end
        )
    ' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json"

else

    echo "   ✅ No disabled users."

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 04 - DISABLED USERS WITH LICENSES
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "04. LEAVERS WITH ACTIVE LICENSES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

DISABLED_LICENSED=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        .accountEnabled == false
        and
        ((.assignedLicenses // []) | length > 0)
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json")


echo "Disabled + Licensed : $DISABLED_LICENSED"
echo


if [[ "$DISABLED_LICENSED" -gt 0 ]]; then

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(
            .accountEnabled == false
            and
            ((.assignedLicenses // []) | length > 0)
        )
        |
        "   ⚠️  \(.displayName)"
        + " | \(.userPrincipalName)"
        + " | LICENSE STILL ASSIGNED"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json"

else

    echo "   ✅ No disabled users retaining licenses."

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 05 - ADMIN ROLES
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "05. DISABLED USERS WITH ADMIN ROLES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

ROLES_FILE="$TMP_DIR/roles.json"

az rest \
    --method GET \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/directoryRoles?\$select=id,displayName" \
    > "$ROLES_FILE" \
    2> "$TMP_DIR/roles-error.txt"


if [[ $? -ne 0 ]]; then

    echo "⚠️ Unable to retrieve directory roles."

else

    jq '.value // []' "$ROLES_FILE" \
        > "$TMP_DIR/roles-array.json"

    : > "$TMP_DIR/admin-users.json"


    while IFS=$'\t' read -r ROLE_ID ROLE_NAME; do

        MEMBERS_FILE="$TMP_DIR/members-$RANDOM.json"

        az rest \
            --method GET \
            --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/directoryRoles/$ROLE_ID/members?\$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,accountEnabled" \
            > "$MEMBERS_FILE" \
            2>/dev/null || continue


        jq \
            --arg role "$ROLE_NAME" '
            .value // []
            | .[]
            | {
                id: .id,
                displayName: .displayName,
                userPrincipalName: .userPrincipalName,
                accountEnabled: .accountEnabled,
                role: $role
            }
        ' "$MEMBERS_FILE" >> "$TMP_DIR/admin-users.json"

    done < <(
        jq -r '
            .[]
            | [
                .id,
                .displayName
            ]
            | @tsv
        ' "$TMP_DIR/roles-array.json"
    )


    # ----------------------------------------------------------
    # Normalize
    # ----------------------------------------------------------

    if [[ -s "$TMP_DIR/admin-users.json" ]]; then

        jq -s '.' "$TMP_DIR/admin-users.json" \
            > "$TMP_DIR/admin-users-array.json"

    else

        echo "[]" > "$TMP_DIR/admin-users-array.json"

    fi


    DISABLED_ADMINS=$(jq '
        [
            .[]
            | select(.accountEnabled == false)
        ] | length
    ' "$TMP_DIR/admin-users-array.json")


    echo "Disabled Admin Assignments : $DISABLED_ADMINS"
    echo


    if [[ "$DISABLED_ADMINS" -gt 0 ]]; then

        jq -r '
            .[]
            | select(.accountEnabled == false)
            |
            "   🚨 \(.displayName)"
            + " | \(.userPrincipalName)"
            + " | Role: \(.role)"
        ' "$TMP_DIR/admin-users-array.json"

    else

        echo "   ✅ No disabled users with directory-role assignments."

    fi

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 06 - GUEST USERS
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "06. GUEST USERS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

GUESTS=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(.userType == "Guest")
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json")


echo "Guest Users : $GUESTS"
echo


if [[ "$GUESTS" -gt 0 ]]; then

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(.userType == "Guest")
        |
        "   👤 \(.displayName)"
        + " | \(.userPrincipalName)"
        + " | Enabled: \(.accountEnabled)"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 07 - NEVER USED
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "07. NEVER-USED ACCOUNTS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

NEVER_USED=$(jq '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == null
        or
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == ""
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json")


echo "Never-used users : $NEVER_USED"
echo


if [[ "$NEVER_USED" -gt 0 ]]; then

    jq -r '
        .[]
        | select(
            .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == null
            or
            .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime == ""
        )
        |
        "   ⚠️  \(.displayName)"
        + " | \(.userPrincipalName)"
        + " | Enabled: \(.accountEnabled)"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json" |
    head -50

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 08 - STALE USERS
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "08. STALE USERS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

STALE_USERS=$(jq \
    --arg cutoff "$STALE_CUTOFF" '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        .accountEnabled == true
        and
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime != null
        and
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime != ""
        and
        .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime < $cutoff
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json")


echo "Stale users > $STALE_DAYS days : $STALE_USERS"
echo


if [[ "$STALE_USERS" -gt 0 ]]; then

    jq -r \
        --arg cutoff "$STALE_CUTOFF" '
        .[]
        | select(
            .accountEnabled == true
            and
            .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime != null
            and
            .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime != ""
            and
            .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime < $cutoff
        )
        |
        "   ⚠️  \(.displayName)"
        + " | \(.userPrincipalName)"
        + " | Last Sign-in: \(.signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime)"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/users-array.json" |
    head -50

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 09 - LIFECYCLE RISK
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "09. LIFECYCLE RISK"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

RISK_COUNT=0


# Disabled + licensed
RISK_COUNT=$((RISK_COUNT + DISABLED_LICENSED))

# Disabled admins
RISK_COUNT=$((RISK_COUNT + DISABLED_ADMINS))


echo "Lifecycle Risk Candidates : $RISK_COUNT"
echo


if [[ "$RISK_COUNT" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "🚨 Lifecycle actions recommended:"
    echo

    if [[ "$DISABLED_LICENSED" -gt 0 ]]; then
        echo "   • Review licenses assigned to disabled users"
    fi

    if [[ "$DISABLED_ADMINS" -gt 0 ]]; then
        echo "   • Review admin-role assignments for disabled users"
    fi

else

    echo "   ✅ No immediate lifecycle risks detected."

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# FINAL SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | USER LIFECYCLE SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Total Users              : $TOTAL_USERS"
echo "New Users / Joiners      : $JOINERS"
echo "Disabled / Leavers       : $LEAVERS"
echo "Disabled + Licensed      : $DISABLED_LICENSED"
echo "Disabled + Admin         : $DISABLED_ADMINS"
echo "Guest Users              : $GUESTS"
echo "Never Used               : $NEVER_USED"
echo "Stale Users              : $STALE_USERS"
echo
echo "Lifecycle Risk           : $RISK_COUNT"
echo


# ==============================================================
# STATUS
# ==============================================================

if [[ "$DISABLED_ADMINS" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status           : 🚨 HIGH RISK"

elif [[ "$DISABLED_LICENSED" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status           : 🔴 ACTION REQUIRED"

elif [[ "$STALE_USERS" -gt 0 ||
        "$NEVER_USED" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status           : 🟡 REVIEW REQUIRED"

else

    echo "Overall Status           : 🟢 HEALTHY"

fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZenAiOps | Microsoft 365 Managed Services"
echo " Identity • Lifecycle • License • Security"
echo "============================================================"
echo

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

22 — SharePoint & OneDrive Governance

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS 22 - SHAREPOINT & ONEDRIVE GOVERNANCE
# ==============================================================
#
# Checks:
#   01. SharePoint tenant sharing configuration
#   02. External sharing
#   03. Anonymous / Anyone links
#   04. SharePoint sites
#   05. OneDrive sites
#   06. Site storage
#   07. Inactive sites
#   08. Governance summary
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#   - pwsh
#   - Microsoft.Online.SharePoint.PowerShell
#
# Permissions:
#   SharePoint Administrator
#   Graph Sites.Read.All
#
# ==============================================================

set -u

INACTIVE_DAYS="${1:-90}"

TMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)

cleanup() {
    rm -rf "$TMP_DIR"
}

trap cleanup EXIT


# ==============================================================
# COUNTERS
# ==============================================================

TOTAL_SITES=0
EXTERNAL_SITES=0
ANONYMOUS_SITES=0
INACTIVE_SITES=0

TOTAL_STORAGE_GB=0


# ==============================================================
# HEADER
# ==============================================================

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 22 - SHAREPOINT & ONEDRIVE GOVERNANCE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Inactive threshold : $INACTIVE_DAYS days"
echo


# ==============================================================
# REQUIREMENTS
# ==============================================================

if ! command -v az >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ Azure CLI is required."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ jq is required."
    exit 1
fi

if ! command -v pwsh >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "❌ PowerShell 7 is required."
    exit 1
fi


# ==============================================================
# 01 - SHAREPOINT TENANT CONFIGURATION
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "01. SHAREPOINT TENANT SHARING CONFIGURATION"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

SPO_CONFIG="$TMP_DIR/spo-config.json"


pwsh -NoProfile -Command '

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

try {

    Import-Module Microsoft.Online.SharePoint.PowerShell `
        -ErrorAction Stop

    # ----------------------------------------------------------
    # Find SharePoint admin URL from tenant domains
    # ----------------------------------------------------------

    $domains = Get-SPOTenant `
        -ErrorAction Stop

    [PSCustomObject]@{

        SharingCapability =
            $domains.SharingCapability

        DefaultSharingLinkType =
            $domains.DefaultSharingLinkType

        DefaultLinkPermission =
            $domains.DefaultLinkPermission

        OneDriveSharingCapability =
            $domains.OneDriveSharingCapability

        ShowPeoplePickerSuggestionsForGuestUsers =
            $domains.ShowPeoplePickerSuggestionsForGuestUsers

        ExternalUserExpirationRequired =
            $domains.ExternalUserExpirationRequired

        ExternalUserExpirationInDays =
            $domains.ExternalUserExpirationInDays

    } | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 5

}
catch {

    Write-Error $_.Exception.Message
    exit 1

}

' > "$SPO_CONFIG" 2>/dev/null


if [[ -s "$SPO_CONFIG" ]]; then

    echo

    SHARING_CAPABILITY=$(jq -r \
        '.SharingCapability // "Unknown"' \
        "$SPO_CONFIG")

    DEFAULT_LINK=$(jq -r \
        '.DefaultSharingLinkType // "Unknown"' \
        "$SPO_CONFIG")

    DEFAULT_PERMISSION=$(jq -r \
        '.DefaultLinkPermission // "Unknown"' \
        "$SPO_CONFIG")

    ONEDRIVE_SHARING=$(jq -r \
        '.OneDriveSharingCapability // "Unknown"' \
        "$SPO_CONFIG")


    echo "Sharing Capability      : $SHARING_CAPABILITY"
    echo "Default Link Type       : $DEFAULT_LINK"
    echo "Default Link Permission : $DEFAULT_PERMISSION"
    echo "OneDrive Sharing        : $ONEDRIVE_SHARING"
    echo


    case "$SHARING_CAPABILITY" in

        Disabled)

            echo "   🟢 External sharing disabled"
            ;;

        ExistingExternalUserSharingOnly)

            echo "   🟢 Existing external users only"
            ;;

        ExternalUserSharingOnly)

            echo "   🟡 External sharing allowed"
            ;;

        Anyone)

            echo "   🔴 Anyone links permitted"
            ;;

        *)

            echo "   ⚪ Sharing configuration: $SHARING_CAPABILITY"
            ;;

    esac

else

    echo "⚠️ Unable to retrieve SharePoint tenant configuration."

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 02 - SHAREPOINT SITES
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "02. SHAREPOINT SITES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

SITES_FILE="$TMP_DIR/sites.json"


az rest \
    --method GET \
    --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/sites?search=*&\$select=id,name,displayName,webUrl,createdDateTime,lastModifiedDateTime,siteCollection" \
    > "$SITES_FILE" \
    2> "$TMP_DIR/sites-error.txt"


if [[ $? -ne 0 ]]; then

    echo "❌ Unable to retrieve SharePoint sites."
    cat "$TMP_DIR/sites-error.txt"
    exit 1

fi


if jq -e '.error' "$SITES_FILE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo "❌ Microsoft Graph error:"
    jq -r '.error.message' "$SITES_FILE"
    exit 1

fi


jq '.value // []' "$SITES_FILE" \
    > "$TMP_DIR/sites-array.json"


TOTAL_SITES=$(jq 'length' "$TMP_DIR/sites-array.json")


echo "Sites Returned : $TOTAL_SITES"
echo


jq -r '
    .[]
    |
    [
        .displayName // .name // "Unnamed",
        .webUrl // "N/A",
        .createdDateTime // "N/A",
        .lastModifiedDateTime // "N/A"
    ]
    | @tsv
' "$TMP_DIR/sites-array.json" |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r NAME URL CREATED MODIFIED; do

    echo "   📁 $NAME"
    echo "      URL      : $URL"
    echo "      Created  : $CREATED"
    echo "      Modified : $MODIFIED"
    echo

done

echo


# ==============================================================
# 03 - INACTIVE SITES
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "03. INACTIVE SHAREPOINT SITES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


INACTIVE_CUTOFF=$(date -u \
    -d "$INACTIVE_DAYS days ago" \
    +"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")


INACTIVE_SITES=$(jq \
    --arg cutoff "$INACTIVE_CUTOFF" '
[
    .[]
    | select(
        .lastModifiedDateTime != null
        and
        .lastModifiedDateTime < $cutoff
    )
] | length
' "$TMP_DIR/sites-array.json")


echo "Inactive Sites > $INACTIVE_DAYS days : $INACTIVE_SITES"
echo


if [[ "$INACTIVE_SITES" -gt 0 ]]; then

    jq -r \
        --arg cutoff "$INACTIVE_CUTOFF" '
        .[]
        | select(
            .lastModifiedDateTime != null
            and
            .lastModifiedDateTime < $cutoff
        )
        |
        "   ⚠️  \(.displayName // .name)"
        + " | Last Modified: \(.lastModifiedDateTime)"
    ' "$TMP_DIR/sites-array.json"

else

    echo "   ✅ No inactive sites detected."

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 04 - SITE PERMISSIONS / EXTERNAL SHARING
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "04. EXTERNAL SHARING"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo
echo "Checking site permissions..."
echo


# Check first 50 sites to avoid excessive Graph requests
SITE_IDS=$(jq -r '.[].id' "$TMP_DIR/sites-array.json" |
    head -50)


for SITE_ID in $SITE_IDS; do

    PERMISSIONS_FILE="$TMP_DIR/permissions-$RANDOM.json"

    az rest \
        --method GET \
        --url "https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0/sites/$SITE_ID/permissions" \
        > "$PERMISSIONS_FILE" \
        2>/dev/null || continue


    if jq -e '.error' "$PERMISSIONS_FILE" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        continue
    fi


    EXTERNAL_COUNT=$(jq '
        [
            .value[]?
            |
            select(
                (.grantedToIdentitiesV2 // []) |
                tostring |
                test(
                    "external|guest|anonymous",
                    "i"
                )
            )
        ] | length
    ' "$PERMISSIONS_FILE" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)


    if [[ "$EXTERNAL_COUNT" -gt 0 ]]; then

        EXTERNAL_SITES=$((EXTERNAL_SITES + 1))

    fi

done


echo "Sites with possible external permissions : $EXTERNAL_SITES"
echo


# ==============================================================
# 05 - ONEDRIVE
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "05. ONEDRIVE GOVERNANCE"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo

if [[ -s "$SPO_CONFIG" ]]; then

    case "$ONEDRIVE_SHARING" in

        Disabled)

            echo "   🟢 OneDrive external sharing disabled"
            ;;

        ExistingExternalUserSharingOnly)

            echo "   🟢 OneDrive limited to existing external users"
            ;;

        ExternalUserSharingOnly)

            echo "   🟡 OneDrive external sharing enabled"
            ;;

        Anyone)

            echo "   🔴 OneDrive Anyone sharing enabled"
            ;;

        *)

            echo "   ⚪ OneDrive sharing: $ONEDRIVE_SHARING"
            ;;

    esac

else

    echo "   ⚠️ OneDrive sharing configuration unavailable"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 06 - ANONYMOUS / ANYONE LINK
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "06. ANONYMOUS / ANYONE SHARING"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


if [[ "$SHARING_CAPABILITY" == "Anyone" ||
      "$ONEDRIVE_SHARING" == "Anyone" ]]; then

    ANONYMOUS_SITES=1

    echo "   🔴 Anyone-link sharing is permitted"

else

    ANONYMOUS_SITES=0

    echo "   🟢 Tenant-level Anyone sharing is not enabled"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 07 - EXTERNAL USER EXPIRATION
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "07. EXTERNAL USER EXPIRATION"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"


if [[ -s "$SPO_CONFIG" ]]; then

    EXPIRATION_REQUIRED=$(jq -r \
        '.ExternalUserExpirationRequired // false' \
        "$SPO_CONFIG")

    EXPIRATION_DAYS=$(jq -r \
        '.ExternalUserExpirationInDays // "N/A"' \
        "$SPO_CONFIG")


    echo "Expiration Required : $EXPIRATION_REQUIRED"
    echo "Expiration Days     : $EXPIRATION_DAYS"
    echo

    if [[ "$EXPIRATION_REQUIRED" == "true" ||
          "$EXPIRATION_REQUIRED" == "True" ]]; then

        echo "   ✅ External user expiration configured"

    else

        echo "   ⚠️ External user expiration is not enforced"

    fi

else

    echo "   ⚠️ Configuration unavailable"

fi

echo


# ==============================================================
# 08 - GOVERNANCE SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | SHAREPOINT GOVERNANCE SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "SharePoint Sites             : $TOTAL_SITES"
echo "Inactive Sites               : $INACTIVE_SITES"
echo "Possible External Sites      : $EXTERNAL_SITES"
echo "Anyone Sharing               : $ANONYMOUS_SITES"
echo
echo "Tenant Sharing               : $SHARING_CAPABILITY"
echo "OneDrive Sharing             : $ONEDRIVE_SHARING"
echo "Default Link Type            : $DEFAULT_LINK"
echo "Default Link Permission      : $DEFAULT_PERMISSION"
echo


# ==============================================================
# STATUS
# ==============================================================

if [[ "$ANONYMOUS_SITES" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status               : 🔴 ACTION REQUIRED"

elif [[ "$EXTERNAL_SITES" -gt 10 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status               : 🟠 REVIEW REQUIRED"

elif [[ "$INACTIVE_SITES" -gt 0 ]]; then

    echo "Overall Status               : 🟡 REVIEW REQUIRED"

else

    echo "Overall Status               : 🟢 HEALTHY"

fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZenAiOps | Microsoft 365 Managed Services"
echo " SharePoint • OneDrive • Data Governance"
echo "============================================================"
echo

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
23 — Microsoft 365 Teams Governance

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - 23 MICROSOFT 365 TEAMS GOVERNANCE
# ==============================================================
#
# Checks:
#   1. Teams inventory
#   2. Public / Private Teams
#   3. Team owners
#   4. Teams with no owner
#   5. Teams with only one owner
#   6. Guest members
#   7. Guest-heavy Teams
#   8. Teams activity
#   9. Inactive Teams
#  10. External users
#  11. Expiring Teams
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#   - Microsoft Graph application permissions:
#
#     Team.ReadBasic.All
#     TeamMember.Read.All
#     Reports.Read.All
#
# ==============================================================
# Microsoft Graph:
#   https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0
#
# ==============================================================

set -o pipefail

GRAPH="https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0"
