# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CONFIGURATION
# --------------------------------------------------------------

ACTIVITY_PERIOD="D30"

# Teams with no activity for this many days = INACTIVE
INACTIVE_DAYS=30

# Teams with this percentage of members as guests = REVIEW
GUEST_PERCENT_THRESHOLD=30

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# COLORS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
RED='\033[0;31m'
CYAN='\033[0;36m'
NC='\033[0m'

PASS="${GREEN}PASS${NC}"
WARN="${YELLOW}WARN${NC}"
FAIL="${RED}FAIL${NC}"

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# FUNCTIONS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

graph_get() {

    local URL="$1"

    az rest \
        --method get \
        --url "$URL" \
        --headers "Accept=application/json"
}

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# HEADER
# --------------------------------------------------------------

clear

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | MICROSOFT 365 TEAMS GOVERNANCE"
echo "============================================================"
echo
echo " Activity Period : $ACTIVITY_PERIOD"
echo " Inactive Days   : $INACTIVE_DAYS"
echo " Guest Threshold : ${GUEST_PERCENT_THRESHOLD}%"
echo
echo "============================================================"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CHECK LOGIN
# --------------------------------------------------------------

if ! az account show >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} Azure CLI login required"
    echo
    echo "Run:"
    echo "  az login"
    echo

    exit 1
fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 01 - GET ALL TEAMS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Getting Microsoft Teams..."

TEAMS_JSON=$(graph_get \
    "$GRAPH/teams?\$top=999&\$select=id,displayName,description,visibility")

if [ $? -ne 0 ] || [ -z "$TEAMS_JSON" ]; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} Unable to retrieve Teams"
    echo

    exit 1
fi

TEAM_COUNT=$(echo "$TEAMS_JSON" | jq '.value | length')

echo -e "${PASS} Teams retrieved: $TEAM_COUNT"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 02 - TEAM TYPE SUMMARY
# --------------------------------------------------------------

PRIVATE_TEAMS=$(echo "$TEAMS_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.visibility=="private")] | length')

PUBLIC_TEAMS=$(echo "$TEAMS_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.visibility=="public")] | length')

OTHER_TEAMS=$(echo "$TEAMS_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.visibility!="private" and .visibility!="public")] | length')

echo "============================================================"
echo " TEAM SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo
echo "Total Teams       : $TEAM_COUNT"
echo "Private Teams     : $PRIVATE_TEAMS"
echo "Public Teams      : $PUBLIC_TEAMS"
echo "Other             : $OTHER_TEAMS"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 03 - PREPARE TEAM INVENTORY
# --------------------------------------------------------------

REPORT_FILE="/tmp/zenaiops-teams-$$.json"

echo "[]" > "$REPORT_FILE"

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 04 - PROCESS EACH TEAM
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " TEAM GOVERNANCE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

INDEX=0

while read -r TEAM; do

    INDEX=$((INDEX + 1))

    TEAM_ID=$(echo "$TEAM" | jq -r '.id')
    TEAM_NAME=$(echo "$TEAM" | jq -r '.displayName')
    VISIBILITY=$(echo "$TEAM" | jq -r '.visibility')

    echo
    echo "[$INDEX/$TEAM_COUNT] $TEAM_NAME"

    # ----------------------------------------------------------
    # MEMBERS
    # ----------------------------------------------------------

    MEMBERS_JSON=$(graph_get \
        "$GRAPH/teams/$TEAM_ID/members?\$top=999")

    if [ $? -ne 0 ] || [ -z "$MEMBERS_JSON" ]; then

        echo -e "  ${WARN} Unable to retrieve members"

        MEMBER_COUNT=0
        OWNER_COUNT=0
        GUEST_COUNT=0

    else

        MEMBER_COUNT=$(echo "$MEMBERS_JSON" |
            jq '.value | length')

        OWNER_COUNT=$(echo "$MEMBERS_JSON" |
            jq '[.value[] | select(.roles != null and (.roles | index("owner")) != null)] | length')

        GUEST_COUNT=$(echo "$MEMBERS_JSON" |
            jq '[.value[] | select(.tenantId != null and .email != null)] | length')

    fi

    # ----------------------------------------------------------
    # GUEST CALCULATION
    # ----------------------------------------------------------

    if [ "$MEMBER_COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

        GUEST_PERCENT=$(awk \
            "BEGIN {printf \"%.1f\", ($GUEST_COUNT/$MEMBER_COUNT)*100}")

    else

        GUEST_PERCENT="0.0"

    fi

    # ----------------------------------------------------------
    # OWNER STATUS
    # ----------------------------------------------------------

    OWNER_STATUS="OK"

    if [ "$OWNER_COUNT" -eq 0 ]; then

        OWNER_STATUS="NO_OWNER"

        echo -e "  ${FAIL} No Team Owner"

    elif [ "$OWNER_COUNT" -eq 1 ]; then

        OWNER_STATUS="SINGLE_OWNER"

        echo -e "  ${WARN} Single Owner"

    else

        echo -e "  ${PASS} Owners: $OWNER_COUNT"

    fi

    # ----------------------------------------------------------
    # GUEST STATUS
    # ----------------------------------------------------------

    GUEST_STATUS="NORMAL"

    if [ "$GUEST_COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

        if awk "BEGIN {exit !($GUEST_PERCENT >= $GUEST_PERCENT_THRESHOLD)}"; then

            GUEST_STATUS="HIGH_GUEST_RATIO"

            echo -e "  ${WARN} Guest ratio: ${GUEST_PERCENT}%"

        else

            echo "  Guests: $GUEST_COUNT (${GUEST_PERCENT}%)"

        fi

    else

        echo "  Guests: 0"

    fi

    # ----------------------------------------------------------
    # STORE RESULT
    # ----------------------------------------------------------

    jq \
        --arg id "$TEAM_ID" \
        --arg name "$TEAM_NAME" \
        --arg visibility "$VISIBILITY" \
        --argjson members "$MEMBER_COUNT" \
        --argjson owners "$OWNER_COUNT" \
        --argjson guests "$GUEST_COUNT" \
        --arg guestPercent "$GUEST_PERCENT" \
        --arg ownerStatus "$OWNER_STATUS" \
        --arg guestStatus "$GUEST_STATUS" \
        '. + [{
            id: $id,
            displayName: $name,
            visibility: $visibility,
            members: $members,
            owners: $owners,
            guests: $guests,
            guestPercent: ($guestPercent | tonumber),
            ownerStatus: $ownerStatus,
            guestStatus: $guestStatus
        }]' \
        "$REPORT_FILE" > "${REPORT_FILE}.tmp" &&
        mv "${REPORT_FILE}.tmp" "$REPORT_FILE"

done < <(echo "$TEAMS_JSON" | jq -c '.value[]')

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 05 - GOVERNANCE SUMMARY
# --------------------------------------------------------------

NO_OWNER=$(jq \
    '[.[] | select(.ownerStatus=="NO_OWNER")] | length' \
    "$REPORT_FILE")

SINGLE_OWNER=$(jq \
    '[.[] | select(.ownerStatus=="SINGLE_OWNER")] | length' \
    "$REPORT_FILE")

TEAMS_WITH_GUESTS=$(jq \
    '[.[] | select(.guests > 0)] | length' \
    "$REPORT_FILE")

HIGH_GUEST_TEAMS=$(jq \
    '[.[] | select(.guestStatus=="HIGH_GUEST_RATIO")] | length' \
    "$REPORT_FILE")

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " GOVERNANCE SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo
echo "Total Teams              : $TEAM_COUNT"
echo "No Owner                 : $NO_OWNER"
echo "Single Owner             : $SINGLE_OWNER"
echo "Teams With Guests        : $TEAMS_WITH_GUESTS"
echo "High Guest Ratio         : $HIGH_GUEST_TEAMS"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 06 - TEAMS ACTIVITY REPORT
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " TEAMS ACTIVITY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Getting Microsoft Teams activity report..."

ACTIVITY_LOCATION=$(az rest \
    --method get \
    --url "$GRAPH/reports/getTeamsTeamActivityDetail(period='$ACTIVITY_PERIOD')" \
    --headers "Accept=text/csv" \
    --query 'headers' \
    -o tsv 2>/dev/null)

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# Microsoft Graph reports API returns a redirect.
# Get Location header directly.
# --------------------------------------------------------------

ACTIVITY_HEADERS=$(az rest \
    --method get \
    --url "$GRAPH/reports/getTeamsTeamActivityDetail(period='$ACTIVITY_PERIOD')" \
    --headers "Accept=text/csv" \
    --query 'headers' \
    -o json 2>/dev/null)

DOWNLOAD_URL=$(echo "$ACTIVITY_HEADERS" |
    jq -r '.location // empty')

if [ -n "$DOWNLOAD_URL" ] && [ "$DOWNLOAD_URL" != "null" ]; then

    ACTIVITY_FILE="/tmp/zenaiops-team-activity-$$.csv"

    curl -L \
        --silent \
        --show-error \
        "$DOWNLOAD_URL" \
        -o "$ACTIVITY_FILE"

    if [ $? -eq 0 ] && [ -s "$ACTIVITY_FILE" ]; then

        echo -e "${PASS} Activity report downloaded"
        echo

        echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
        echo " INACTIVE TEAMS"
        echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
        echo

        python3 - "$ACTIVITY_FILE" "$INACTIVE_DAYS" <<'PY'
import csv
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone

file = sys.argv[1]
inactive_days = int(sys.argv[2])

rows = []

with open(file, encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:
    reader = csv.DictReader(f)

    for row in reader:

        team = row.get("Team Name", "")
        team_id = row.get("Team Id", "")
        last_activity = row.get("Last Activity Date", "")
        active_users = row.get("Active users", "0")
        guests = row.get("Guests", "0")
        external = row.get("Active External Users", "0")

        if not team:
            continue

        if not last_activity:
            print(
                f"NO ACTIVITY DATA | {team} | "
                f"Users:{active_users} | Guests:{guests}"
            )
            continue

        try:
            dt = datetime.strptime(
                last_activity,
                "%Y-%m-%d"
            ).replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)

            age = (
                datetime.now(timezone.utc) - dt
            ).days

        except Exception:
            continue

        if age >= inactive_days:

            rows.append(
                (
                    age,
                    team,
                    team_id,
                    active_users,
                    guests,
                    external,
                    last_activity
                )
            )

rows.sort(reverse=True)

if not rows:

    print("No inactive Teams found.")

else:

    for r in rows:

        age, team, team_id, users, guests, external, last = r

        print(
            f"INACTIVE {age:>4} days | "
            f"{team} | "
            f"Last:{last} | "
            f"Users:{users} | "
            f"Guests:{guests} | "
            f"External:{external}"
        )

print()
print(f"Inactive Teams: {len(rows)}")

PY

        echo
        echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
        echo " ACTIVITY REPORT SUMMARY"
        echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
        echo

        python3 - "$ACTIVITY_FILE" <<'PY'
import csv
import sys

file = sys.argv[1]

total = 0
active_users = 0
guests = 0
external = 0

with open(file, encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:

    reader = csv.DictReader(f)

    for row in reader:

        total += 1

        try:
            active_users += int(row.get("Active users", 0) or 0)
        except:
            pass

        try:
            guests += int(row.get("Guests", 0) or 0)
        except:
            pass

        try:
            external += int(
                row.get("Active External Users", 0) or 0
            )
        except:
            pass

print(f"Teams in Report       : {total}")
print(f"Active Users           : {active_users}")
print(f"Guest Activity         : {guests}")
print(f"External User Activity : {external}")

PY

    else

        echo -e "${WARN} Could not download Teams activity report"

    fi

else

    echo -e "${WARN} Teams activity report URL not returned"
    echo "Check Reports.Read.All permission."

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 07 - EXPIRING GROUPS / TEAMS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " TEAM / GROUP EXPIRATION"
echo "============================================================"
echo

GROUPS_JSON=$(graph_get \
    "$GRAPH/groups?\$filter=groupTypes/any(c:c%20eq%20'Unified')&\$select=id,displayName,expirationDateTime,createdDateTime,visibility&\$top=999")

if [ $? -eq 0 ] && [ -n "$GROUPS_JSON" ]; then

    EXPIRING=$(echo "$GROUPS_JSON" |
        jq '[.value[] | select(.expirationDateTime != null)] | length')

    echo "Unified Groups With Expiration : $EXPIRING"

    echo

    echo "$GROUPS_JSON" |
        jq -r '
        .value[]
        | select(.expirationDateTime != null)
        | [
            .displayName,
            .visibility,
            .expirationDateTime
          ]
        | @tsv
        ' |
        while IFS=$'\t' read -r NAME VISIBILITY EXPIRY; do

            echo "  $NAME"
            echo "    Visibility : $VISIBILITY"
            echo "    Expiry     : $EXPIRY"

        done

else

    echo -e "${WARN} Unable to retrieve Unified Groups"

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 08 - FINAL ZENAIOPS POSTURE
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | TEAMS GOVERNANCE POSTURE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

if [ "$NO_OWNER" -gt 0 ]; then
    echo -e "${FAIL} Teams without owners      : $NO_OWNER"
else
    echo -e "${PASS} Teams without owners      : 0"
fi

if [ "$SINGLE_OWNER" -gt 0 ]; then
    echo -e "${WARN} Teams with single owner   : $SINGLE_OWNER"
else
    echo -e "${PASS} Teams with single owner   : 0"
fi

if [ "$HIGH_GUEST_TEAMS" -gt 0 ]; then
    echo -e "${WARN} High guest ratio Teams    : $HIGH_GUEST_TEAMS"
else
    echo -e "${PASS} High guest ratio Teams    : 0"
fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | TEAMS CHECK COMPLETE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

rm -f "$REPORT_FILE"
rm -f "/tmp/zenaiops-team-activity-$$.csv"

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

24 — Microsoft 365 Exchange Online Governance

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - 24 MICROSOFT 365 EXCHANGE ONLINE GOVERNANCE
# ==============================================================
#
# Checks:
#   01. Total mailboxes
#   02. User mailboxes
#   03. Shared mailboxes
#   04. Room / equipment mailboxes
#   05. Mailbox sizes
#   06. Large mailboxes
#   07. Archive enabled
#   08. Litigation Hold
#   09. Inactive mailboxes
#   10. Mailbox forwarding
#   11. Full Access delegates
#   12. Send As delegates
#   13. Send On Behalf
#   14. SMTP AUTH
#   15. POP / IMAP
#   16. Mailbox auditing
#   17. Disabled users with mailbox
#   18. Shared mailbox governance
#
# Requirements:
#   - bash
#   - pwsh
#   - ExchangeOnlineManagement module
#
# Run:
#   chmod +x 24-m365-exchange-governance.sh
#   ./24-m365-exchange-governance.sh
#
# ==============================================================

set -o pipefail

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CONFIGURATION
# --------------------------------------------------------------

LARGE_MAILBOX_GB=45

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# COLORS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
RED='\033[0;31m'
CYAN='\033[0;36m'
NC='\033[0m'

PASS="${GREEN}PASS${NC}"
WARN="${YELLOW}WARN${NC}"
FAIL="${RED}FAIL${NC}"

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# HEADER
# --------------------------------------------------------------

clear

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | EXCHANGE ONLINE GOVERNANCE"
echo "============================================================"
echo
echo " Large Mailbox Threshold : ${LARGE_MAILBOX_GB} GB"
echo
echo "============================================================"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CHECK PWSH
# --------------------------------------------------------------

if ! command -v pwsh >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} PowerShell 7 (pwsh) is not installed."
    echo
    echo "Install PowerShell and ExchangeOnlineManagement."
    echo

    exit 1
fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CHECK EXCHANGE MODULE
# --------------------------------------------------------------

MODULE_CHECK=$(pwsh -NoProfile -Command \
    "Get-Module -ListAvailable ExchangeOnlineManagement | Select-Object -First 1 -ExpandProperty Name" \
    2>/dev/null)

if [ "$MODULE_CHECK" != "ExchangeOnlineManagement" ]; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} ExchangeOnlineManagement module not found."
    echo
    echo "Install with:"
    echo
    echo "  Install-Module ExchangeOnlineManagement -Scope CurrentUser"
    echo

    exit 1
fi

echo -e "${PASS} ExchangeOnlineManagement module found"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# RUN EXCHANGE CHECKS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

pwsh -NoProfile -Command - <<'PS'

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

# ==============================================================
# CONNECT
# ==============================================================

Import-Module ExchangeOnlineManagement

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "Connecting to Exchange Online..." -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ""

try {

    Connect-ExchangeOnline `
        -ShowBanner:$false `
        -ErrorAction Stop

}
catch {

    Write-Host "FAIL - Exchange Online connection failed" -ForegroundColor Red
    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    exit 1
}

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "PASS - Connected to Exchange Online" -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host ""

# ==============================================================
# CONFIGURATION
# ==============================================================

$LargeMailboxGB = 45

# ==============================================================
# GET MAILBOX INVENTORY
# ==============================================================

Write-Host "Collecting mailbox inventory..." -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ""

$mailboxes = Get-EXOMailbox `
    -ResultSize Unlimited `
    -Properties `
        DisplayName,
       PrimarySmtpAddress,
       UserPrincipalName,
       RecipientTypeDetails,
       AccountDisabled,
       ArchiveStatus,
       LitigationHoldEnabled,
       LitigationHoldDate,
       LitigationHoldDuration,
       ForwardingSmtpAddress,
       ForwardingAddress,
       DeliverToMailboxAndForward,
       AuditEnabled,
       WhenCreated,
       ExternalDirectoryObjectId

$total = $mailboxes.Count

$userMailboxes = @(
    $mailboxes |
    Where-Object {
        $_.RecipientTypeDetails -eq "UserMailbox"
    }
)

$sharedMailboxes = @(
    $mailboxes |
    Where-Object {
        $_.RecipientTypeDetails -eq "SharedMailbox"
    }
)

$roomMailboxes = @(
    $mailboxes |
    Where-Object {
        $_.RecipientTypeDetails -eq "RoomMailbox"
    }
)

$equipmentMailboxes = @(
    $mailboxes |
    Where-Object {
        $_.RecipientTypeDetails -eq "EquipmentMailbox"
    }
)

# ==============================================================
# SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " MAILBOX INVENTORY"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

Write-Host ("Total Mailboxes       : {0}" -f $total)
Write-Host ("User Mailboxes        : {0}" -f $userMailboxes.Count)
Write-Host ("Shared Mailboxes      : {0}" -f $sharedMailboxes.Count)
Write-Host ("Room Mailboxes        : {0}" -f $roomMailboxes.Count)
Write-Host ("Equipment Mailboxes   : {0}" -f $equipmentMailboxes.Count)

Write-Host ""

# ==============================================================
# MAILBOX STATISTICS
# ==============================================================

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " MAILBOX SIZE ANALYSIS"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

Write-Host "Collecting mailbox statistics..." -ForegroundColor Cyan

$statistics = @()

foreach ($mbx in $mailboxes) {

    try {

        $stat = Get-EXOMailboxStatistics `
            -Identity $mbx.PrimarySmtpAddress `
            -ErrorAction Stop

        $sizeBytes = 0

        if ($stat.TotalItemSize) {

            $sizeText = $stat.TotalItemSize.ToString()

            if ($sizeText -match '\(([\d,]+) bytes\)') {

                $sizeBytes = [int64](
                    $Matches[1] -replace ",",""
                )
            }
        }

        $sizeGB = 0

        if ($sizeBytes -gt 0) {

            $sizeGB = [math]::Round(
                $sizeBytes / 1GB,
                2
            )
        }

        $statistics += [PSCustomObject]@{

            DisplayName =
                $mbx.DisplayName

            PrimarySmtpAddress =
                $mbx.PrimarySmtpAddress

            RecipientType =
                $mbx.RecipientTypeDetails

            SizeGB =
                $sizeGB

            ItemCount =
                $stat.ItemCount

        }

    }
    catch {

        Write-Host (
            "WARN - Statistics unavailable: {0}" `
            -f $mbx.PrimarySmtpAddress
        ) -ForegroundColor Yellow
    }
}

$largeMailboxes = @(
    $statistics |
    Where-Object {
        $_.SizeGB -ge $LargeMailboxGB
    }
)

$totalStorageGB = [math]::Round(
    ($statistics | Measure-Object SizeGB -Sum).Sum,
    2
)

Write-Host ("Total Mailbox Storage : {0} GB" -f $totalStorageGB)
Write-Host ("Large Mailboxes       : {0}" -f $largeMailboxes.Count)
Write-Host ""

if ($largeMailboxes.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------"
    Write-Host " LARGE MAILBOXES"
    Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------"
    Write-Host ""

    $largeMailboxes |
        Sort-Object SizeGB -Descending |
        Select-Object `
            DisplayName,
            PrimarySmtpAddress,
            SizeGB,
            ItemCount |
        Format-Table -AutoSize
}

# ==============================================================
# ARCHIVE
# ==============================================================

$archiveEnabled = @(
    $mailboxes |
    Where-Object {
        $_.ArchiveStatus -eq "Active"
    }
)

$archiveDisabledUser = @(
    $userMailboxes |
    Where-Object {
        $_.ArchiveStatus -ne "Active"
    }
)

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " ARCHIVE GOVERNANCE"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

Write-Host ("Archive Enabled       : {0}" -f $archiveEnabled.Count)
Write-Host ("User Mailboxes Without Archive : {0}" -f $archiveDisabledUser.Count)

if ($archiveDisabledUser.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "User mailboxes without archive:" `
        -ForegroundColor Yellow

    $archiveDisabledUser |
        Select-Object DisplayName,PrimarySmtpAddress |
        Format-Table -AutoSize
}

# ==============================================================
# LITIGATION HOLD
# ==============================================================

$litigationHold = @(
    $mailboxes |
    Where-Object {
        $_.LitigationHoldEnabled -eq $true
    }
)

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " RETENTION / LITIGATION HOLD"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

Write-Host ("Mailboxes on Litigation Hold : {0}" -f $litigationHold.Count)

if ($litigationHold.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Mailboxes with Litigation Hold:" `
        -ForegroundColor Cyan

    $litigationHold |
        Select-Object `
            DisplayName,
            PrimarySmtpAddress,
            LitigationHoldDate,
            LitigationHoldDuration |
        Format-Table -AutoSize
}

# ==============================================================
# INACTIVE MAILBOXES
# ==============================================================

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " INACTIVE MAILBOXES"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

try {

    $inactiveMailboxes = @(
        Get-EXOMailbox `
            -InactiveMailboxOnly `
            -ResultSize Unlimited
    )

    Write-Host (
        "Inactive Mailboxes : {0}" `
        -f $inactiveMailboxes.Count
    )

    if ($inactiveMailboxes.Count -gt 0) {

        $inactiveMailboxes |
            Select-Object `
                DisplayName,
                PrimarySmtpAddress,
                ExchangeGuid |
            Format-Table -AutoSize
    }

}
catch {

    Write-Host "WARN - Unable to retrieve inactive mailboxes" `
        -ForegroundColor Yellow
}

# ==============================================================
# FORWARDING
# ==============================================================

$forwarding = @(
    $mailboxes |
    Where-Object {
        $_.ForwardingSmtpAddress -or
        $_.ForwardingAddress
    }
)

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " MAILBOX FORWARDING"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

Write-Host ("Mailboxes With Forwarding : {0}" -f $forwarding.Count)

if ($forwarding.Count -gt 0) {

    $forwarding |
        Select-Object `
            DisplayName,
            PrimarySmtpAddress,
            ForwardingSmtpAddress,
            ForwardingAddress,
            DeliverToMailboxAndForward |
        Format-Table -AutoSize
}

# ==============================================================
# FULL ACCESS DELEGATES
# ==============================================================

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " FULL ACCESS DELEGATES"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$fullAccess = @()

foreach ($mbx in $mailboxes) {

    try {

        $permissions = Get-EXOMailboxPermission `
            -Identity $mbx.PrimarySmtpAddress `
            -ResultSize Unlimited `
            -ErrorAction Stop

        foreach ($perm in $permissions) {

            if (
                $perm.AccessRights -contains "FullAccess" -and
                $perm.User -notmatch "NT AUTHORITY|SELF|S-1-5"
            ) {

                $fullAccess += [PSCustomObject]@{

                    Mailbox =
                        $mbx.PrimarySmtpAddress

                    Delegate =
                        $perm.User

                    Access =
                        "FullAccess"

                    IsInherited =
                        $perm.IsInherited
                }
            }
        }

    }
    catch {
    }
}

Write-Host (
    "Full Access Delegations : {0}" `
    -f $fullAccess.Count
)

if ($fullAccess.Count -gt 0) {

    $fullAccess |
        Format-Table -AutoSize
}

# ==============================================================
# SEND AS
# ==============================================================

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " SEND AS DELEGATES"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$sendAs = @()

foreach ($mbx in $mailboxes) {

    try {

        $permissions = Get-EXORecipientPermission `
            -Identity $mbx.PrimarySmtpAddress `
            -ResultSize Unlimited `
            -ErrorAction Stop

        foreach ($perm in $permissions) {

            if (
                $perm.AccessRights -contains "SendAs" -and
                $perm.Trustee -notmatch "NT AUTHORITY|SELF"
            ) {

                $sendAs += [PSCustomObject]@{

                    Mailbox =
                        $mbx.PrimarySmtpAddress

                    Delegate =
                        $perm.Trustee

                    Access =
                        "SendAs"
                }
            }
        }

    }
    catch {
    }
}

Write-Host (
    "Send As Delegations : {0}" `
    -f $sendAs.Count
)

if ($sendAs.Count -gt 0) {

    $sendAs |
        Format-Table -AutoSize
}

# ==============================================================
# SEND ON BEHALF
# ==============================================================

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " SEND ON BEHALF"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$sendOnBehalf = @(
    $mailboxes |
    Where-Object {
        $_.GrantSendOnBehalfTo
    }
)

Write-Host (
    "Mailboxes With Send-On-Behalf : {0}" `
    -f $sendOnBehalf.Count
)

# ==============================================================
# CLIENT ACCESS / SMTP AUTH
# ==============================================================

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " CLIENT ACCESS SECURITY"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

Write-Host "Collecting CAS settings..." -ForegroundColor Cyan

$cas = @()

foreach ($mbx in $userMailboxes) {

    try {

        $settings = Get-EXOCasMailbox `
            -Identity $mbx.PrimarySmtpAddress `
            -ErrorAction Stop

        $cas += [PSCustomObject]@{

            DisplayName =
                $mbx.DisplayName

            Email =
                $mbx.PrimarySmtpAddress

            MAPI =
                $settings.MAPIEnabled

            OWA =
                $settings.OWAEnabled

            ActiveSync =
                $settings.ActiveSyncEnabled

            POP =
                $settings.POPEnabled

            IMAP =
                $settings.IMAPEnabled

            EWS =
                $settings.EwsEnabled
        }

    }
    catch {
    }
}

$popEnabled = @(
    $cas | Where-Object { $_.POP -eq $true }
)

$imapEnabled = @(
    $cas | Where-Object { $_.IMAP -eq $true }
)

Write-Host ("POP Enabled     : {0}" -f $popEnabled.Count)
Write-Host ("IMAP Enabled    : {0}" -f $imapEnabled.Count)

if ($popEnabled.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "POP enabled mailboxes:" -ForegroundColor Yellow

    $popEnabled |
        Select-Object DisplayName,Email |
        Format-Table -AutoSize
}

if ($imapEnabled.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "IMAP enabled mailboxes:" -ForegroundColor Yellow

    $imapEnabled |
        Select-Object DisplayName,Email |
        Format-Table -AutoSize
}

# ==============================================================
# SMTP AUTH
# ==============================================================

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " SMTP AUTH"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

try {

    $orgConfig = Get-TransportConfig

    Write-Host (
        "Organization SMTP AUTH Disabled : {0}" `
        -f $orgConfig.SmtpClientAuthenticationDisabled
    )

}
catch {

    Write-Host "WARN - Unable to read SMTP AUTH organization setting" `
        -ForegroundColor Yellow
}

# ==============================================================
# MAILBOX AUDITING
# ==============================================================

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " MAILBOX AUDITING"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$auditEnabled = @(
    $mailboxes |
    Where-Object {
        $_.AuditEnabled -eq $true
    }
)

$auditDisabled = @(
    $mailboxes |
    Where-Object {
        $_.AuditEnabled -ne $true
    }
)

Write-Host ("Audit Enabled  : {0}" -f $auditEnabled.Count)
Write-Host ("Audit Disabled : {0}" -f $auditDisabled.Count)

if ($auditDisabled.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "Mailboxes with auditing disabled:" `
        -ForegroundColor Yellow

    $auditDisabled |
        Select-Object DisplayName,PrimarySmtpAddress |
        Format-Table -AutoSize
}

# ==============================================================
# ACCOUNT DISABLED + MAILBOX
# ==============================================================

$disabledMailboxes = @(
    $userMailboxes |
    Where-Object {
        $_.AccountDisabled -eq $true
    }
)

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " DISABLED USERS WITH MAILBOXES"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

Write-Host (
    "Disabled User Mailboxes : {0}" `
    -f $disabledMailboxes.Count
)

if ($disabledMailboxes.Count -gt 0) {

    $disabledMailboxes |
        Select-Object `
            DisplayName,
            PrimarySmtpAddress,
            AccountDisabled,
            ArchiveStatus |
        Format-Table -AutoSize
}

# ==============================================================
# SHARED MAILBOX GOVERNANCE
# ==============================================================

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " SHARED MAILBOX GOVERNANCE"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

Write-Host (
    "Shared Mailboxes : {0}" `
    -f $sharedMailboxes.Count
)

$sharedWithLicense = @(
    $sharedMailboxes |
    Where-Object {
        $_.IsDirSynced -eq $true
    }
)

Write-Host (
    "Directory Synced Shared Mailboxes : {0}" `
    -f $sharedWithLicense.Count
)

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS FINAL POSTURE
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " ZENAIOPS | EXCHANGE GOVERNANCE POSTURE"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

if ($disabledMailboxes.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host (
        "WARN - Disabled users with mailboxes : {0}" `
        -f $disabledMailboxes.Count
    ) -ForegroundColor Yellow

}
else {

    Write-Host "PASS - No disabled user mailboxes" `
        -ForegroundColor Green
}

if ($forwarding.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host (
        "WARN - Mailboxes with forwarding : {0}" `
        -f $forwarding.Count
    ) -ForegroundColor Yellow

}
else {

    Write-Host "PASS - No mailbox forwarding detected" `
        -ForegroundColor Green
}

if ($auditDisabled.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host (
        "WARN - Mailboxes without audit enabled : {0}" `
        -f $auditDisabled.Count
    ) -ForegroundColor Yellow

}
else {

    Write-Host "PASS - Mailbox auditing enabled" `
        -ForegroundColor Green
}

if ($largeMailboxes.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host (
        "WARN - Large mailboxes >= ${LargeMailboxGB}GB : {0}" `
        -f $largeMailboxes.Count
    ) -ForegroundColor Yellow

}
else {

    Write-Host "PASS - No large mailboxes detected" `
        -ForegroundColor Green
}

if ($popEnabled.Count -gt 0 -or $imapEnabled.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host (
        "WARN - Legacy POP/IMAP enabled"
    ) -ForegroundColor Yellow

}
else {

    Write-Host "PASS - POP/IMAP not enabled" `
        -ForegroundColor Green
}

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " ZENAIOPS | EXCHANGE CHECK COMPLETE"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

# ==============================================================
# DISCONNECT
# ==============================================================

Disconnect-ExchangeOnline `
    -Confirm:$false `
    -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

PS

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | EXCHANGE GOVERNANCE COMPLETE"
echo "============================================================"
echo
