25 — Microsoft 365 SharePoint & OneDrive Data Governance

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - 25 MICROSOFT 365 SHAREPOINT & ONEDRIVE GOVERNANCE
# ==============================================================
#
# Checks:
#   01. SharePoint site usage
#   02. Site storage
#   03. Large sites
#   04. Inactive sites
#   05. Site owners
#   06. File counts
#   07. Active files
#   08. Storage utilization
#   09. SharePoint tenant sharing configuration
#   10. OneDrive tenant sharing configuration
#   11. Default sharing link
#   12. Anyone sharing
#   13. External sharing
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#   - curl
#   - pwsh
#   - ExchangeOnlineManagement is NOT required
#   - Microsoft.Online.SharePoint.PowerShell
#
# Graph permissions:
#   Reports.Read.All
#   Sites.Read.All
#
# SharePoint PowerShell:
#   SharePoint Administrator permissions
#
# ==============================================================

set -o pipefail

GRAPH="https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0"

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CONFIGURATION
# --------------------------------------------------------------

REPORT_PERIOD="D30"

INACTIVE_DAYS=90

LARGE_SITE_GB=100

HIGH_UTILIZATION_PERCENT=80

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# COLORS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
RED='\033[0;31m'
CYAN='\033[0;36m'
NC='\033[0m'

PASS="${GREEN}PASS${NC}"
WARN="${YELLOW}WARN${NC}"
FAIL="${RED}FAIL${NC}"

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# HEADER
# --------------------------------------------------------------

clear

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | SHAREPOINT & ONEDRIVE GOVERNANCE"
echo "============================================================"
echo
echo " Report Period       : $REPORT_PERIOD"
echo " Inactive Threshold  : $INACTIVE_DAYS days"
echo " Large Site          : ${LARGE_SITE_GB} GB"
echo " High Utilization    : ${HIGH_UTILIZATION_PERCENT}%"
echo
echo "============================================================"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CHECK AZ LOGIN
# --------------------------------------------------------------

if ! az account show >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} Azure CLI login required"
    echo
    echo "Run:"
    echo "  az login"
    echo

    exit 1
fi

echo -e "${PASS} Azure CLI authenticated"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CHECK JQ
# --------------------------------------------------------------

if ! command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} jq is required"
    exit 1

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# GRAPH REPORT FUNCTION
# --------------------------------------------------------------

get_report_location() {

    local URL="$1"

    az rest \
        --method get \
        --url "$URL" \
        --headers "Accept=text/csv" \
        --query 'headers' \
        -o json 2>/dev/null |
        jq -r '.location // empty'
}

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 01 - SHAREPOINT SITE USAGE REPORT
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " SHAREPOINT SITE USAGE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Requesting SharePoint usage report..."

USAGE_URL=$(get_report_location \
    "$GRAPH/reports/getSharePointSiteUsageDetail(period='$REPORT_PERIOD')")

if [ -z "$USAGE_URL" ]; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} Could not obtain SharePoint usage report URL"
    echo
    echo "Verify Reports.Read.All permission."
    echo

    exit 1
fi

USAGE_FILE="/tmp/zenaiops-sharepoint-usage-$$.csv"

curl \
    --silent \
    --show-error \
    --location \
    "$USAGE_URL" \
    -o "$USAGE_FILE"

if [ $? -ne 0 ] || [ ! -s "$USAGE_FILE" ]; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} Failed to download SharePoint usage report"
    exit 1

fi

echo -e "${PASS} SharePoint usage report downloaded"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 02 - ANALYZE REPORT
# --------------------------------------------------------------

python3 \
    "$USAGE_FILE" \
    "$INACTIVE_DAYS" \
    "$LARGE_SITE_GB" \
    "$HIGH_UTILIZATION_PERCENT" <<'PY'

PY

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# Python analysis
# --------------------------------------------------------------

python3 - "$USAGE_FILE" \
    "$INACTIVE_DAYS" \
    "$LARGE_SITE_GB" \
    "$HIGH_UTILIZATION_PERCENT" <<'PY'

import csv
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone

file = sys.argv[1]

inactive_days = int(sys.argv[2])

large_site_gb = float(sys.argv[3])

high_util = float(sys.argv[4])

sites = []

with open(
    file,
    encoding="utf-8-sig",
    newline=""
) as f:

    reader = csv.DictReader(f)

    for row in reader:

        site_url = row.get("Site URL", "").strip()

        if not site_url:
            continue

        owner = row.get(
            "Owner Display Name",
            ""
        ).strip()

        owner_upn = row.get(
            "Owner Principal Name",
            ""
        ).strip()

        last_activity = row.get(
            "Last Activity Date",
            ""
        ).strip()

        try:

            storage_used = int(
                row.get(
                    "Storage Used (Byte)",
                    "0"
                ) or 0
            )

        except:

            storage_used = 0

        try:

            storage_allocated = int(
                row.get(
                    "Storage Allocated (Byte)",
                    "0"
                ) or 0
            )

        except:

            storage_allocated = 0

        try:

            file_count = int(
                row.get(
                    "File Count",
                    "0"
                ) or 0
            )

        except:

            file_count = 0

        try:

            active_files = int(
                row.get(
                    "Active File Count",
                    "0"
                ) or 0
            )

        except:

            active_files = 0

        used_gb = storage_used / (1024 ** 3)

        allocated_gb = storage_allocated / (1024 ** 3)

        utilization = 0

        if storage_allocated > 0:

            utilization = (
                storage_used /
                storage_allocated
            ) * 100

        age_days = None

        if last_activity:

            try:

                dt = datetime.strptime(
                    last_activity,
                    "%Y-%m-%d"
                ).replace(
                    tzinfo=timezone.utc
                )

                age_days = (
                    datetime.now(
                        timezone.utc
                    ) - dt
                ).days

            except:

                pass

        sites.append({

            "url": site_url,

            "owner": owner,

            "owner_upn": owner_upn,

            "last_activity": last_activity,

            "age_days": age_days,

            "used_gb": used_gb,

            "allocated_gb": allocated_gb,

            "utilization": utilization,

            "files": file_count,

            "active_files": active_files

        })


# ==============================================================
# SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

total_sites = len(sites)

total_storage = sum(
    x["used_gb"]
    for x in sites
)

total_allocated = sum(
    x["allocated_gb"]
    for x in sites
)

large_sites = [
    x for x in sites
    if x["used_gb"] >= large_site_gb
]

inactive_sites = [
    x for x in sites
    if x["age_days"] is not None
    and x["age_days"] >= inactive_days
]

no_owner = [
    x for x in sites
    if not x["owner"]
    and not x["owner_upn"]
]

high_utilization = [
    x for x in sites
    if x["utilization"] >= high_util
]


print()
print("=" * 60)
print(" SHAREPOINT STORAGE SUMMARY")
print("=" * 60)
print()

print(
    f"Total Sites             : {total_sites}"
)

print(
    f"Storage Used            : {total_storage:.2f} GB"
)

print(
    f"Storage Allocated       : {total_allocated:.2f} GB"
)

print(
    f"Large Sites             : {len(large_sites)}"
)

print(
    f"Inactive Sites          : {len(inactive_sites)}"
)

print(
    f"Sites Without Owner     : {len(no_owner)}"
)

print(
    f"High Utilization Sites  : {len(high_utilization)}"
)


# ==============================================================
# LARGE SITES
# ==============================================================

print()
print("=" * 60)
print(" LARGE SHAREPOINT SITES")
print("=" * 60)
print()

for x in sorted(
    large_sites,
    key=lambda y: y["used_gb"],
    reverse=True
):

    print(
        f"{x['used_gb']:>10.2f} GB | "
        f"{x['utilization']:>6.1f}% | "
        f"{x['url']}"
    )


# ==============================================================
# INACTIVE
# ==============================================================

print()
print("=" * 60)
print(" INACTIVE SHAREPOINT SITES")
print("=" * 60)
print()

for x in sorted(
    inactive_sites,
    key=lambda y: y["age_days"] or 0,
    reverse=True
):

    print(
        f"{x['age_days']:>4} days | "
        f"{x['used_gb']:>8.2f} GB | "
        f"{x['url']}"
    )


# ==============================================================
# HIGH UTILIZATION
# ==============================================================

print()
print("=" * 60)
print(" HIGH STORAGE UTILIZATION")
print("=" * 60)
print()

for x in sorted(
    high_utilization,
    key=lambda y: y["utilization"],
    reverse=True
):

    print(
        f"{x['utilization']:>6.1f}% | "
        f"{x['used_gb']:>8.2f} GB / "
        f"{x['allocated_gb']:>8.2f} GB | "
        f"{x['url']}"
    )


# ==============================================================
# OWNERS
# ==============================================================

print()
print("=" * 60)
print(" SITES WITHOUT OWNER DATA")
print("=" * 60)
print()

for x in no_owner:

    print(
        f"{x['url']}"
    )


# ==============================================================
# TOP STORAGE CONSUMERS
# ==============================================================

print()
print("=" * 60)
print(" TOP 10 STORAGE CONSUMERS")
print("=" * 60)
print()

for x in sorted(
    sites,
    key=lambda y: y["used_gb"],
    reverse=True
)[:10]:

    owner = (
        x["owner"]
        or x["owner_upn"]
        or "Unknown"
    )

    print(
        f"{x['used_gb']:>10.2f} GB | "
        f"{owner:<30} | "
        f"{x['url']}"
    )

PY

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 03 - SHAREPOINT ADMIN SETTINGS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " SHAREPOINT TENANT SHARING"
echo "============================================================"
echo

if command -v pwsh >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    pwsh -NoProfile -Command - <<'PS'

$ErrorActionPreference = "SilentlyContinue"

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# SharePoint module
# --------------------------------------------------------------

$module = Get-Module `
    -ListAvailable `
    -Name Microsoft.Online.SharePoint.PowerShell |
    Select-Object -First 1

if (-not $module) {

    Write-Host `
        "WARN - Microsoft.Online.SharePoint.PowerShell not installed" `
        -ForegroundColor Yellow

    exit
}

Import-Module Microsoft.Online.SharePoint.PowerShell

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "Connecting to SharePoint Online..." `
    -ForegroundColor Cyan

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# Tenant URL
# --------------------------------------------------------------

$tenantUrl = Read-Host `
    "Enter SharePoint Admin URL (e.g. https://contoso-admin.sharepoint.com)"

if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($tenantUrl)) {

    Write-Host `
        "WARN - SharePoint Admin URL not supplied" `
        -ForegroundColor Yellow

    exit
}

try {

    Connect-SPOService `
        -Url $tenantUrl `
        -ErrorAction Stop

}
catch {

    Write-Host `
        "FAIL - SharePoint connection failed" `
        -ForegroundColor Red

    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message

    exit
}

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "PASS - Connected to SharePoint Online" `
    -ForegroundColor Green

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# TENANT SETTINGS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

$tenant = Get-SPOTenant

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------"
Write-Host " SHAREPOINT SHARING SETTINGS"
Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------"
Write-Host ""

Write-Host (
    "Default Sharing Link Type : {0}" `
    -f $tenant.DefaultSharingLinkType
)

Write-Host (
    "Default Link Permission   : {0}" `
    -f $tenant.DefaultLinkPermission
)

Write-Host (
    "Sharing Capability        : {0}" `
    -f $tenant.SharingCapability
)

Write-Host (
    "External User Expiration  : {0} days" `
    -f $tenant.ExternalUserExpirationRequiredInDays
)

Write-Host (
    "Anyone Link Expiration    : {0} days" `
    -f $tenant.OneDriveDefaultLinkPermission
)

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# ONEDRIVE SETTINGS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------"
Write-Host " ONEDRIVE SHARING SETTINGS"
Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------"
Write-Host ""

Write-Host (
    "OneDrive Sharing Capability : {0}" `
    -f $tenant.OneDriveSharingCapability
)

Write-Host (
    "OneDrive Default Link Type  : {0}" `
    -f $tenant.OneDriveDefaultSharingLinkType
)

Write-Host (
    "OneDrive Default Permission : {0}" `
    -f $tenant.OneDriveDefaultLinkPermission
)

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# SITE INVENTORY
# --------------------------------------------------------------

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------"
Write-Host " SHAREPOINT SITE INVENTORY"
Write-Host "------------------------------------------------------------"
Write-Host ""

try {

    $sites = Get-SPOSite `
        -Limit All `
        -IncludePersonalSite $false

    $siteCount = @($sites).Count

    Write-Host (
        "SharePoint Sites : {0}" `
        -f $siteCount
    )

    $externalSites = @(
        $sites |
        Where-Object {
            $_.SharingCapability -ne "Disabled"
        }
    )

    Write-Host (
        "Sites With External Sharing Capability : {0}" `
        -f $externalSites.Count
    )

    $anyoneSites = @(
        $sites |
        Where-Object {
            $_.SharingCapability -eq "ExternalUserAndGuestSharing"
        }
    )

    Write-Host (
        "Sites Allowing External/Guest Sharing : {0}" `
        -f $anyoneSites.Count
    )

}
catch {

    Write-Host `
        "WARN - Unable to enumerate SharePoint sites" `
        -ForegroundColor Yellow
}

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# DISCONNECT
# --------------------------------------------------------------

Disconnect-SPOService

Write-Host ""

PS

else

    echo -e "${WARN} pwsh not installed - tenant-level sharing checks skipped"

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 04 - STORAGE TREND
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " SHAREPOINT STORAGE TREND"
echo "============================================================"
echo

STORAGE_URL=$(get_report_location \
    "$GRAPH/reports/getSharePointSiteUsageStorage(period='$REPORT_PERIOD')")

if [ -n "$STORAGE_URL" ]; then

    STORAGE_FILE="/tmp/zenaiops-sharepoint-storage-$$.csv"

    curl \
        --silent \
        --show-error \
        --location \
        "$STORAGE_URL" \
        -o "$STORAGE_FILE"

    if [ -s "$STORAGE_FILE" ]; then

        python3 - "$STORAGE_FILE" <<'PY'

import csv
import sys

file = sys.argv[1]

rows = []

with open(
    file,
    encoding="utf-8-sig",
    newline=""
) as f:

    reader = csv.DictReader(f)

    for row in reader:

        try:

            used = int(
                row.get(
                    "Storage Used (Byte)",
                    "0"
                ) or 0
            )

        except:

            continue

        rows.append(
            (
                row.get(
                    "Report Date",
                    ""
                ),
                used / (1024 ** 3)
            )
        )

print()

for date, gb in rows:

    print(
        f"{date} | {gb:,.2f} GB"
    )

if len(rows) >= 2:

    first = rows[0][1]

    last = rows[-1][1]

    delta = last - first

    print()

    print(
        f"Storage Change : {delta:+,.2f} GB"
    )

PY

    else

        echo -e "${WARN} Storage trend report unavailable"

    fi

else

    echo -e "${WARN} Storage trend URL unavailable"

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 05 - ZENAIOPS POSTURE
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | SHAREPOINT & ONEDRIVE POSTURE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo -e "${PASS} Site usage analysis completed"

echo -e "${PASS} Storage consumption analyzed"

echo -e "${PASS} Inactive site analysis completed"

echo -e "${PASS} Large site analysis completed"

echo -e "${PASS} Storage utilization analyzed"

echo
echo "Review especially:"
echo "  1. Inactive sites"
echo "  2. High storage sites"
echo "  3. Sites without owner information"
echo "  4. External sharing"
echo "  5. Anyone links"
echo "  6. OneDrive external sharing"
echo "  7. Storage growth"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CLEANUP
# --------------------------------------------------------------

rm -f "$USAGE_FILE"
rm -f "/tmp/zenaiops-sharepoint-storage-$$.csv"

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | SHAREPOINT CHECK COMPLETE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

26 — Microsoft Intune & Device Governance

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - 26 MICROSOFT INTUNE DEVICE GOVERNANCE
# ==============================================================
#
# Checks:
#   01. Total managed devices
#   02. Windows / macOS / iOS / Android
#   03. Compliant devices
#   04. Non-compliant devices
#   05. Unknown / error compliance
#   06. Company vs personal devices
#   07. Stale devices
#   08. Jailbroken / rooted devices
#   09. Encryption status
#   10. OS versions
#   11. Management agents
#   12. Enrollment types
#   13. Devices without user
#   14. Exchange access state
#   15. Device compliance policies
#
# Requirements:
#   - Azure CLI
#   - jq
#
# Graph permissions:
#   DeviceManagementManagedDevices.Read.All
#   DeviceManagementConfiguration.Read.All
#
# ==============================================================

set -o pipefail

GRAPH="https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0"

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CONFIGURATION
# --------------------------------------------------------------

STALE_DAYS=90

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# COLORS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
RED='\033[0;31m'
CYAN='\033[0;36m'
NC='\033[0m'

PASS="${GREEN}PASS${NC}"
WARN="${YELLOW}WARN${NC}"
FAIL="${RED}FAIL${NC}"

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# HEADER
# --------------------------------------------------------------

clear

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | INTUNE DEVICE GOVERNANCE"
echo "============================================================"
echo
echo " Stale Device Threshold : $STALE_DAYS days"
echo
echo "============================================================"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CHECK AZ LOGIN
# --------------------------------------------------------------

if ! az account show >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} Azure CLI login required"
    echo
    echo "Run:"
    echo "  az login"
    echo

    exit 1
fi

echo -e "${PASS} Azure CLI authenticated"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# GRAPH GET FUNCTION
# --------------------------------------------------------------

graph_get() {

    local URL="$1"

    az rest \
        --method get \
        --url "$URL" \
        --headers "Accept=application/json"
}

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 01 - MANAGED DEVICES
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo "Getting Intune managed devices..."

DEVICES_JSON=$(graph_get \
    "$GRAPH/deviceManagement/managedDevices?\$top=999&\$select=id,deviceName,userPrincipalName,userId,operatingSystem,osVersion,complianceState,managedDeviceOwnerType,managementAgent,deviceEnrollmentType,azureADRegistered,azureADDeviceId,deviceRegistrationState,lastSyncDateTime,approximateLastSignInDateTime,jailBroken,exchangeAccessState,isEncrypted,model,manufacturer,emailAddress")

if [ $? -ne 0 ] || [ -z "$DEVICES_JSON" ]; then

    echo
    echo -e "${FAIL} Unable to retrieve Intune managed devices"
    echo
    echo "Verify:"
    echo "  DeviceManagementManagedDevices.Read.All"
    echo "  Intune licensing"
    echo

    exit 1
fi

DEVICE_COUNT=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '.value | length')

echo -e "${PASS} Devices retrieved: $DEVICE_COUNT"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 02 - BASIC INVENTORY
# --------------------------------------------------------------

WINDOWS=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select((.operatingSystem // "" | ascii_downcase) == "windows")] | length')

MACOS=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select((.operatingSystem // "" | ascii_downcase) == "macos")] | length')

IOS=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select((.operatingSystem // "" | ascii_downcase) == "ios")] | length')

ANDROID=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select((.operatingSystem // "" | ascii_downcase) == "android")] | length')

LINUX=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select((.operatingSystem // "" | ascii_downcase) == "linux")] | length')

OTHER_OS=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq "[.value[] | select(
        (.operatingSystem // \"\" | ascii_downcase) != \"windows\" and
        (.operatingSystem // \"\" | ascii_downcase) != \"macos\" and
        (.operatingSystem // \"\" | ascii_downcase) != \"ios\" and
        (.operatingSystem // \"\" | ascii_downcase) != \"android\" and
        (.operatingSystem // \"\" | ascii_downcase) != \"linux\"
    )] | length")

echo "============================================================"
echo " DEVICE INVENTORY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Total Devices : $DEVICE_COUNT"
echo "Windows       : $WINDOWS"
echo "macOS         : $MACOS"
echo "iOS           : $IOS"
echo "Android       : $ANDROID"
echo "Linux         : $LINUX"
echo "Other         : $OTHER_OS"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 03 - COMPLIANCE
# --------------------------------------------------------------

COMPLIANT=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.complianceState=="compliant")] | length')

NONCOMPLIANT=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.complianceState=="noncompliant")] | length')

UNKNOWN=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.complianceState=="unknown")] | length')

CONFLICT=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.complianceState=="conflict")] | length')

ERROR_STATE=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.complianceState=="error")] | length')

GRACE=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.complianceState=="inGracePeriod")] | length')

echo "============================================================"
echo " DEVICE COMPLIANCE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Compliant        : $COMPLIANT"
echo "Non-Compliant    : $NONCOMPLIANT"
echo "Unknown          : $UNKNOWN"
echo "Conflict         : $CONFLICT"
echo "Error            : $ERROR_STATE"
echo "Grace Period     : $GRACE"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 04 - COMPLIANCE PERCENTAGE
# --------------------------------------------------------------

if [ "$DEVICE_COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

    COMPLIANCE_PERCENT=$(awk \
        "BEGIN {printf \"%.1f\", ($COMPLIANT/$DEVICE_COUNT)*100")

else

    COMPLIANCE_PERCENT="0.0"

fi

echo "Compliance Rate : ${COMPLIANCE_PERCENT}%"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 05 - DEVICE OWNERSHIP
# --------------------------------------------------------------

COMPANY=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.managedDeviceOwnerType=="company")] | length')

PERSONAL=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.managedDeviceOwnerType=="personal")] | length')

UNKNOWN_OWNER=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.managedDeviceOwnerType==null or .managedDeviceOwnerType=="unknown")] | length')

echo "============================================================"
echo " DEVICE OWNERSHIP"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Company Devices  : $COMPANY"
echo "Personal Devices : $PERSONAL"
echo "Unknown          : $UNKNOWN_OWNER"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 06 - ENCRYPTION
# --------------------------------------------------------------

ENCRYPTED=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.isEncrypted==true)] | length')

NOT_ENCRYPTED=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.isEncrypted==false)] | length')

ENCRYPTION_UNKNOWN=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.isEncrypted==null)] | length')

echo "============================================================"
echo " DEVICE ENCRYPTION"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Encrypted        : $ENCRYPTED"
echo "Not Encrypted    : $NOT_ENCRYPTED"
echo "Unknown          : $ENCRYPTION_UNKNOWN"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 07 - JAILBREAK / ROOT
# --------------------------------------------------------------

JAILBROKEN=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(
        .jailBroken != null and
        .jailBroken != "" and
        (.jailBroken | ascii_downcase) != "false" and
        (.jailBroken | ascii_downcase) != "none"
    )] | length')

echo "============================================================"
echo " JAILBREAK / ROOT DETECTION"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Potential Jailbroken / Rooted Devices : $JAILBROKEN"
echo

if [ "$JAILBROKEN" -gt 0 ]; then

    echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
        jq -r '
        .value[]
        | select(
            .jailBroken != null and
            .jailBroken != "" and
            (.jailBroken | ascii_downcase) != "false" and
            (.jailBroken | ascii_downcase) != "none"
        )
        | [
            .deviceName,
            .userPrincipalName,
            .operatingSystem,
            .jailBroken
          ]
        | @tsv
        ' |
        column -t -s $'\t'

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 08 - DEVICES WITHOUT USER
# --------------------------------------------------------------

NO_USER=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(
        (.userPrincipalName == null or .userPrincipalName == "") and
        (.emailAddress == null or .emailAddress == "")
    )] | length')

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " USER ASSOCIATION"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Devices Without User : $NO_USER"
echo

if [ "$NO_USER" -gt 0 ]; then

    echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
        jq -r '
        .value[]
        | select(
            (.userPrincipalName == null or .userPrincipalName == "") and
            (.emailAddress == null or .emailAddress == "")
        )
        | [
            .deviceName,
            .operatingSystem,
            .deviceEnrollmentType,
            .managementAgent
          ]
        | @tsv
        ' |
        column -t -s $'\t'

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 09 - STALE DEVICES
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " STALE DEVICES"
echo "============================================================"
echo

python3 - "$STALE_DAYS" <<'PY'

import json
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone

days = int(sys.argv[1])

data = json.loads(
    '''__DEVICE_DATA__'''
)

PY

# Use jq to create stale-device JSON
STALE_JSON=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq --argjson days "$STALE_DAYS" '
    [
      .value[]
      | select(.lastSyncDateTime != null)
      | select(
          (
            (
              (now - (
                .lastSyncDateTime
                | fromdateiso8601
              )) / 86400
            ) >= $days
          )
        )
    ]')

STALE_COUNT=$(echo "$STALE_JSON" |
    jq 'length')

echo "Stale Devices : $STALE_COUNT"
echo

if [ "$STALE_COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

    echo "$STALE_JSON" |
        jq -r '
        .[]
        | [
            .deviceName,
            .userPrincipalName,
            .operatingSystem,
            .lastSyncDateTime,
            .complianceState
          ]
        | @tsv
        ' |
        column -t -s $'\t'

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 10 - OS VERSION INVENTORY
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " OS VERSION INVENTORY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq -r '
    .value[]
    | [
        (.operatingSystem // "Unknown"),
        (.osVersion // "Unknown")
      ]
    | @tsv
    ' |
    sort |
    uniq -c |
    sort -nr |
    head -30

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 11 - MANAGEMENT AGENT
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " MANAGEMENT AGENT"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq -r '
    .value[]
    | (.managementAgent // "unknown")
    ' |
    sort |
    uniq -c |
    sort -nr

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 12 - ENROLLMENT TYPE
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ENROLLMENT TYPE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq -r '
    .value[]
    | (.deviceEnrollmentType // "unknown")
    ' |
    sort |
    uniq -c |
    sort -nr

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 13 - EXCHANGE ACCESS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

EXCHANGE_BLOCKED=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(
        .exchangeAccessState=="blocked"
    )] | length')

EXCHANGE_ALLOWED=$(echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(
        .exchangeAccessState=="allowed"
    )] | length')

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " EXCHANGE DEVICE ACCESS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Allowed  : $EXCHANGE_ALLOWED"
echo "Blocked  : $EXCHANGE_BLOCKED"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 14 - NON-COMPLIANT DEVICE LIST
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " NON-COMPLIANT DEVICES"
echo "============================================================"
echo

if [ "$NONCOMPLIANT" -gt 0 ]; then

    echo "$DEVICES_JSON" |
        jq -r '
        .value[]
        | select(.complianceState=="noncompliant")
        | [
            .deviceName,
            .userPrincipalName,
            .operatingSystem,
            .osVersion,
            .isEncrypted,
            .lastSyncDateTime
          ]
        | @tsv
        ' |
        column -t -s $'\t'

else

    echo "No non-compliant devices detected."

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 15 - COMPLIANCE POLICIES
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " INTUNE COMPLIANCE POLICIES"
echo "============================================================"
echo

POLICIES_JSON=$(graph_get \
    "$GRAPH/deviceManagement/deviceCompliancePolicies?\$top=999&\$select=id,displayName,description,createdDateTime,lastModifiedDateTime,version")

if [ $? -eq 0 ] && [ -n "$POLICIES_JSON" ]; then

    POLICY_COUNT=$(echo "$POLICIES_JSON" |
        jq '.value | length')

    echo "Compliance Policies : $POLICY_COUNT"
    echo

    echo "$POLICIES_JSON" |
        jq -r '
        .value[]
        | [
            .displayName,
            .version,
            .lastModifiedDateTime
          ]
        | @tsv
        ' |
        column -t -s $'\t'

else

    echo -e "${WARN} Unable to retrieve compliance policies"
    echo "Verify DeviceManagementConfiguration.Read.All"

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 16 - DEVICE GOVERNANCE SUMMARY
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | DEVICE GOVERNANCE POSTURE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

if [ "$NONCOMPLIANT" -gt 0 ]; then

    echo -e "${WARN} Non-compliant devices       : $NONCOMPLIANT"

else

    echo -e "${PASS} Non-compliant devices       : 0"

fi

if [ "$STALE_COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

    echo -e "${WARN} Stale devices               : $STALE_COUNT"

else

    echo -e "${PASS} Stale devices               : 0"

fi

if [ "$NOT_ENCRYPTED" -gt 0 ]; then

    echo -e "${WARN} Unencrypted devices         : $NOT_ENCRYPTED"

else

    echo -e "${PASS} Unencrypted devices         : 0"

fi

if [ "$JAILBROKEN" -gt 0 ]; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} Jailbroken/rooted devices   : $JAILBROKEN"

else

    echo -e "${PASS} Jailbroken/rooted devices   : 0"

fi

if [ "$NO_USER" -gt 0 ]; then

    echo -e "${WARN} Devices without user        : $NO_USER"

else

    echo -e "${PASS} Devices without user        : 0"

fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | INTUNE CHECK COMPLETE"
echo "============================================================"
echo


----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
27 — Microsoft 365 Apps & Application Usage

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - 27 MICROSOFT 365 APPLICATION USAGE
# ==============================================================
#
# Checks:
#   01. Microsoft 365 app usage
#   02. Outlook usage
#   03. Word usage
#   04. Excel usage
#   05. PowerPoint usage
#   06. OneNote usage
#   07. Teams usage
#   08. Desktop vs Web vs Mobile
#   09. Inactive M365 users
#   10. Licensed but no recent app activity
#   11. Office activation
#   12. Last activation
#   13. Application adoption
#
# Graph permission:
#   Reports.Read.All
#
# ==============================================================

set -o pipefail

GRAPH="https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0"

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CONFIGURATION
# --------------------------------------------------------------

REPORT_PERIOD="D30"

INACTIVE_DAYS=30

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# COLORS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
RED='\033[0;31m'
CYAN='\033[0;36m'
NC='\033[0m'

PASS="${GREEN}PASS${NC}"
WARN="${YELLOW}WARN${NC}"
FAIL="${RED}FAIL${NC}"

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# HEADER
# --------------------------------------------------------------

clear

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | MICROSOFT 365 APPLICATION USAGE"
echo "============================================================"
echo
echo " Usage Period       : $REPORT_PERIOD"
echo " Inactive Threshold : $INACTIVE_DAYS days"
echo
echo "============================================================"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# AZ LOGIN
# --------------------------------------------------------------

if ! az account show >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} Azure CLI login required"
    echo
    echo "Run:"
    echo "  az login"
    echo

    exit 1
fi

echo -e "${PASS} Azure CLI authenticated"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# REPORT LOCATION
# --------------------------------------------------------------

get_report_location() {

    local URL="$1"

    az rest \
        --method get \
        --url "$URL" \
        --headers "Accept=text/csv" \
        --query 'headers' \
        -o json 2>/dev/null |
        jq -r '.location // empty'
}

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 01 - M365 APP USER REPORT
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " MICROSOFT 365 APPLICATION USAGE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Requesting Microsoft 365 Apps usage report..."

APP_URL=$(get_report_location \
    "$GRAPH/reports/getM365AppUserDetail(period='$REPORT_PERIOD')")

if [ -z "$APP_URL" ]; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} Unable to obtain M365 Apps report"
    echo
    echo "Verify Reports.Read.All."
    exit 1
fi

APP_FILE="/tmp/zenaiops-m365-apps-$$.csv"

curl \
    --silent \
    --show-error \
    --location \
    "$APP_URL" \
    -o "$APP_FILE"

if [ $? -ne 0 ] || [ ! -s "$APP_FILE" ]; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} Unable to download M365 Apps report"
    exit 1
fi

echo -e "${PASS} M365 Apps report downloaded"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 02 - ANALYZE APPLICATION USAGE
# --------------------------------------------------------------

python3 - "$APP_FILE" "$INACTIVE_DAYS" <<'PY'

import csv
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone

file = sys.argv[1]
inactive_days = int(sys.argv[2])

rows = []

with open(
    file,
    encoding="utf-8-sig",
    newline=""
) as f:

    reader = csv.DictReader(f)

    for row in reader:

        rows.append(row)


# ==============================================================
# HELPERS
# ==============================================================

def is_used(row, column):

    value = str(
        row.get(column, "")
    ).strip().lower()

    return value in (
        "1",
        "true",
        "yes"
    )


def parse_date(value):

    if not value:
        return None

    value = value.strip()

    formats = [
        "%Y-%m-%d",
        "%m/%d/%Y",
        "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S"
    ]

    for fmt in formats:

        try:

            return datetime.strptime(
                value,
                fmt
            ).replace(
                tzinfo=timezone.utc
            )

        except:

            pass

    return None


# ==============================================================
# COUNTERS
# ==============================================================

total = len(rows)

outlook = 0
word = 0
excel = 0
powerpoint = 0
onenote = 0
teams = 0

windows = 0
mac = 0
mobile = 0
web = 0

inactive = []

# ==============================================================
# PROCESS USERS
# ==============================================================

for row in rows:

    if is_used(row, "Outlook"):
        outlook += 1

    if is_used(row, "Word"):
        word += 1

    if is_used(row, "Excel"):
        excel += 1

    if is_used(row, "PowerPoint"):
        powerpoint += 1

    if is_used(row, "OneNote"):
        onenote += 1

    if is_used(row, "Teams"):
        teams += 1

    if is_used(row, "Windows"):
        windows += 1

    if is_used(row, "Mac"):
        mac += 1

    if is_used(row, "Mobile"):
        mobile += 1

    if is_used(row, "Web"):
        web += 1

    last_activity = parse_date(
        row.get(
            "Last Activity Date",
            ""
        )
    )

    if last_activity:

        age = (
            datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            - last_activity
        ).days

        if age >= inactive_days:

            inactive.append(
                (
                    age,
                    row.get(
                        "Display Name",
                        ""
                    ),
                    row.get(
                        "User Principal Name",
                        ""
                    ),
                    row.get(
                        "Last Activity Date",
                        ""
                    )
                )
            )

    else:

        inactive.append(
            (
                9999,
                row.get(
                    "Display Name",
                    ""
                ),
                row.get(
                    "User Principal Name",
                    ""
                ),
                "Never / No Activity"
            )
        )


# ==============================================================
# SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

print()
print("=" * 60)
print(" APPLICATION ADOPTION")
print("=" * 60)
print()

print(
    f"Users in Report       : {total}"
)

print()

print(
    f"Outlook               : {outlook}"
)

print(
    f"Word                  : {word}"
)

print(
    f"Excel                 : {excel}"
)

print(
    f"PowerPoint            : {powerpoint}"
)

print(
    f"OneNote               : {onenote}"
)

print(
    f"Teams                 : {teams}"
)

print()

print("=" * 60)
print(" PLATFORM USAGE")
print("=" * 60)
print()

print(
    f"Windows               : {windows}"
)

print(
    f"Mac                   : {mac}"
)

print(
    f"Mobile                : {mobile}"
)

print(
    f"Web                   : {web}"
)


# ==============================================================
# ADOPTION PERCENTAGES
# ==============================================================

if total > 0:

    print()
    print("=" * 60)
    print(" ADOPTION %")
    print("=" * 60)
    print()

    apps = [
        ("Outlook", outlook),
        ("Word", word),
        ("Excel", excel),
        ("PowerPoint", powerpoint),
        ("OneNote", onenote),
        ("Teams", teams)
    ]

    for name, count in apps:

        percentage = (
            count / total
        ) * 100

        print(
            f"{name:<15} : "
            f"{percentage:>6.1f}%"
        )


# ==============================================================
# INACTIVE USERS
# ==============================================================

print()
print("=" * 60)
print(" INACTIVE M365 APP USERS")
print("=" * 60)
print()

inactive.sort(
    reverse=True
)

print(
    f"Users with no activity >= "
    f"{inactive_days} days : "
    f"{len(inactive)}"
)

print()

for age, name, upn, last in inactive[:50]:

    if age == 9999:

        age_text = "NO ACTIVITY"

    else:

        age_text = f"{age} days"

    print(
        f"{age_text:<15} | "
        f"{name:<30} | "
        f"{upn:<40} | "
        f"{last}"
    )

PY

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 03 - APP COUNTS REPORT
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " M365 APP USER COUNTS TREND"
echo "============================================================"
echo

COUNT_URL=$(get_report_location \
    "$GRAPH/reports/getM365AppUserCounts(period='$REPORT_PERIOD')")

if [ -n "$COUNT_URL" ]; then

    COUNT_FILE="/tmp/zenaiops-m365-app-counts-$$.csv"

    curl \
        --silent \
        --show-error \
        --location \
        "$COUNT_URL" \
        -o "$COUNT_FILE"

    if [ -s "$COUNT_FILE" ]; then

        cat "$COUNT_FILE"

    else

        echo -e "${WARN} App counts report unavailable"

    fi

else

    echo -e "${WARN} Could not obtain app counts report"

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 04 - OFFICE ACTIVATIONS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " MICROSOFT 365 OFFICE ACTIVATIONS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Requesting Office activation report..."

ACTIVATION_URL=$(get_report_location \
    "$GRAPH/reports/getOffice365ActivationsUserDetail")

if [ -n "$ACTIVATION_URL" ]; then

    ACTIVATION_FILE="/tmp/zenaiops-office-activation-$$.csv"

    curl \
        --silent \
        --show-error \
        --location \
        "$ACTIVATION_URL" \
        -o "$ACTIVATION_FILE"

    if [ -s "$ACTIVATION_FILE" ]; then

        python3 - "$ACTIVATION_FILE" <<'PY'

import csv
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone

file = sys.argv[1]

rows = []

with open(
    file,
    encoding="utf-8-sig",
    newline=""
) as f:

    reader = csv.DictReader(f)

    for row in reader:

        rows.append(row)


print()
print("=" * 60)
print(" OFFICE ACTIVATION SUMMARY")
print("=" * 60)
print()

print(
    f"Activation Records : {len(rows)}"
)

print()

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# LAST ACTIVATION
# --------------------------------------------------------------

dates = []

for row in rows:

    value = row.get(
        "Last Activated Date",
        ""
    )

    if value:

        try:

            date = datetime.strptime(
                value,
                "%Y-%m-%d"
            )

            dates.append(date)

        except:

            pass


if dates:

    print(
        "Oldest Activation : "
        f"{min(dates).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}"
    )

    print(
        "Newest Activation : "
        f"{max(dates).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}"
    )

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# PRODUCT TYPES
# --------------------------------------------------------------

products = {}

for row in rows:

    product = row.get(
        "Product Type",
        "Unknown"
    )

    products[product] = (
        products.get(product, 0) + 1
    )

print()
print("Product Activation Records:")
print()

for product, count in sorted(
    products.items(),
    key=lambda x: x[1],
    reverse=True
):

    print(
        f"{product:<35} : {count}"
    )

PY

    else

        echo -e "${WARN} Office activation report unavailable"

    fi

else

    echo -e "${WARN} Could not obtain Office activation report"

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 05 - ZENAIOPS APP GOVERNANCE
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | APPLICATION GOVERNANCE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "Key actions:"
echo
echo "  1. Identify licensed users with low application usage"
echo "  2. Identify inactive users still consuming licenses"
echo "  3. Identify application adoption gaps"
echo "  4. Compare Office activation with license assignment"
echo "  5. Identify users using only Web/Mobile"
echo "  6. Review users with no recent M365 activity"
echo

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | APPLICATION CHECK COMPLETE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------

# --------------------------------------------------------------

rm -f "$APP_FILE"
rm -f "/tmp/zenaiops-m365-app-counts-$$.csv"
rm -f "/tmp/zenaiops-office-activation-$$.csv"

28 — Microsoft 365 Backup & Recovery Governance

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - 28 MICROSOFT 365 BACKUP & RECOVERY GOVERNANCE
# ==============================================================
#
# READ-ONLY ASSESSMENT
#
# Checks:
#   01. Microsoft 365 Backup service
#   02. SharePoint backup policies
#   03. OneDrive backup policies
#   04. Exchange backup policies
#   05. SharePoint protected units
#   06. OneDrive protected units
#   07. Exchange protected units
#   08. Restore points
#   09. Restore sessions
#   10. Policy state
#   11. Backup coverage
#   12. Protection gaps
#
# Graph permission:
#
#   BackupRestore-Configuration.Read.All
#
# IMPORTANT:
#   This script does NOT:
#       - create policies
#       - modify policies
#       - activate protection rules
#       - start restore
#
# ==============================================================

set -o pipefail

GRAPH="https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0"

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# COLORS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
RED='\033[0;31m'
CYAN='\033[0;36m'
NC='\033[0m'

PASS="${GREEN}PASS${NC}"
WARN="${YELLOW}WARN${NC}"
FAIL="${RED}FAIL${NC}"

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# HEADER
# --------------------------------------------------------------

clear

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | MICROSOFT 365 BACKUP & RECOVERY"
echo "============================================================"
echo
echo " Mode : READ-ONLY ASSESSMENT"
echo
echo "============================================================"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CHECK AZ LOGIN
# --------------------------------------------------------------

if ! az account show >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} Azure CLI login required"
    echo
    echo "Run:"
    echo "  az login"
    echo

    exit 1
fi

echo -e "${PASS} Azure CLI authenticated"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# GRAPH GET
# --------------------------------------------------------------

graph_get() {

    local URL="$1"

    az rest \
        --method get \
        --url "$URL" \
        --headers "Accept=application/json"
}

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# PAGINATED GRAPH GET
# --------------------------------------------------------------

graph_get_all() {

    local URL="$1"

    local RESULT='{"value":[]}'

    while [ -n "$URL" ]; do

        RESPONSE=$(graph_get "$URL")

        if [ $? -ne 0 ] || [ -z "$RESPONSE" ]; then

            echo "$RESULT"

            return 0
        fi

        RESULT=$(jq -s '
            .[0].value += .[1].value
            | .[0]
        ' \
            <(echo "$RESULT") \
            <(echo "$RESPONSE"))

        URL=$(echo "$RESPONSE" |
            jq -r '."@odata.nextLink" // empty')

    done

    echo "$RESULT"
}

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 01 - BACKUP SERVICE
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " BACKUP SERVICE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

BACKUP_ROOT=$(graph_get \
    "$GRAPH/solutions/backupRestore")

if [ $? -ne 0 ] || [ -z "$BACKUP_ROOT" ]; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} Microsoft 365 Backup API unavailable"
    echo
    echo "Verify:"
    echo "  BackupRestore-Configuration.Read.All"
    echo
    exit 1
fi

SERVICE_STATUS=$(echo "$BACKUP_ROOT" |
    jq -r '.serviceStatus.status // .serviceStatus // "Unknown"')

echo "Backup API        : Available"
echo "Service Status    : $SERVICE_STATUS"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 02 - SHAREPOINT POLICIES
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " SHAREPOINT BACKUP POLICIES"
echo "============================================================"
echo

SP_POLICIES=$(graph_get_all \
    "$GRAPH/solutions/backupRestore/sharePointProtectionPolicies?\$top=999")

SP_POLICY_COUNT=$(echo "$SP_POLICIES" |
    jq '.value | length')

echo "Policies : $SP_POLICY_COUNT"
echo

if [ "$SP_POLICY_COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

    echo "$SP_POLICIES" |
        jq -r '
        .value[]
        | [
            .id,
            .displayName,
            .status
          ]
        | @tsv
        ' |
        column -t -s $'\t'

else

    echo -e "${WARN} No SharePoint backup policy detected"

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 03 - ONEDRIVE POLICIES
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ONEDRIVE BACKUP POLICIES"
echo "============================================================"
echo

OD_POLICIES=$(graph_get_all \
    "$GRAPH/solutions/backupRestore/oneDriveForBusinessProtectionPolicies?\$top=999")

OD_POLICY_COUNT=$(echo "$OD_POLICIES" |
    jq '.value | length')

echo "Policies : $OD_POLICY_COUNT"
echo

if [ "$OD_POLICY_COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

    echo "$OD_POLICIES" |
        jq -r '
        .value[]
        | [
            .id,
            .displayName,
            .status
          ]
        | @tsv
        ' |
        column -t -s $'\t'

else

    echo -e "${WARN} No OneDrive backup policy detected"

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 04 - EXCHANGE POLICIES
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " EXCHANGE BACKUP POLICIES"
echo "============================================================"
echo

EX_POLICIES=$(graph_get_all \
    "$GRAPH/solutions/backupRestore/exchangeProtectionPolicies?\$top=999")

EX_POLICY_COUNT=$(echo "$EX_POLICIES" |
    jq '.value | length')

echo "Policies : $EX_POLICY_COUNT"
echo

if [ "$EX_POLICY_COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

    echo "$EX_POLICIES" |
        jq -r '
        .value[]
        | [
            .id,
            .displayName,
            .status
          ]
        | @tsv
        ' |
        column -t -s $'\t'

else

    echo -e "${WARN} No Exchange backup policy detected"

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# COUNTERS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

SP_UNITS=0
OD_UNITS=0
EX_UNITS=0

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 05 - SHAREPOINT PROTECTED UNITS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " SHAREPOINT PROTECTION COVERAGE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

if [ "$SP_POLICY_COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

    while read -r POLICY_ID; do

        [ -z "$POLICY_ID" ] && continue

        UNITS=$(graph_get_all \
            "$GRAPH/solutions/backupRestore/sharePointProtectionPolicies/$POLICY_ID/siteProtectionUnits?\$top=999")

        COUNT=$(echo "$UNITS" |
            jq '.value | length')

        SP_UNITS=$((SP_UNITS + COUNT))

        echo "Policy $POLICY_ID"
        echo "Protected Sites : $COUNT"
        echo

    done < <(
        echo "$SP_POLICIES" |
        jq -r '.value[].id'
    )

else

    echo "Protected Sites : 0"

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 06 - ONEDRIVE PROTECTED UNITS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " ONEDRIVE PROTECTION COVERAGE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

if [ "$OD_POLICY_COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

    while read -r POLICY_ID; do

        [ -z "$POLICY_ID" ] && continue

        UNITS=$(graph_get_all \
            "$GRAPH/solutions/backupRestore/oneDriveForBusinessProtectionPolicies/$POLICY_ID/driveProtectionUnits?\$top=999")

        COUNT=$(echo "$UNITS" |
            jq '.value | length')

        OD_UNITS=$((OD_UNITS + COUNT))

        echo "Policy $POLICY_ID"
        echo "Protected OneDrive Accounts : $COUNT"
        echo

    done < <(
        echo "$OD_POLICIES" |
        jq -r '.value[].id'
    )

else

    echo "Protected OneDrive Accounts : 0"

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 07 - EXCHANGE PROTECTED UNITS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " EXCHANGE PROTECTION COVERAGE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

if [ "$EX_POLICY_COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

    while read -r POLICY_ID; do

        [ -z "$POLICY_ID" ] && continue

        UNITS=$(graph_get_all \
            "$GRAPH/solutions/backupRestore/exchangeProtectionPolicies/$POLICY_ID/mailboxProtectionUnits?\$top=999")

        COUNT=$(echo "$UNITS" |
            jq '.value | length')

        EX_UNITS=$((EX_UNITS + COUNT))

        echo "Policy $POLICY_ID"
        echo "Protected Mailboxes : $COUNT"
        echo

    done < <(
        echo "$EX_POLICIES" |
        jq -r '.value[].id'
    )

else

    echo "Protected Mailboxes : 0"

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 08 - RESTORE POINTS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " RESTORE POINTS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

RESTORE_POINTS=$(graph_get_all \
    "$GRAPH/solutions/backupRestore/restorePoints?\$top=999")

RESTORE_POINT_COUNT=$(echo "$RESTORE_POINTS" |
    jq '.value | length')

echo "Restore Points Available : $RESTORE_POINT_COUNT"
echo

if [ "$RESTORE_POINT_COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

    echo "$RESTORE_POINTS" |
        jq -r '
        .value[]
        | [
            (.id // ""),
            (.createdDateTime // ""),
            (.status // "")
          ]
        | @tsv
        ' |
        head -20 |
        column -t -s $'\t'

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 09 - RESTORE SESSIONS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " RESTORE SESSIONS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

RESTORE_SESSIONS=$(graph_get_all \
    "$GRAPH/solutions/backupRestore/restoreSessions?\$top=999")

RESTORE_SESSION_COUNT=$(echo "$RESTORE_SESSIONS" |
    jq '.value | length')

echo "Restore Sessions : $RESTORE_SESSION_COUNT"
echo

if [ "$RESTORE_SESSION_COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

    echo "$RESTORE_SESSIONS" |
        jq -r '
        .value[]
        | [
            (.id // ""),
            (.status // ""),
            (.createdDateTime // ""),
            (.lastModifiedDateTime // "")
          ]
        | @tsv
        ' |
        column -t -s $'\t'

fi

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 10 - PROTECTION UNIT MASTER INVENTORY
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " PROTECTION UNIT INVENTORY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

ALL_UNITS=$(graph_get_all \
    "$GRAPH/solutions/backupRestore/protectionUnits?\$top=999")

ALL_UNIT_COUNT=$(echo "$ALL_UNITS" |
    jq '.value | length')

echo "Protection Units : $ALL_UNIT_COUNT"
echo

echo "$ALL_UNITS" |
    jq -r '
    .value[]
    | [
        (. "@odata.type" // .["@@odata.type"] // "unknown"),
        (.id // ""),
        (.status // "")
      ]
    | @tsv
    ' 2>/dev/null |
    head -30 |
    column -t -s $'\t'

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 11 - COVERAGE SUMMARY
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " BACKUP COVERAGE SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

echo "SharePoint Policies       : $SP_POLICY_COUNT"
echo "SharePoint Protected Sites: $SP_UNITS"
echo
echo "OneDrive Policies         : $OD_POLICY_COUNT"
echo "OneDrive Protected Users  : $OD_UNITS"
echo
echo "Exchange Policies         : $EX_POLICY_COUNT"
echo "Exchange Protected Mailboxes : $EX_UNITS"
echo
echo "Restore Points            : $RESTORE_POINT_COUNT"
echo "Restore Sessions          : $RESTORE_SESSION_COUNT"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# 12 - ZENAIOPS POSTURE
# --------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | BACKUP GOVERNANCE POSTURE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

if [ "$SP_POLICY_COUNT" -eq 0 ]; then

    echo -e "${WARN} SharePoint backup policy : NOT DETECTED"

else

    echo -e "${PASS} SharePoint backup policy : DETECTED"

fi

if [ "$OD_POLICY_COUNT" -eq 0 ]; then

    echo -e "${WARN} OneDrive backup policy   : NOT DETECTED"

else

    echo -e "${PASS} OneDrive backup policy   : DETECTED"

fi

if [ "$EX_POLICY_COUNT" -eq 0 ]; then

    echo -e "${WARN} Exchange backup policy   : NOT DETECTED"

else

    echo -e "${PASS} Exchange backup policy   : DETECTED"

fi

if [ "$RESTORE_POINT_COUNT" -eq 0 ]; then

    echo -e "${WARN} Restore points           : NONE DETECTED"

else

    echo -e "${PASS} Restore points           : AVAILABLE"

fi

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | BACKUP CHECK COMPLETE"
echo "============================================================"
echo
