29 — Microsoft 365 Purview Security & Compliance Governance

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - 29 MICROSOFT 365 PURVIEW GOVERNANCE
# ==============================================================
#
# READ-ONLY ASSESSMENT
#
# Checks:
#   01. DLP policies
#   02. DLP policy mode
#   03. DLP policy locations
#   04. DLP rules
#   05. Retention policies
#   06. Retention policy mode
#   07. Retention rules
#   08. Sensitivity labels
#   09. Sensitivity label policies
#   10. Hold / retention configuration
#   11. Policy errors
#   12. Disabled policies
#   13. Test-mode policies
#   14. Compliance posture summary
#
# Requirements:
#   - PowerShell 7
#   - ExchangeOnlineManagement
#   - Security & Compliance PowerShell permissions
#
# IMPORTANT:
#   This script is READ-ONLY.
#
# ==============================================================

set -o pipefail

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# COLORS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
RED='\033[0;31m'
CYAN='\033[0;36m'
NC='\033[0m'

PASS="${GREEN}PASS${NC}"
WARN="${YELLOW}WARN${NC}"
FAIL="${RED}FAIL${NC}"

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# HEADER
# --------------------------------------------------------------

clear

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | MICROSOFT PURVIEW GOVERNANCE"
echo "============================================================"
echo
echo " Mode : READ-ONLY"
echo
echo "============================================================"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CHECK PWSH
# --------------------------------------------------------------

if ! command -v pwsh >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} PowerShell 7 not found"
    echo
    echo "Install PowerShell 7 and ExchangeOnlineManagement."
    echo

    exit 1

fi

echo -e "${PASS} PowerShell available"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CHECK MODULE
# --------------------------------------------------------------

MODULE=$(pwsh -NoProfile -Command \
    "Get-Module -ListAvailable ExchangeOnlineManagement | Select-Object -First 1 -ExpandProperty Name" \
    2>/dev/null)

if [ "$MODULE" != "ExchangeOnlineManagement" ]; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} ExchangeOnlineManagement module not found"
    echo
    echo "Install:"
    echo
    echo "Install-Module ExchangeOnlineManagement -Scope CurrentUser"
    echo

    exit 1

fi

echo -e "${PASS} ExchangeOnlineManagement module found"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# RUN POWERSHELL
# --------------------------------------------------------------

pwsh -NoProfile -Command - <<'PS'

$ErrorActionPreference = "Continue"

Import-Module ExchangeOnlineManagement

# ==============================================================
# CONNECT
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "Connecting to Microsoft Purview Security & Compliance..." `
    -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host ""

try {

    Connect-IPPSSession `
        -ShowBanner:$false `
        -ErrorAction Stop

}
catch {

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "FAIL - Unable to connect to Purview" `
        -ForegroundColor Red

    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message

    exit 1
}

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "PASS - Purview session established" `
    -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host ""

# ==============================================================
# 01 - DLP POLICIES
# ==============================================================

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " DLP POLICIES"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$dlpPolicies = @(
    Get-DlpCompliancePolicy `
        -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
)

Write-Host (
    "Total DLP Policies : {0}" `
    -f $dlpPolicies.Count
)

Write-Host ""

if ($dlpPolicies.Count -gt 0) {

    $dlpPolicies |
        Select-Object `
            Name,
            Mode,
            Enabled,
            Priority |
        Format-Table -AutoSize

}
else {

    Write-Host `
        "WARN - No DLP policies detected" `
        -ForegroundColor Yellow
}

# ==============================================================
# 02 - DLP POLICY DETAILS
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " DLP POLICY DETAILS"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

foreach ($policy in $dlpPolicies) {

    Write-Host "Policy : $($policy.Name)" `
        -ForegroundColor Cyan

    Write-Host "  Mode       : $($policy.Mode)"
    Write-Host "  Enabled    : $($policy.Enabled)"
    Write-Host "  Priority   : $($policy.Priority)"

    try {

        $detail = Get-DlpCompliancePolicy `
            -Identity $policy.Name `
            -IncludeExtendedProperties $true `
            -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

        Write-Host (
            "  Exchange   : {0}" `
            -f $detail.ExchangeLocation
        )

        Write-Host (
            "  SharePoint : {0}" `
            -f $detail.SharePointLocation
        )

        Write-Host (
            "  OneDrive   : {0}" `
            -f $detail.OneDriveLocation
        )

        Write-Host (
            "  Teams      : {0}" `
            -f $detail.TeamsLocation
        )

    }
    catch {

        Write-Host `
            "  WARN - Detail unavailable" `
            -ForegroundColor Yellow
    }

    Write-Host ""

}

# ==============================================================
# 03 - DLP RULES
# ==============================================================

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " DLP RULES"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$dlpRules = @(
    Get-DlpComplianceRule `
        -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
)

Write-Host (
    "Total DLP Rules : {0}" `
    -f $dlpRules.Count
)

Write-Host ""

if ($dlpRules.Count -gt 0) {

    $dlpRules |
        Select-Object `
            Name,
            Policy,
            Mode,
            Disabled |
        Format-Table -AutoSize

}

# ==============================================================
# 04 - DISABLED DLP RULES
# ==============================================================

$disabledDlpRules = @(
    $dlpRules |
    Where-Object {
        $_.Disabled -eq $true
    }
)

Write-Host ""
Write-Host (
    "Disabled DLP Rules : {0}" `
    -f $disabledDlpRules.Count
)

if ($disabledDlpRules.Count -gt 0) {

    $disabledDlpRules |
        Select-Object Name,Policy,Disabled |
        Format-Table -AutoSize

}

# ==============================================================
# 05 - RETENTION POLICIES
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " RETENTION POLICIES"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$retentionPolicies = @(
    Get-RetentionCompliancePolicy `
        -DistributionDetail `
        -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
)

Write-Host (
    "Total Retention Policies : {0}" `
    -f $retentionPolicies.Count
)

Write-Host ""

if ($retentionPolicies.Count -gt 0) {

    $retentionPolicies |
        Select-Object `
            Name,
            Enabled,
            Mode,
            Workload |
        Format-Table -AutoSize

}
else {

    Write-Host `
        "WARN - No retention policies detected" `
        -ForegroundColor Yellow
}

# ==============================================================
# 06 - RETENTION POLICY ERRORS
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " RETENTION POLICY ERRORS"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$retentionErrors = @(
    Get-RetentionCompliancePolicy `
        -ErrorPolicyOnly `
        -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
)

Write-Host (
    "Policies With Errors : {0}" `
    -f $retentionErrors.Count
)

if ($retentionErrors.Count -gt 0) {

    $retentionErrors |
        Select-Object `
            Name,
            Enabled,
            Mode |
        Format-Table -AutoSize

}

# ==============================================================
# 07 - RETENTION RULES
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " RETENTION RULES"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$retentionRules = @(
    Get-RetentionComplianceRule `
        -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
)

Write-Host (
    "Total Retention Rules : {0}" `
    -f $retentionRules.Count
)

Write-Host ""

if ($retentionRules.Count -gt 0) {

    $retentionRules |
        Select-Object `
            Name,
            Disabled,
            Mode,
            Comment |
        Format-Table -AutoSize

}

# ==============================================================
# 08 - APP RETENTION POLICIES
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " APP RETENTION POLICIES"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$appRetentionPolicies = @(
    Get-AppRetentionCompliancePolicy `
        -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
)

Write-Host (
    "App Retention Policies : {0}" `
    -f $appRetentionPolicies.Count
)

if ($appRetentionPolicies.Count -gt 0) {

    $appRetentionPolicies |
        Select-Object `
            Name,
            Enabled,
            Mode |
        Format-Table -AutoSize

}

# ==============================================================
# 09 - SENSITIVITY LABELS
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " SENSITIVITY LABELS"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$labels = @(
    Get-Label `
        -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
)

Write-Host (
    "Sensitivity Labels : {0}" `
    -f $labels.Count
)

Write-Host ""

if ($labels.Count -gt 0) {

    $labels |
        Select-Object `
            Name,
            DisplayName,
            Comment,
            ContentType |
        Format-Table -AutoSize

}
else {

    Write-Host `
        "WARN - No sensitivity labels detected" `
        -ForegroundColor Yellow
}

# ==============================================================
# 10 - LABEL POLICIES
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " SENSITIVITY LABEL POLICIES"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$labelPolicies = @(
    Get-LabelPolicy `
        -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
)

Write-Host (
    "Label Policies : {0}" `
    -f $labelPolicies.Count
)

Write-Host ""

if ($labelPolicies.Count -gt 0) {

    $labelPolicies |
        Select-Object `
            Name,
            Enabled |
        Format-Table -AutoSize

}

# ==============================================================
# 11 - DISABLED RETENTION RULES
# ==============================================================

$disabledRetentionRules = @(
    $retentionRules |
    Where-Object {
        $_.Disabled -eq $true
    }
)

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " DISABLED COMPLIANCE RULES"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

Write-Host (
    "Disabled Retention Rules : {0}" `
    -f $disabledRetentionRules.Count
)

if ($disabledRetentionRules.Count -gt 0) {

    $disabledRetentionRules |
        Select-Object `
            Name,
            Mode,
            Disabled |
        Format-Table -AutoSize

}

# ==============================================================
# 12 - TEST MODE
# ==============================================================

$testModeDlp = @(
    $dlpPolicies |
    Where-Object {
        $_.Mode -match "Test"
    }
)

$testModeRetention = @(
    $retentionPolicies |
    Where-Object {
        $_.Mode -match "Test"
    }
)

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " TEST / SIMULATION MODE"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

Write-Host (
    "DLP Test Policies        : {0}" `
    -f $testModeDlp.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "Retention Test Policies  : {0}" `
    -f $testModeRetention.Count
)

# ==============================================================
# 13 - POLICY MODE SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " POLICY MODE SUMMARY"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

Write-Host "DLP:"
Write-Host ""

$dlpPolicies |
    Group-Object Mode |
    Select-Object `
        Name,
        Count |
    Format-Table -AutoSize

Write-Host "Retention:"
Write-Host ""

$retentionPolicies |
    Group-Object Mode |
    Select-Object `
        Name,
        Count |
    Format-Table -AutoSize

# ==============================================================
# 14 - PURVIEW POSTURE
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " ZENAIOPS | PURVIEW GOVERNANCE POSTURE"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# DLP
# --------------------------------------------------------------

if ($dlpPolicies.Count -eq 0) {

    Write-Host `
        "WARN - No DLP policies detected" `
        -ForegroundColor Yellow

}
else {

    Write-Host `
        "PASS - DLP policies detected" `
        -ForegroundColor Green
}

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# RETENTION
# --------------------------------------------------------------

if ($retentionPolicies.Count -eq 0) {

    Write-Host `
        "WARN - No retention policies detected" `
        -ForegroundColor Yellow

}
else {

    Write-Host `
        "PASS - Retention policies detected" `
        -ForegroundColor Green
}

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# LABELS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

if ($labels.Count -eq 0) {

    Write-Host `
        "WARN - No sensitivity labels detected" `
        -ForegroundColor Yellow

}
else {

    Write-Host `
        "PASS - Sensitivity labels detected" `
        -ForegroundColor Green
}

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# POLICY ERRORS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

if ($retentionErrors.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host (
        "WARN - Retention policies with errors : {0}" `
        -f $retentionErrors.Count
    ) -ForegroundColor Yellow

}
else {

    Write-Host `
        "PASS - No retention policy errors detected" `
        -ForegroundColor Green
}

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# DISABLED RULES
# --------------------------------------------------------------

if (
    $disabledDlpRules.Count -gt 0 -or
    $disabledRetentionRules.Count -gt 0
) {

    Write-Host `
        "WARN - Disabled compliance rules detected" `
        -ForegroundColor Yellow

}
else {

    Write-Host `
        "PASS - No disabled compliance rules detected" `
        -ForegroundColor Green
}

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# TEST MODE
# --------------------------------------------------------------

if (
    $testModeDlp.Count -gt 0 -or
    $testModeRetention.Count -gt 0
) {

    Write-Host `
        "WARN - Policies in test/simulation mode detected" `
        -ForegroundColor Yellow

}
else {

    Write-Host `
        "PASS - No test-mode policies detected" `
        -ForegroundColor Green
}

# ==============================================================
# FINAL SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " ZENAIOPS | PURVIEW SUMMARY"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

Write-Host (
    "DLP Policies             : {0}" `
    -f $dlpPolicies.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "DLP Rules                : {0}" `
    -f $dlpRules.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "Retention Policies       : {0}" `
    -f $retentionPolicies.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "Retention Rules          : {0}" `
    -f $retentionRules.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "App Retention Policies   : {0}" `
    -f $appRetentionPolicies.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "Sensitivity Labels       : {0}" `
    -f $labels.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "Label Policies           : {0}" `
    -f $labelPolicies.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "Retention Policy Errors  : {0}" `
    -f $retentionErrors.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "Disabled DLP Rules       : {0}" `
    -f $disabledDlpRules.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "Disabled Retention Rules : {0}" `
    -f $disabledRetentionRules.Count
)

Write-Host ""

# ==============================================================
# DISCONNECT
# ==============================================================

Disconnect-ExchangeOnline `
    -Confirm:$false `
    -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " ZENAIOPS | PURVIEW CHECK COMPLETE"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

PS

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | PURVIEW GOVERNANCE COMPLETE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

30 — Microsoft 365 Audit & Investigation Readiness

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - 30 MICROSOFT 365 AUDIT & INVESTIGATION
# ==============================================================
#
# READ-ONLY SECURITY / AUDIT ASSESSMENT
#
# Checks:
#   01. Unified Audit Log ingestion
#   02. Audit configuration
#   03. Recent audit activity
#   04. Administrative activity
#   05. Entra ID activity
#   06. Exchange activity
#   07. SharePoint activity
#   08. OneDrive activity
#   09. Teams activity
#   10. Sharing activity
#   11. Mailbox deletion activity
#   12. High-risk operations
#   13. Audit retention policies
#   14. Investigation readiness
#
# Requirements:
#   - PowerShell 7
#   - ExchangeOnlineManagement
#   - Audit Logs role
#
# ==============================================================

set -o pipefail

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CONFIGURATION
# --------------------------------------------------------------

LOOKBACK_DAYS=7

RESULT_SIZE=500

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# COLORS
# --------------------------------------------------------------

GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
RED='\033[0;31m'
CYAN='\033[0;36m'
NC='\033[0m'

PASS="${GREEN}PASS${NC}"
WARN="${YELLOW}WARN${NC}"
FAIL="${RED}FAIL${NC}"

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# HEADER
# --------------------------------------------------------------

clear

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | M365 AUDIT & INVESTIGATION"
echo "============================================================"
echo
echo " Lookback Period : $LOOKBACK_DAYS days"
echo " Result Limit    : $RESULT_SIZE"
echo " Mode            : READ-ONLY"
echo
echo "============================================================"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CHECK PWSH
# --------------------------------------------------------------

if ! command -v pwsh >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} PowerShell 7 not installed"
    exit 1

fi

echo -e "${PASS} PowerShell available"

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# CHECK EXCHANGE MODULE
# --------------------------------------------------------------

MODULE=$(pwsh -NoProfile -Command \
    "Get-Module -ListAvailable ExchangeOnlineManagement | Select-Object -First 1 -ExpandProperty Name" \
    2>/dev/null)

if [ "$MODULE" != "ExchangeOnlineManagement" ]; then

    echo -e "${FAIL} ExchangeOnlineManagement module not found"
    echo
    echo "Install:"
    echo
    echo "Install-Module ExchangeOnlineManagement -Scope CurrentUser"
    exit 1

fi

echo -e "${PASS} ExchangeOnlineManagement available"
echo

# --------------------------------------------------------------
# RUN POWERSHELL
# --------------------------------------------------------------

pwsh -NoProfile -Command - <<'PS'

$ErrorActionPreference = "Continue"

Import-Module ExchangeOnlineManagement

# ==============================================================
# DATE RANGE
# ==============================================================

$LookbackDays = 7
$ResultSize = 500

$EndDate = Get-Date

$StartDate = $EndDate.AddDays(
    -$LookbackDays
)

# ==============================================================
# CONNECT
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "Connecting to Exchange Online..." `
    -ForegroundColor Cyan

try {

    Connect-ExchangeOnline `
        -ShowBanner:$false `
        -ErrorAction Stop

}
catch {

    Write-Host ""
    Write-Host "FAIL - Exchange Online connection failed" `
        -ForegroundColor Red

    Write-Host $_.Exception.Message

    exit 1
}

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "PASS - Connected to Exchange Online" `
    -ForegroundColor Green

# ==============================================================
# 01 - AUDIT INGESTION
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " UNIFIED AUDIT LOG"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

try {

    $auditConfig = Get-AdminAuditLogConfig

    Write-Host (
        "Unified Audit Log Ingestion : {0}" `
        -f $auditConfig.UnifiedAuditLogIngestionEnabled
    )

    $auditEnabled =
        $auditConfig.UnifiedAuditLogIngestionEnabled

}
catch {

    Write-Host `
        "FAIL - Unable to read audit configuration" `
        -ForegroundColor Red

    $auditEnabled = $false
}

# ==============================================================
# 02 - RECENT AUDIT ACTIVITY
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " RECENT AUDIT ACTIVITY"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$recentAudit = @()

if ($auditEnabled -eq $true) {

    try {

        $recentAudit = @(
            Search-UnifiedAuditLog `
                -StartDate $StartDate `
                -EndDate $EndDate `
                -ResultSize $ResultSize `
                -SessionCommand ReturnLargeSet `
                -SessionId "ZenAiOpsAudit-$([guid]::NewGuid())" `
                -ErrorAction Stop
        )

        Write-Host (
            "Audit Records Retrieved : {0}" `
            -f $recentAudit.Count
        )

    }
    catch {

        Write-Host `
            "WARN - Audit search failed" `
            -ForegroundColor Yellow

        Write-Host $_.Exception.Message
    }

}
else {

    Write-Host `
        "FAIL - Unified Audit Log ingestion is not enabled" `
        -ForegroundColor Red
}

# ==============================================================
# 03 - ACTIVITY BY RECORD TYPE
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " ACTIVITY BY WORKLOAD"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

if ($recentAudit.Count -gt 0) {

    $recentAudit |
        Group-Object RecordType |
        Sort-Object Count -Descending |
        Select-Object `
            Name,
            Count |
        Format-Table -AutoSize

}
else {

    Write-Host "No audit records returned."

}

# ==============================================================
# 04 - ACTIVITY BY USER
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " TOP USERS BY AUDIT ACTIVITY"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

if ($recentAudit.Count -gt 0) {

    $recentAudit |
        Group-Object UserIds |
        Sort-Object Count -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 20 `
            Name,
            Count |
        Format-Table -AutoSize

}

# ==============================================================
# 05 - ADMINISTRATIVE ACTIVITY
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " ADMINISTRATIVE ACTIVITY"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$adminOperations = @(
    "Add member to role.",
    "Remove member from role.",
    "Add eligible member to role.",
    "Remove eligible member from role.",
    "Activate eligible role assignment.",
    "Add user.",
    "Delete user.",
    "Update user.",
    "Add group.",
    "Delete group.",
    "Update group.",
    "Set-AdminAuditLogConfig",
    "New-TransportRule",
    "Set-TransportRule",
    "Remove-TransportRule"
)

$adminEvents = @()

foreach ($operation in $adminOperations) {

    try {

        $events = @(
            Search-UnifiedAuditLog `
                -StartDate $StartDate `
                -EndDate $EndDate `
                -Operations $operation `
                -ResultSize 100 `
                -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        )

        $adminEvents += $events

    }
    catch {
    }
}

Write-Host (
    "Administrative Events : {0}" `
    -f $adminEvents.Count
)

if ($adminEvents.Count -gt 0) {

    $adminEvents |
        Select-Object `
            CreationDate,
            UserIds,
            Operations,
            RecordType |
        Sort-Object CreationDate -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 50 |
        Format-Table -AutoSize

}

# ==============================================================
# 06 - ENTRA / IDENTITY ACTIVITY
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " ENTRA ID ACTIVITY"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$entraEvents = @()

try {

    $entraEvents = @(
        Search-UnifiedAuditLog `
            -StartDate $StartDate `
            -EndDate $EndDate `
            -RecordType AzureActiveDirectory `
            -ResultSize $ResultSize `
            -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    )

}
catch {
}

Write-Host (
    "Entra Audit Events : {0}" `
    -f $entraEvents.Count
)

# ==============================================================
# 07 - EXCHANGE ACTIVITY
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " EXCHANGE ACTIVITY"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$exchangeEvents = @()

try {

    $exchangeEvents = @(
        Search-UnifiedAuditLog `
            -StartDate $StartDate `
            -EndDate $EndDate `
            -RecordType ExchangeAdmin `
            -ResultSize $ResultSize `
            -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    )

}
catch {
}

Write-Host (
    "Exchange Admin Events : {0}" `
    -f $exchangeEvents.Count
)

# ==============================================================
# 08 - SHAREPOINT ACTIVITY
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " SHAREPOINT ACTIVITY"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$sharePointEvents = @()

try {

    $sharePointEvents = @(
        Search-UnifiedAuditLog `
            -StartDate $StartDate `
            -EndDate $EndDate `
            -RecordType SharePoint `
            -ResultSize $ResultSize `
            -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    )

}
catch {
}

Write-Host (
    "SharePoint Events : {0}" `
    -f $sharePointEvents.Count
)

# ==============================================================
# 09 - ONEDRIVE ACTIVITY
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " ONEDRIVE ACTIVITY"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$oneDriveEvents = @()

try {

    $oneDriveEvents = @(
        Search-UnifiedAuditLog `
            -StartDate $StartDate `
            -EndDate $EndDate `
            -RecordType OneDrive `
            -ResultSize $ResultSize `
            -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    )

}
catch {
}

Write-Host (
    "OneDrive Events : {0}" `
    -f $oneDriveEvents.Count
)

# ==============================================================
# 10 - TEAMS ACTIVITY
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " TEAMS ACTIVITY"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$teamsEvents = @()

try {

    $teamsEvents = @(
        Search-UnifiedAuditLog `
            -StartDate $StartDate `
            -EndDate $EndDate `
            -RecordType MicrosoftTeams `
            -ResultSize $ResultSize `
            -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    )

}
catch {
}

Write-Host (
    "Teams Events : {0}" `
    -f $teamsEvents.Count
)

# ==============================================================
# 11 - FILE SHARING
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " SHAREPOINT / ONEDRIVE SHARING"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$sharingEvents = @()

try {

    $sharingEvents = @(
        Search-UnifiedAuditLog `
            -StartDate $StartDate `
            -EndDate $EndDate `
            -RecordType SharePointSharingOperation `
            -ResultSize $ResultSize `
            -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    )

}
catch {
}

Write-Host (
    "Sharing Events : {0}" `
    -f $sharingEvents.Count
)

if ($sharingEvents.Count -gt 0) {

    $sharingEvents |
        Select-Object `
            CreationDate,
            UserIds,
            Operations |
        Sort-Object CreationDate -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 30 |
        Format-Table -AutoSize

}

# ==============================================================
# 12 - EXTERNAL SHARING
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " EXTERNAL SHARING ACTIVITY"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$externalSharing = @()

foreach ($event in $sharingEvents) {

    try {

        $data = $event.AuditData |
            ConvertFrom-Json

        if (
            $data.TargetUserOrGroupName -and
            $data.TargetUserOrGroupName -notmatch "@$(
                $env:USERDNSDOMAIN
            )"
        ) {

            $externalSharing += $event
        }

    }
    catch {
    }
}

Write-Host (
    "Potential External Sharing Events : {0}" `
    -f $externalSharing.Count
)

# ==============================================================
# 13 - MAILBOX DELETION ACTIVITY
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " MAILBOX DELETION ACTIVITY"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$deletionEvents = @()

$deleteOperations = @(
    "SoftDelete",
    "HardDelete",
    "MoveToDeletedItems"
)

foreach ($operation in $deleteOperations) {

    try {

        $events = @(
            Search-UnifiedAuditLog `
                -StartDate $StartDate `
                -EndDate $EndDate `
                -Operations $operation `
                -ResultSize 100 `
                -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        )

        $deletionEvents += $events

    }
    catch {
    }
}

Write-Host (
    "Deletion Events : {0}" `
    -f $deletionEvents.Count
)

# ==============================================================
# 14 - HIGH-RISK ACTIVITY
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " HIGH-RISK AUDIT ACTIVITY"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

$highRiskOperations = @(
    "Set-AdminAuditLogConfig",
    "Add member to role.",
    "Remove member from role.",
    "Activate eligible role assignment.",
    "Add user.",
    "Delete user.",
    "Update user.",
    "Add group.",
    "Delete group.",
    "Update group.",
    "New-TransportRule",
    "Set-TransportRule",
    "Remove-TransportRule",
    "HardDelete",
    "RemoveFolder"
)

$highRiskEvents = @()

foreach ($operation in $highRiskOperations) {

    try {

        $events = @(
            Search-UnifiedAuditLog `
                -StartDate $StartDate `
                -EndDate $EndDate `
                -Operations $operation `
                -ResultSize 100 `
                -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
        )

        $highRiskEvents += $events

    }
    catch {
    }
}

$highRiskEvents =
    $highRiskEvents |
    Sort-Object `
        CreationDate,
        UserIds,
        Operations `
    -Unique

Write-Host (
    "High-Risk Events : {0}" `
    -f $highRiskEvents.Count
)

if ($highRiskEvents.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host ""

    $highRiskEvents |
        Select-Object `
            CreationDate,
            UserIds,
            Operations,
            RecordType |
        Sort-Object CreationDate -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 50 |
        Format-Table -AutoSize

}

# ==============================================================
# 15 - AUDIT RETENTION POLICIES
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " AUDIT RETENTION POLICIES"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

try {

    $retentionPolicies = @(
        Get-UnifiedAuditLogRetentionPolicy `
            -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    )

    Write-Host (
        "Custom Audit Retention Policies : {0}" `
        -f $retentionPolicies.Count
    )

    if ($retentionPolicies.Count -gt 0) {

        $retentionPolicies |
            Sort-Object Priority |
            Select-Object `
                Priority,
                Name,
                RecordTypes,
                Operations,
                UserIds,
                RetentionDuration |
            Format-Table -AutoSize

    }

}
catch {

    Write-Host `
        "WARN - Unable to retrieve audit retention policies" `
        -ForegroundColor Yellow
}

# ==============================================================
# 16 - AUDIT SEARCH HEALTH
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " AUDIT SEARCH HEALTH"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

if ($auditEnabled -eq $true) {

    Write-Host `
        "PASS - Unified Audit Log ingestion enabled" `
        -ForegroundColor Green

}
else {

    Write-Host `
        "FAIL - Unified Audit Log ingestion disabled" `
        -ForegroundColor Red
}

if ($recentAudit.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host `
        "PASS - Recent audit events available" `
        -ForegroundColor Green

}
else {

    Write-Host `
        "WARN - No recent audit events returned" `
        -ForegroundColor Yellow
}

# ==============================================================
# 17 - FINAL POSTURE
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " ZENAIOPS | AUDIT INVESTIGATION POSTURE"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

if ($auditEnabled -eq $true) {

    Write-Host `
        "PASS - Audit logging enabled" `
        -ForegroundColor Green

}
else {

    Write-Host `
        "FAIL - Audit logging disabled" `
        -ForegroundColor Red
}

if ($recentAudit.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host (
        "PASS - {0} recent audit records available" `
        -f $recentAudit.Count
    ) -ForegroundColor Green

}

if ($highRiskEvents.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host (
        "WARN - {0} high-risk events require review" `
        -f $highRiskEvents.Count
    ) -ForegroundColor Yellow

}
else {

    Write-Host `
        "PASS - No configured high-risk operations detected" `
        -ForegroundColor Green
}

if ($externalSharing.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host (
        "WARN - {0} potential external sharing events" `
        -f $externalSharing.Count
    ) -ForegroundColor Yellow

}

if ($deletionEvents.Count -gt 0) {

    Write-Host (
        "INFO - {0} mailbox deletion events" `
        -f $deletionEvents.Count
    )

}

# ==============================================================
# FINAL SUMMARY
# ==============================================================

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " ZENAIOPS | AUDIT SUMMARY"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

Write-Host (
    "Recent Audit Events       : {0}" `
    -f $recentAudit.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "Administrative Events     : {0}" `
    -f $adminEvents.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "Entra Events              : {0}" `
    -f $entraEvents.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "Exchange Events           : {0}" `
    -f $exchangeEvents.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "SharePoint Events         : {0}" `
    -f $sharePointEvents.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "OneDrive Events           : {0}" `
    -f $oneDriveEvents.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "Teams Events              : {0}" `
    -f $teamsEvents.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "Sharing Events            : {0}" `
    -f $sharingEvents.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "High-Risk Events          : {0}" `
    -f $highRiskEvents.Count
)

Write-Host (
    "Deletion Events           : {0}" `
    -f $deletionEvents.Count
)

Write-Host ""

# ==============================================================
# DISCONNECT
# ==============================================================

Disconnect-ExchangeOnline `
    -Confirm:$false `
    -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host " ZENAIOPS | AUDIT CHECK COMPLETE"
Write-Host "============================================================"
Write-Host ""

PS

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS | M365 AUDIT & INVESTIGATION COMPLETE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

31 — M365 Identity Governance & Access Review

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 31 - IDENTITY GOVERNANCE & ACCESS REVIEW
# ==============================================================

set -u

GRAPH="https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0"

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 31 - IDENTITY GOVERNANCE & ACCESS REVIEW"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Helper
# ------------------------------------------------------------

graph_get() {
    local URL="$1"

    az rest \
        --method GET \
        --url "$URL" \
        --headers "Content-Type=application/json" \
        2>/dev/null
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. ACCESS REVIEW DEFINITIONS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "1. ACCESS REVIEW DEFINITIONS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

REVIEWS=$(graph_get \
    "$GRAPH/identityGovernance/accessReviews/definitions?\$select=id,displayName,status,createdDateTime,lastModifiedDateTime,scope,instanceEnumerationScope,settings")

if echo "$REVIEWS" | jq -e '.value' >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    REVIEW_COUNT=$(echo "$REVIEWS" | jq '.value | length')

    echo "Total Access Reviews : $REVIEW_COUNT"
    echo

    if [ "$REVIEW_COUNT" -eq 0 ]; then
        echo "WARN : No Access Reviews configured"
    else
        echo "$REVIEWS" | jq -r '
        .value[] |
        [
            .displayName,
            .status,
            .createdDateTime,
            .lastModifiedDateTime
        ] |
        @tsv' |
        while IFS=$'\t' read -r NAME STATUS CREATED MODIFIED
        do
            echo "Review  : $NAME"
            echo "Status  : $STATUS"
            echo "Created : $CREATED"
            echo "Updated : $MODIFIED"
            echo
        done
    fi

else
    echo "ERROR : Unable to read Access Reviews"
    echo "$REVIEWS" | jq -r '.error.message // .' 2>/dev/null
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. ACCESS REVIEW STATUS SUMMARY
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "2. ACCESS REVIEW STATUS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "$REVIEWS" | jq -r '
.value
| group_by(.status)
| .[]
| "\(. [0].status): \(length)"
' 2>/dev/null || true

echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. REVIEW SETTINGS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "3. ACCESS REVIEW GOVERNANCE SETTINGS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "$REVIEWS" | jq -r '
.value[] |
[
    .displayName,
    (.settings.mailNotificationsEnabled // false),
    (.settings.reminderNotificationsEnabled // false),
    (.settings.justificationRequiredOnApproval // false),
    (.settings.recommendationsEnabled // false),
    (.settings.autoApplyDecisionsEnabled // false)
] |
@tsv
' 2>/dev/null |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r NAME MAIL REMINDER JUSTIFICATION RECOMMENDATIONS AUTO
do
    echo "Review       : $NAME"
    echo "Notifications: $MAIL"
    echo "Reminders    : $REMINDER"
    echo "Justification: $JUSTIFICATION"
    echo "Recommendations: $RECOMMENDATIONS"
    echo "Auto Apply   : $AUTO"
    echo
done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. GROUP INVENTORY
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "4. GROUP GOVERNANCE"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

GROUPS=$(graph_get \
    "$GRAPH/groups?\$select=id,displayName,description,groupTypes,securityEnabled,mailEnabled,membershipRule")

if echo "$GROUPS" | jq -e '.value' >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    GROUP_COUNT=$(echo "$GROUPS" | jq '.value | length')

    echo "Groups returned : $GROUP_COUNT"

    echo
    echo "Group Type Summary:"
    
    echo "$GROUPS" | jq -r '
    .value
    | map(
        if (.groupTypes | index("Unified")) then "Microsoft 365"
        elif .securityEnabled == true then "Security"
        else "Other"
        end
      )
    | group_by(.)
    | .[]
    | "\(.[0]): \(length)"
    '

else
    echo "ERROR : Unable to read groups"
fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. LARGE GROUPS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "5. LARGE GROUPS - REVIEW CANDIDATES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "$GROUPS" | jq -r '.value[].id' 2>/dev/null |
while read -r GROUP_ID
do

    GROUP_NAME=$(echo "$GROUPS" |
        jq -r --arg id "$GROUP_ID" \
        '.value[] | select(.id==$id) | .displayName')

    MEMBERS=$(graph_get \
        "$GRAPH/groups/$GROUP_ID/members?\$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,userType,accountEnabled")

    COUNT=$(echo "$MEMBERS" | jq '.value | length' 2>/dev/null || echo 0)

    if [ "$COUNT" -ge 50 ]; then
        echo "REVIEW : $GROUP_NAME"
        echo "Members: $COUNT"
        echo
    fi

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. GUEST MEMBERS IN GROUPS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "6. GUEST ACCESS REVIEW CANDIDATES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

GUEST_GROUPS=0

echo "$GROUPS" | jq -r '.value[].id' 2>/dev/null |
while read -r GROUP_ID
do

    GROUP_NAME=$(echo "$GROUPS" |
        jq -r --arg id "$GROUP_ID" \
        '.value[] | select(.id==$id) | .displayName')

    MEMBERS=$(graph_get \
        "$GRAPH/groups/$GROUP_ID/members?\$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,userType")

    GUEST_COUNT=$(echo "$MEMBERS" |
        jq '[.value[] | select(.userType=="Guest")] | length' \
        2>/dev/null || echo 0)

    if [ "$GUEST_COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

        echo "Group       : $GROUP_NAME"
        echo "Guest Users : $GUEST_COUNT"
        echo

        echo "$MEMBERS" | jq -r '
        .value[]
        | select(.userType=="Guest")
        | "  - \(.displayName) | \(.userPrincipalName)"
        '

        echo
    fi

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. SUMMARY
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 31 - SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"

REVIEW_COUNT=$(echo "$REVIEWS" | jq '.value | length' 2>/dev/null || echo 0)

echo "Access Reviews configured : $REVIEW_COUNT"

if [ "$REVIEW_COUNT" -eq 0 ]; then
    echo "Governance Status         : WARN"
    echo "Recommendation            : Configure periodic Access Reviews"
else
    echo "Governance Status         : REVIEW"
    echo "Recommendation            : Validate review scope, frequency and completion"
fi

echo
echo "Checks completed:"
echo "  ✓ Access Review definitions"
echo "  ✓ Review status"
echo "  ✓ Review governance settings"
echo "  ✓ Group inventory"
echo "  ✓ Large group review candidates"
echo "  ✓ Guest access review candidates"
echo
echo "============================================================"

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

32 — M365 Privileged Identity & Standing Admin Access

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 32 - PRIVILEGED IDENTITY & STANDING ACCESS
# ==============================================================

set -u

GRAPH="https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0"

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 32 - PRIVILEGED IDENTITY & STANDING ACCESS"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Helper
# ------------------------------------------------------------

graph_get() {
    local URL="$1"

    az rest \
        --method GET \
        --url "$URL" \
        --headers "Content-Type=application/json" \
        2>/dev/null
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. ROLE DEFINITIONS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "1. PRIVILEGED ROLE DEFINITIONS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

ROLES=$(graph_get \
    "$GRAPH/roleManagement/directory/roleDefinitions?\$select=id,displayName,description,isBuiltIn,isEnabled")

if echo "$ROLES" | jq -e '.value' >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    ROLE_COUNT=$(echo "$ROLES" | jq '.value | length')

    echo "Total Role Definitions : $ROLE_COUNT"
    echo

    echo "$ROLES" | jq -r '
    .value[]
    | select(.isEnabled == true)
    | [
        .id,
        .displayName,
        .isBuiltIn
      ]
    | @tsv
    ' |
    while IFS=$'\t' read -r ID NAME BUILTIN
    do
        echo "$NAME"
    done

else

    echo "ERROR : Unable to retrieve role definitions"
    echo "$ROLES" | jq -r '.error.message // .' 2>/dev/null

fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. ROLE ASSIGNMENTS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "2. DIRECTORY ROLE ASSIGNMENTS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

ASSIGNMENTS=$(graph_get \
    "$GRAPH/roleManagement/directory/roleAssignments?\$select=id,principalId,roleDefinitionId,directoryScopeId")

if echo "$ASSIGNMENTS" | jq -e '.value' >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    ASSIGNMENT_COUNT=$(echo "$ASSIGNMENTS" | jq '.value | length')

    echo "Total Role Assignments : $ASSIGNMENT_COUNT"
    echo

else

    echo "ERROR : Unable to retrieve role assignments"
    echo "$ASSIGNMENTS" | jq -r '.error.message // .' 2>/dev/null

fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. PRIVILEGED ROLE ASSIGNMENTS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "3. PRIVILEGED ROLE ASSIGNMENTS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

# Build list of roles commonly considered highly privileged.
# Role definitions are used instead of hard-coded role IDs.

PRIV_ROLE_IDS=$(echo "$ROLES" | jq -r '
.value[]
| select(
    .displayName == "Global Administrator"
    or .displayName == "Privileged Role Administrator"
    or .displayName == "Security Administrator"
    or .displayName == "Conditional Access Administrator"
    or .displayName == "Application Administrator"
    or .displayName == "Cloud Application Administrator"
    or .displayName == "Exchange Administrator"
    or .displayName == "SharePoint Administrator"
    or .displayName == "User Administrator"
    or .displayName == "Authentication Administrator"
    or .displayName == "Privileged Authentication Administrator"
    or .displayName == "Intune Administrator"
)
| .id
')

PRIV_COUNT=0

while read -r ROLE_ID
do

    [ -z "$ROLE_ID" ] && continue

    ROLE_NAME=$(echo "$ROLES" |
        jq -r --arg id "$ROLE_ID" \
        '.value[] | select(.id==$id) | .displayName')

    ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS=$(echo "$ASSIGNMENTS" |
        jq --arg role "$ROLE_ID" '
        [.value[] | select(.roleDefinitionId==$role)]
        ')

    COUNT=$(echo "$ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS" | jq 'length')

    if [ "$COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

        echo
        echo "ROLE : $ROLE_NAME"
        echo "ASSIGNMENTS : $COUNT"

        echo "$ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS" |
        jq -r '
        .[] |
        [
            .principalId,
            .directoryScopeId
        ] |
        @tsv
        ' |
        while IFS=$'\t' read -r PRINCIPAL SCOPE
        do

            echo "  Principal : $PRINCIPAL"
            echo "  Scope     : $SCOPE"

            if [ "$SCOPE" = "/" ]; then
                echo "  Access    : TENANT-WIDE"
            else
                echo "  Access    : SCOPED"
            fi

            echo

        done

        PRIV_COUNT=$((PRIV_COUNT + COUNT))

    fi

done <<< "$PRIV_ROLE_IDS"

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. ASSIGNMENTS BY ROLE
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "4. ASSIGNMENT COUNT BY ROLE"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "$ROLES" | jq -r '.value[] | [.id,.displayName] | @tsv' |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r ROLE_ID ROLE_NAME
do

    COUNT=$(echo "$ASSIGNMENTS" |
        jq --arg role "$ROLE_ID" \
        '[.value[] | select(.roleDefinitionId==$role)] | length')

    if [ "$COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then
        printf "%-45s %5s\n" "$ROLE_NAME" "$COUNT"
    fi

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. TENANT-WIDE PRIVILEGED ASSIGNMENTS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "5. TENANT-WIDE PRIVILEGED ACCESS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "$ASSIGNMENTS" |
jq --argjson roles "$(
    echo "$ROLES" | jq '
    [.value[]
    | select(
        .displayName == "Global Administrator"
        or .displayName == "Privileged Role Administrator"
        or .displayName == "Security Administrator"
        or .displayName == "Conditional Access Administrator"
        or .displayName == "Application Administrator"
        or .displayName == "Cloud Application Administrator"
        or .displayName == "Exchange Administrator"
        or .displayName == "SharePoint Administrator"
        or .displayName == "Authentication Administrator"
        or .displayName == "Privileged Authentication Administrator"
    )
    | {id:.id,name:.displayName}]
    ' 
)" '
def role_name($id):
    ($roles[] | select(.id==$id) | .name);

.value[]
| select(.directoryScopeId == "/")
| select([role_name(.roleDefinitionId)] | length > 0)
| [
    role_name(.roleDefinitionId),
    .principalId,
    .directoryScopeId
]
| @tsv
' 2>/dev/null |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r ROLE PRINCIPAL SCOPE
do
    echo "Role      : $ROLE"
    echo "Principal : $PRINCIPAL"
    echo "Scope     : $SCOPE"
    echo "Review    : TENANT-WIDE STANDING ACCESS"
    echo
done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. PRINCIPAL TYPE RESOLUTION
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "6. PRIVILEGED PRINCIPAL TYPE"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

for PRINCIPAL_ID in $(echo "$ASSIGNMENTS" |
    jq -r --argjson roles "$(
        echo "$ROLES" | jq '
        [.value[]
        | select(
            .displayName == "Global Administrator"
            or .displayName == "Privileged Role Administrator"
            or .displayName == "Security Administrator"
            or .displayName == "Conditional Access Administrator"
            or .displayName == "Application Administrator"
            or .displayName == "Cloud Application Administrator"
        )
        | .id]
        '
    )" '
    .value[]
    | select(.roleDefinitionId as $r | $roles | index($r))
    | .principalId
    ' | sort -u)
do

    USER=$(graph_get \
        "$GRAPH/users/$PRINCIPAL_ID?\$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,accountEnabled,userType")

    if echo "$USER" | jq -e '.id' >/dev/null 2>&1; then

        echo "$USER" | jq -r '
        "USER : \(.displayName)",
        "UPN  : \(.userPrincipalName)",
        "Type : \(.userType)",
        "Enabled : \(.accountEnabled)",
        ""
        '

        continue
    fi

    GROUP=$(graph_get \
        "$GRAPH/groups/$PRINCIPAL_ID?\$select=id,displayName,mail,securityEnabled")

    if echo "$GROUP" | jq -e '.id' >/dev/null 2>&1; then

        echo "$GROUP" | jq -r '
        "GROUP : \(.displayName)",
        "Security Enabled : \(.securityEnabled)",
        ""
        '

        continue
    fi

    APP=$(graph_get \
        "$GRAPH/servicePrincipals/$PRINCIPAL_ID?\$select=id,displayName,appId,accountEnabled")

    if echo "$APP" | jq -e '.id' >/dev/null 2>&1; then

        echo "$APP" | jq -r '
        "SERVICE PRINCIPAL : \(.displayName)",
        "App ID : \(.appId)",
        "Enabled : \(.accountEnabled)",
        ""
        '

        continue
    fi

    echo "PRINCIPAL : $PRINCIPAL_ID"
    echo "Type      : Unable to resolve"
    echo

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. GOVERNANCE SUMMARY
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 32 - SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"

echo "Total role assignments       : ${ASSIGNMENT_COUNT:-0}"
echo "Privileged assignments       : $PRIV_COUNT"

TENANT_WIDE=$(echo "$ASSIGNMENTS" |
    jq --argjson roles "$(
        echo "$ROLES" | jq '
        [.value[]
        | select(
            .displayName == "Global Administrator"
            or .displayName == "Privileged Role Administrator"
            or .displayName == "Security Administrator"
            or .displayName == "Conditional Access Administrator"
            or .displayName == "Application Administrator"
            or .displayName == "Cloud Application Administrator"
            or .displayName == "Authentication Administrator"
            or .displayName == "Privileged Authentication Administrator"
        )
        | .id]
        '
    )" '
    [
      .value[]
      | select(.directoryScopeId == "/")
      | select(.roleDefinitionId as $r | $roles | index($r))
    ]
    | length
    ')

echo "Tenant-wide privileged      : $TENANT_WIDE"

echo
echo "Governance Review:"
echo "  ✓ Privileged roles"
echo "  ✓ Role assignments"
echo "  ✓ Tenant-wide assignments"
echo "  ✓ Principal identification"
echo "  ✓ Service principal access"
echo "  ✓ Group-based privileged access"
echo
echo "Recommended ZenAiOps action:"
echo "  Review standing privileged assignments"
echo "  and evaluate eligible/JIT PIM governance."
echo
echo "============================================================"

33 M365 Application Registrations & Enterprise Apps Security.
#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 33 - APPLICATION REGISTRATIONS & APP SECURITY
# ==============================================================

set -u

GRAPH="https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0"

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 33 - APPLICATION & ENTERPRISE APP SECURITY"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Helper
# ------------------------------------------------------------

graph_get() {
    local URL="$1"

    az rest \
        --method GET \
        --url "$URL" \
        --headers "Content-Type=application/json" \
        2>/dev/null
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. APP REGISTRATIONS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "1. APP REGISTRATIONS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

APPS=$(graph_get \
"$GRAPH/applications?\$select=id,appId,displayName,accountEnabled,signInAudience,createdDateTime,passwordCredentials,keyCredentials")

if echo "$APPS" | jq -e '.value' >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    APP_COUNT=$(echo "$APPS" | jq '.value | length')

    echo "Applications returned : $APP_COUNT"
    echo

    echo "$APPS" | jq -r '
    .value[] |
    [
        .displayName,
        .appId,
        .signInAudience,
        .accountEnabled
    ] |
    @tsv
    ' |
    while IFS=$'\t' read -r NAME APPID AUDIENCE ENABLED
    do
        echo "Application : $NAME"
        echo "App ID      : $APPID"
        echo "Audience    : $AUDIENCE"
        echo "Enabled     : $ENABLED"
        echo
    done

else

    echo "ERROR : Unable to retrieve applications"
    echo "$APPS" | jq -r '.error.message // .' 2>/dev/null

fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. APPLICATION OWNER CHECK
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "2. APPLICATION OWNER GOVERNANCE"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

OWNERLESS=0

echo "$APPS" | jq -r '.value[] | [.id,.displayName] | @tsv' |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r APP_ID APP_NAME
do

    OWNERS=$(graph_get \
        "$GRAPH/applications/$APP_ID/owners?\$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName")

    OWNER_COUNT=$(echo "$OWNERS" |
        jq '.value | length' 2>/dev/null || echo 0)

    if [ "$OWNER_COUNT" -eq 0 ]; then

        echo "REVIEW : $APP_NAME"
        echo "Reason  : No application owner found"
        echo

    fi

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. CREDENTIAL EXPIRY
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "3. APPLICATION CREDENTIAL EXPIRY"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

NOW_EPOCH=$(date +%s)
WARNING_DAYS=30
WARNING_EPOCH=$((NOW_EPOCH + WARNING_DAYS*86400))

echo "$APPS" | jq -r '
.value[]
| .displayName as $app
| .passwordCredentials[]
| [$app, "SECRET", .displayName, .endDateTime]
| @tsv
' |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r APP TYPE CRED END_DATE
do

    END_EPOCH=$(date -d "$END_DATE" +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)

    if [ "$END_EPOCH" -lt "$NOW_EPOCH" ]; then

        echo "EXPIRED"
        echo "Application : $APP"
        echo "Credential  : $CRED"
        echo "Expiry      : $END_DATE"
        echo

    elif [ "$END_EPOCH" -le "$WARNING_EPOCH" ]; then

        echo "EXPIRING SOON"
        echo "Application : $APP"
        echo "Credential  : $CRED"
        echo "Expiry      : $END_DATE"
        echo

    fi

done

echo "$APPS" | jq -r '
.value[]
| .displayName as $app
| .keyCredentials[]
| [$app, "CERTIFICATE", .displayName, .endDateTime]
| @tsv
' |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r APP TYPE CRED END_DATE
do

    END_EPOCH=$(date -d "$END_DATE" +%s 2>/dev/null || echo 0)

    if [ "$END_EPOCH" -lt "$NOW_EPOCH" ]; then

        echo "EXPIRED"
        echo "Application : $APP"
        echo "Credential  : $CRED"
        echo "Expiry      : $END_DATE"
        echo

    elif [ "$END_EPOCH" -le "$WARNING_EPOCH" ]; then

        echo "EXPIRING SOON"
        echo "Application : $APP"
        echo "Credential  : $CRED"
        echo "Expiry      : $END_DATE"
        echo

    fi

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. MULTI-TENANT APPLICATIONS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "4. MULTI-TENANT APPLICATIONS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "$APPS" | jq -r '
.value[]
| select(
    .signInAudience == "AzureADMultipleOrgs"
    or .signInAudience == "AzureADandPersonalMicrosoftAccount"
    or .signInAudience == "PersonalMicrosoftAccount"
)
| [
    .displayName,
    .appId,
    .signInAudience
]
| @tsv
' |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r NAME APPID AUDIENCE
do

    echo "REVIEW"
    echo "Application : $NAME"
    echo "App ID      : $APPID"
    echo "Audience    : $AUDIENCE"
    echo

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. ENTERPRISE APPLICATIONS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "5. ENTERPRISE APPLICATIONS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

SPS=$(graph_get \
"$GRAPH/servicePrincipals?\$select=id,appId,displayName,accountEnabled,servicePrincipalType,appOwnerOrganizationId")

if echo "$SPS" | jq -e '.value' >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    SP_COUNT=$(echo "$SPS" | jq '.value | length')

    echo "Enterprise Applications : $SP_COUNT"
    echo

    echo "$SPS" | jq -r '
    .value[]
    | [
        .displayName,
        .servicePrincipalType,
        .accountEnabled
    ]
    | @tsv
    ' |
    while IFS=$'\t' read -r NAME TYPE ENABLED
    do

        echo "$NAME | $TYPE | Enabled=$ENABLED"

    done

else

    echo "ERROR : Unable to retrieve service principals"

fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. DISABLED ENTERPRISE APPLICATIONS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "6. DISABLED ENTERPRISE APPLICATIONS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "$SPS" | jq -r '
.value[]
| select(.accountEnabled == false)
| [
    .displayName,
    .appId,
    .servicePrincipalType
]
| @tsv
' |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r NAME APPID TYPE
do

    echo "Disabled App : $NAME"
    echo "App ID       : $APPID"
    echo "Type         : $TYPE"
    echo

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. APPLICATION PERMISSIONS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "7. APPLICATION PERMISSION GRANTS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

# Microsoft Graph resource service principal

GRAPH_SP=$(graph_get \
"$GRAPH/servicePrincipals?\$filter=appId eq '00000003-0000-0000-c000-000000000000'&\$select=id,displayName,appId,appRoles")

GRAPH_SP_ID=$(echo "$GRAPH_SP" |
jq -r '.value[0].id // empty')

if [ -z "$GRAPH_SP_ID" ]; then

    echo "WARN : Microsoft Graph service principal not found"

else

    echo "Microsoft Graph SP : $GRAPH_SP_ID"
    echo

    echo "$SPS" | jq -r '.value[].id' |
    while read -r SP_ID
    do

        ASSIGNMENTS=$(graph_get \
            "$GRAPH/servicePrincipals/$SP_ID/appRoleAssignments")

        COUNT=$(echo "$ASSIGNMENTS" |
            jq '.value | length' 2>/dev/null || echo 0)

        if [ "$COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

            APP_NAME=$(echo "$SPS" |
                jq -r --arg id "$SP_ID" \
                '.value[] | select(.id==$id) | .displayName')

            echo "Application : $APP_NAME"
            echo "Permissions : $COUNT"

            echo "$ASSIGNMENTS" |
            jq -r '.value[] | .appRoleId' |
            while read -r ROLE_ID
            do

                ROLE=$(echo "$GRAPH_SP" |
                    jq -r --argjson id "$ROLE_ID" '
                    .value[0].appRoles[]
                    | select(.id==$id)
                    | .value
                    ' 2>/dev/null)

                if [ -n "$ROLE" ]; then
                    echo "  - $ROLE"
                else
                    echo "  - AppRoleId: $ROLE_ID"
                fi

            done

            echo

        fi

    done

fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. HIGH-RISK GRAPH PERMISSIONS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "8. HIGH-PRIVILEGE GRAPH PERMISSION CANDIDATES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

HIGH_RISK_REGEX='(Directory\.ReadWrite\.All|Directory\.Read\.All|RoleManagement\.ReadWrite\.Directory|RoleManagement\.Read\.Directory|Application\.ReadWrite\.All|AppRoleAssignment\.ReadWrite\.All|Mail\.ReadWrite\.All|Files\.ReadWrite\.All|Sites\.FullControl\.All|User\.ReadWrite\.All)'

echo "$SPS" | jq -r '.value[].id' |
while read -r SP_ID
do

    ASSIGNMENTS=$(graph_get \
        "$GRAPH/servicePrincipals/$SP_ID/appRoleAssignments")

    APP_NAME=$(echo "$SPS" |
        jq -r --arg id "$SP_ID" \
        '.value[] | select(.id==$id) | .displayName')

    echo "$ASSIGNMENTS" |
    jq -r '.value[] | .appRoleId' |
    while read -r ROLE_ID
    do

        ROLE=$(echo "$GRAPH_SP" |
            jq -r --argjson id "$ROLE_ID" '
            .value[0].appRoles[]
            | select(.id==$id)
            | .value
            ' 2>/dev/null)

        if echo "$ROLE" | grep -Eq "$HIGH_RISK_REGEX"; then

            echo "REVIEW"
            echo "Application : $APP_NAME"
            echo "Permission  : $ROLE"
            echo

        fi

    done

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. SUMMARY
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 33 - SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"

echo "App Registrations       : ${APP_COUNT:-0}"
echo "Enterprise Applications : ${SP_COUNT:-0}"

MULTI_TENANT=$(echo "$APPS" |
    jq '[.value[]
        | select(
            .signInAudience == "AzureADMultipleOrgs"
            or .signInAudience == "AzureADandPersonalMicrosoftAccount"
            or .signInAudience == "PersonalMicrosoftAccount"
        )] | length' 2>/dev/null || echo 0)

DISABLED=$(echo "$SPS" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.accountEnabled == false)] | length' \
    2>/dev/null || echo 0)

echo "Multi-Tenant Apps        : $MULTI_TENANT"
echo "Disabled Enterprise Apps : $DISABLED"

echo
echo "Governance Checks:"
echo "  ✓ App registrations"
echo "  ✓ Application ownership"
echo "  ✓ Secret expiry"
echo "  ✓ Certificate expiry"
echo "  ✓ Multi-tenant applications"
echo "  ✓ Enterprise applications"
echo "  ✓ Disabled applications"
echo "  ✓ Application permissions"
echo "  ✓ High-privilege permission candidates"
echo
echo "============================================================"
