34 — M365 OAuth Consent & Third-Party SaaS Governance

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 34 - OAUTH CONSENT & SAAS GOVERNANCE
# ==============================================================

set -u

GRAPH="https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0"

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 34 - OAUTH CONSENT & SAAS GOVERNANCE"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Helper
# ------------------------------------------------------------

graph_get() {
    local URL="$1"

    az rest \
        --method GET \
        --url "$URL" \
        --headers "Content-Type=application/json" \
        2>/dev/null
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. SERVICE PRINCIPALS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "1. ENTERPRISE APPLICATION INVENTORY"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

SPS=$(graph_get \
"$GRAPH/servicePrincipals?\$select=id,appId,displayName,accountEnabled,servicePrincipalType,appOwnerOrganizationId")

if ! echo "$SPS" | jq -e '.value' >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR : Unable to retrieve service principals"
    echo "$SPS" | jq -r '.error.message // .' 2>/dev/null
    exit 1
fi

SP_COUNT=$(echo "$SPS" | jq '.value | length')

echo "Enterprise Applications : $SP_COUNT"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. OAUTH PERMISSION GRANTS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "2. OAUTH DELEGATED PERMISSION GRANTS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

GRANTS=$(graph_get \
"$GRAPH/oauth2PermissionGrants?\$select=id,clientId,consentType,principalId,resourceId,scope")

if ! echo "$GRANTS" | jq -e '.value' >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    echo "ERROR : Unable to retrieve OAuth permission grants"
    echo "$GRANTS" | jq -r '.error.message // .' 2>/dev/null
    exit 1
fi

GRANT_COUNT=$(echo "$GRANTS" | jq '.value | length')

echo "OAuth Grants : $GRANT_COUNT"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. TENANT-WIDE CONSENT
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "3. TENANT-WIDE CONSENT"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

ALL_PRINCIPAL_COUNT=$(echo "$GRANTS" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.consentType=="AllPrincipals")] | length')

echo "Tenant-wide OAuth grants : $ALL_PRINCIPAL_COUNT"
echo

echo "$GRANTS" |
jq -r '
.value[]
| select(.consentType=="AllPrincipals")
| [
    .clientId,
    .resourceId,
    .scope
]
| @tsv
' |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r CLIENT RESOURCE SCOPE
do

    APP_NAME=$(echo "$SPS" |
        jq -r --arg id "$CLIENT" \
        '.value[] | select(.id==$id) | .displayName // empty')

    [ -z "$APP_NAME" ] && APP_NAME="Unknown Application"

    echo "Application : $APP_NAME"
    echo "Client ID   : $CLIENT"
    echo "Resource ID : $RESOURCE"
    echo "Scopes      : $SCOPE"
    echo "Consent     : ALL USERS"
    echo

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. USER-SPECIFIC CONSENT
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "4. USER-SPECIFIC CONSENT"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

USER_CONSENT_COUNT=$(echo "$GRANTS" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.consentType=="Principal")] | length')

echo "User-specific grants : $USER_CONSENT_COUNT"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. MICROSOFT GRAPH RESOURCE
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "5. MICROSOFT GRAPH OAUTH GRANTS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

GRAPH_SP=$(graph_get \
"$GRAPH/servicePrincipals?\$filter=appId eq '00000003-0000-0000-c000-000000000000'&\$select=id,displayName,appId")

GRAPH_SP_ID=$(echo "$GRAPH_SP" |
    jq -r '.value[0].id // empty')

if [ -z "$GRAPH_SP_ID" ]; then
    echo "WARN : Microsoft Graph service principal not found"
else

    GRAPH_GRANTS=$(echo "$GRANTS" |
        jq --arg rid "$GRAPH_SP_ID" '
        [.value[] | select(.resourceId==$rid)]
        ')

    GRAPH_GRANT_COUNT=$(echo "$GRAPH_GRANTS" | jq 'length')

    echo "Microsoft Graph grants : $GRAPH_GRANT_COUNT"
    echo

fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. HIGH-RISK OAUTH SCOPES
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "6. HIGH-PRIVILEGE OAUTH SCOPES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

HIGH_RISK_SCOPES=(
    "Directory.ReadWrite.All"
    "Directory.Read.All"
    "RoleManagement.ReadWrite.Directory"
    "RoleManagement.Read.Directory"
    "Application.ReadWrite.All"
    "AppRoleAssignment.ReadWrite.All"
    "Mail.ReadWrite"
    "Mail.ReadWrite.Shared"
    "Mail.ReadWrite.All"
    "Files.ReadWrite.All"
    "Sites.ReadWrite.All"
    "Sites.FullControl.All"
    "User.ReadWrite.All"
    "Group.ReadWrite.All"
    "GroupMember.ReadWrite.All"
    "offline_access"
)

echo "$GRANTS" |
jq -r '
.value[]
| [
    .clientId,
    .consentType,
    .scope
]
| @tsv
' |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r CLIENT CONSENT SCOPE
do

    for RISK_SCOPE in "${HIGH_RISK_SCOPES[@]}"
    do

        if echo " $SCOPE " | grep -qE "(^| )${RISK_SCOPE}( |$)"; then

            APP_NAME=$(echo "$SPS" |
                jq -r --arg id "$CLIENT" \
                '.value[] | select(.id==$id) | .displayName // empty')

            [ -z "$APP_NAME" ] && APP_NAME="Unknown Application"

            echo "REVIEW"
            echo "Application : $APP_NAME"
            echo "Permission  : $RISK_SCOPE"
            echo "Consent     : $CONSENT"
            echo

        fi

    done

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. BROAD CONSENT CANDIDATES
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "7. BROAD ACCESS REVIEW CANDIDATES"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "$GRANTS" |
jq -r '
.value[]
| select(.consentType=="AllPrincipals")
| select(
    (.scope | split(" ") | length) >= 5
)
| [
    .clientId,
    (.scope | split(" ") | length),
    .scope
]
| @tsv
' |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r CLIENT SCOPE_COUNT SCOPE
do

    APP_NAME=$(echo "$SPS" |
        jq -r --arg id "$CLIENT" \
        '.value[] | select(.id==$id) | .displayName // empty')

    [ -z "$APP_NAME" ] && APP_NAME="Unknown Application"

    echo "REVIEW"
    echo "Application : $APP_NAME"
    echo "Scope Count : $SCOPE_COUNT"
    echo "Scopes      : $SCOPE"
    echo

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. DISABLED APPLICATIONS WITH CONSENT
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "8. DISABLED APPS WITH EXISTING CONSENT"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "$GRANTS" |
jq -r '.value[].clientId' |
sort -u |
while read -r CLIENT
do

    ENABLED=$(echo "$SPS" |
        jq -r --arg id "$CLIENT" \
        '.value[] | select(.id==$id) | .accountEnabled // true')

    if [ "$ENABLED" = "false" ]; then

        APP_NAME=$(echo "$SPS" |
            jq -r --arg id "$CLIENT" \
            '.value[] | select(.id==$id) | .displayName')

        echo "REVIEW : $APP_NAME"
        echo "Reason : Disabled application still has OAuth grants"
        echo

    fi

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. CONSENT SUMMARY BY APPLICATION
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "9. APPLICATION CONSENT SUMMARY"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "$GRANTS" |
jq -r '.value[].clientId' |
sort -u |
while read -r CLIENT
do

    APP_NAME=$(echo "$SPS" |
        jq -r --arg id "$CLIENT" \
        '.value[] | select(.id==$id) | .displayName // empty')

    [ -z "$APP_NAME" ] && APP_NAME="Unknown Application"

    TOTAL=$(echo "$GRANTS" |
        jq --arg id "$CLIENT" \
        '[.value[] | select(.clientId==$id)] | length')

    TENANT=$(echo "$GRANTS" |
        jq --arg id "$CLIENT" \
        '[.value[] | select(.clientId==$id and .consentType=="AllPrincipals")] | length')

    USER=$(echo "$GRANTS" |
        jq --arg id "$CLIENT" \
        '[.value[] | select(.clientId==$id and .consentType=="Principal")] | length')

    echo "$APP_NAME"
    echo "  Total grants : $TOTAL"
    echo "  Tenant-wide  : $TENANT"
    echo "  User-specific: $USER"
    echo

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 10. SUMMARY
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 34 - SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"

echo "Enterprise Applications : $SP_COUNT"
echo "OAuth Permission Grants : $GRANT_COUNT"
echo "Tenant-Wide Grants      : $ALL_PRINCIPAL_COUNT"
echo "User-Specific Grants    : $USER_CONSENT_COUNT"

HIGH_RISK_COUNT=0

for RISK_SCOPE in "${HIGH_RISK_SCOPES[@]}"
do

    COUNT=$(echo "$GRANTS" |
        jq --arg scope "$RISK_SCOPE" '
        [
            .value[]
            | select(
                (.scope | split(" ") | index($scope)) != null
            )
        ] | length
        ')

    HIGH_RISK_COUNT=$((HIGH_RISK_COUNT + COUNT))

done

echo "High-Privilege Candidates: $HIGH_RISK_COUNT"

echo
echo "Governance Checks:"
echo "  ✓ OAuth delegated grants"
echo "  ✓ Tenant-wide consent"
echo "  ✓ User-specific consent"
echo "  ✓ Microsoft Graph access"
echo "  ✓ High-privilege scopes"
echo "  ✓ Broad permission grants"
echo "  ✓ Disabled apps with consent"
echo
echo "============================================================"

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

35 — M365 External Sharing & Guest Access Risk

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 35 - EXTERNAL SHARING & GUEST ACCESS RISK
# ==============================================================

set -u

GRAPH="https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0"

echo
echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 35 - EXTERNAL SHARING & GUEST ACCESS RISK"
echo "============================================================"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# Helper
# ------------------------------------------------------------

graph_get() {
    local URL="$1"

    az rest \
        --method GET \
        --url "$URL" \
        --headers "Content-Type=application/json" \
        2>/dev/null
}

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. GUEST USERS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "1. GUEST USER INVENTORY"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

GUESTS=$(graph_get \
"$GRAPH/users?\$filter=userType eq 'Guest'&\$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,userType,accountEnabled,createdDateTime,externalUserState,externalUserStateChangeDateTime,signInActivity")

if ! echo "$GUESTS" | jq -e '.value' >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo "ERROR : Unable to retrieve guest users"
    echo "$GUESTS" | jq -r '.error.message // .' 2>/dev/null
    exit 1

fi

GUEST_COUNT=$(echo "$GUESTS" | jq '.value | length')

echo "Total Guest Users : $GUEST_COUNT"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. ENABLED / DISABLED GUESTS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "2. GUEST ACCOUNT STATUS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

ENABLED_GUESTS=$(echo "$GUESTS" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.accountEnabled==true)] | length')

DISABLED_GUESTS=$(echo "$GUESTS" |
    jq '[.value[] | select(.accountEnabled==false)] | length')

echo "Enabled Guests  : $ENABLED_GUESTS"
echo "Disabled Guests : $DISABLED_GUESTS"
echo

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. GUEST DETAILS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "3. GUEST DETAILS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "$GUESTS" |
jq -r '
.value[]
|
[
    .displayName,
    .userPrincipalName,
    .accountEnabled,
    .externalUserState,
    .createdDateTime,
    (.signInActivity.lastSignInDateTime // "Never")
]
| @tsv
' |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r NAME UPN ENABLED STATE CREATED LASTSIGNIN
do

    echo "Guest        : $NAME"
    echo "UPN          : $UPN"
    echo "Enabled      : $ENABLED"
    echo "State        : $STATE"
    echo "Created      : $CREATED"
    echo "Last Sign-In : $LASTSIGNIN"
    echo

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. GUESTS WITHOUT SIGN-IN
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "4. GUESTS WITHOUT SIGN-IN ACTIVITY"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

NO_SIGNIN=$(echo "$GUESTS" |
    jq '[.value[] |
        select(
            (.signInActivity.lastSignInDateTime == null)
            or
            (.signInActivity.lastSignInDateTime == "")
        )
    ]')

NO_SIGNIN_COUNT=$(echo "$NO_SIGNIN" | jq 'length')

echo "Guests without recorded sign-in : $NO_SIGNIN_COUNT"
echo

echo "$NO_SIGNIN" |
jq -r '
.[] |
[
    .displayName,
    .userPrincipalName,
    .createdDateTime,
    .externalUserState
]
| @tsv
' |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r NAME UPN CREATED STATE
do

    echo "REVIEW"
    echo "Guest   : $NAME"
    echo "UPN     : $UPN"
    echo "Created : $CREATED"
    echo "State   : $STATE"
    echo

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. GROUP INVENTORY
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "5. GROUP INVENTORY"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

GROUPS=$(graph_get \
"$GRAPH/groups?\$select=id,displayName,groupTypes,securityEnabled,mailEnabled")

if ! echo "$GROUPS" | jq -e '.value' >/dev/null 2>&1; then

    echo "ERROR : Unable to retrieve groups"

else

    GROUP_COUNT=$(echo "$GROUPS" | jq '.value | length')

    echo "Groups : $GROUP_COUNT"
    echo

fi

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. GROUPS CONTAINING GUESTS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "6. GROUPS CONTAINING GUEST USERS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

GUEST_GROUP_COUNT=0
GUEST_MEMBERSHIP_COUNT=0

echo "$GROUPS" |
jq -r '.value[].id' |
while read -r GROUP_ID
do

    GROUP_NAME=$(echo "$GROUPS" |
        jq -r --arg id "$GROUP_ID" \
        '.value[] | select(.id==$id) | .displayName')

    MEMBERS=$(graph_get \
        "$GRAPH/groups/$GROUP_ID/members?\$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,userType,accountEnabled")

    if ! echo "$MEMBERS" | jq -e '.value' >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        continue
    fi

    GUESTS_IN_GROUP=$(echo "$MEMBERS" |
        jq '[.value[] | select(.userType=="Guest")]')

    COUNT=$(echo "$GUESTS_IN_GROUP" | jq 'length')

    if [ "$COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

        echo "GROUP : $GROUP_NAME"
        echo "Guests: $COUNT"

        echo "$GUESTS_IN_GROUP" |
        jq -r '
        .[] |
        "  - \(.displayName) | \(.userPrincipalName)"
        '

        echo

    fi

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. LARGE GUEST GROUPS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "7. LARGE GUEST GROUPS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "$GROUPS" |
jq -r '.value[].id' |
while read -r GROUP_ID
do

    GROUP_NAME=$(echo "$GROUPS" |
        jq -r --arg id "$GROUP_ID" \
        '.value[] | select(.id==$id) | .displayName')

    MEMBERS=$(graph_get \
        "$GRAPH/groups/$GROUP_ID/members?\$select=id,userType")

    TOTAL=$(echo "$MEMBERS" |
        jq '.value | length' 2>/dev/null || echo 0)

    GUEST_TOTAL=$(echo "$MEMBERS" |
        jq '[.value[] | select(.userType=="Guest")] | length' \
        2>/dev/null || echo 0)

    if [ "$GUEST_TOTAL" -ge 10 ]; then

        echo "REVIEW : $GROUP_NAME"
        echo "Total Members : $TOTAL"
        echo "Guest Members : $GUEST_TOTAL"
        echo

    fi

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. GUESTS WITH DIRECTORY ROLES
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "8. GUESTS WITH DIRECTORY ROLE ASSIGNMENTS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS=$(graph_get \
"$GRAPH/roleManagement/directory/roleAssignments?\$select=principalId,roleDefinitionId,directoryScopeId")

ROLE_DEFINITIONS=$(graph_get \
"$GRAPH/roleManagement/directory/roleDefinitions?\$select=id,displayName")

echo "$GUESTS" |
jq -r '.value[].id' |
while read -r GUEST_ID
do

    ROLES=$(echo "$ROLE_ASSIGNMENTS" |
        jq --arg id "$GUEST_ID" '
        [.value[] | select(.principalId==$id)]
        ')

    ROLE_COUNT=$(echo "$ROLES" | jq 'length')

    if [ "$ROLE_COUNT" -gt 0 ]; then

        GUEST_NAME=$(echo "$GUESTS" |
            jq -r --arg id "$GUEST_ID" \
            '.value[] | select(.id==$id) | .displayName')

        GUEST_UPN=$(echo "$GUESTS" |
            jq -r --arg id "$GUEST_ID" \
            '.value[] | select(.id==$id) | .userPrincipalName')

        echo "REVIEW"
        echo "Guest : $GUEST_NAME"
        echo "UPN   : $GUEST_UPN"

        echo "$ROLES" |
        jq -r '.[] | .roleDefinitionId' |
        while read -r ROLE_ID
        do

            ROLE_NAME=$(echo "$ROLE_DEFINITIONS" |
                jq -r --arg id "$ROLE_ID" \
                '.value[] | select(.id==$id) | .displayName')

            echo "Role  : $ROLE_NAME"

        done

        echo

    fi

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 9. PENDING GUEST INVITATIONS
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "9. PENDING GUEST INVITATIONS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

PENDING=$(echo "$GUESTS" |
    jq '[.value[] |
        select(.externalUserState=="PendingAcceptance")
    ]')

PENDING_COUNT=$(echo "$PENDING" | jq 'length')

echo "Pending Invitations : $PENDING_COUNT"
echo

echo "$PENDING" |
jq -r '
.[] |
[
    .displayName,
    .userPrincipalName,
    .createdDateTime
]
| @tsv
' |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r NAME UPN CREATED
do

    echo "Guest   : $NAME"
    echo "UPN     : $UPN"
    echo "Created : $CREATED"
    echo

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 10. GUEST ACCOUNT AGE
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "10. OLD GUEST ACCOUNTS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

CUTOFF=$(date -d "180 days ago" -u +"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")

echo "Review cutoff : $CUTOFF"
echo

echo "$GUESTS" |
jq -r --arg cutoff "$CUTOFF" '
.value[]
| select(.createdDateTime < $cutoff)
| [
    .displayName,
    .userPrincipalName,
    .createdDateTime,
    (.signInActivity.lastSignInDateTime // "Never")
]
| @tsv
' |
while IFS=$'\t' read -r NAME UPN CREATED LASTSIGNIN
do

    echo "REVIEW"
    echo "Guest        : $NAME"
    echo "UPN          : $UPN"
    echo "Created      : $CREATED"
    echo "Last Sign-In : $LASTSIGNIN"
    echo

done

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# 11. SUMMARY
# ------------------------------------------------------------

echo "============================================================"
echo " ZENAIOPS 35 - SUMMARY"
echo "============================================================"

echo "Total Guests                 : $GUEST_COUNT"
echo "Enabled Guests               : $ENABLED_GUESTS"
echo "Disabled Guests              : $DISABLED_GUESTS"
echo "No Sign-In Activity          : $NO_SIGNIN_COUNT"
echo "Pending Invitations          : $PENDING_COUNT"
echo "Groups                       : ${GROUP_COUNT:-0}"

echo
echo "Governance Checks:"
echo "  ✓ Guest inventory"
echo "  ✓ Guest account status"
echo "  ✓ Guest sign-in activity"
echo "  ✓ Guest group membership"
echo "  ✓ Large guest groups"
echo "  ✓ Guest privileged roles"
echo "  ✓ Pending invitations"
echo "  ✓ Old guest accounts"
echo
echo "============================================================"

#!/usr/bin/env bash

# ==============================================================
# ZENAIOPS - M365 MASTER CUSTOMER ASSESSMENT V2
# ==============================================================

set -u
set +H

GRAPH="https://graph.microsoft.com/v1.0"

CUSTOMER="${1:-Customer}"

# ==============================================================
# COLORS
# ==============================================================

RED="\033[31m"
YELLOW="\033[33m"
GREEN="\033[32m"
CYAN="\033[36m"
RESET="\033[0m"

# ==============================================================
# COUNTERS
# ==============================================================

HIGH_RISK=0
REVIEW=0
OPPORTUNITY=0

# ==============================================================
# GRAPH GET
# ==============================================================

graph_get() {

    local URL="$1"

    az rest \
        --method GET \
        --url "$URL" \
        --headers "Content-Type=application/json"

}

# ==============================================================
# GRAPH COLLECTION
# ==============================================================

graph_all() {

    local URL="$1"
    local RESPONSE
    local NEXT
    local DATA="[]"

    while [ -n "$URL" ]
    do

        RESPONSE=$(graph_get "$URL")

        if ! echo "$RESPONSE" | jq -e '.value' >/dev/null 2>&1
        then
            echo "$RESPONSE" >&2
            return 1
        fi

        DATA=$(jq -n \
            --argjson A "$DATA" \
            --argjson B "$(echo "$RESPONSE" | jq '.value')" \
            '$A + $B')

        NEXT=$(echo "$RESPONSE" |
            jq -r '."@odata.nextLink" // empty')

        URL="$NEXT"

    done

    echo "$DATA"

}

# ==============================================================
# STATUS
# ==============================================================

STATUS_GOOD() {
    echo -e "${GREEN}✓ OK${RESET}"
}

STATUS_REVIEW() {
    echo -e "${YELLOW}⚠ REVIEW${RESET}"
}

STATUS_HIGH() {
    echo -e "${RED}🔴 HIGH RISK${RESET}"
}

STATUS_OPPORTUNITY() {
    echo -e "${CYAN}💡 OPPORTUNITY${RESET}"
}

# ==============================================================
# HEADER
# ==============================================================

clear

echo
echo "╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗"
echo "║                 ZENAIOPS M365 ASSESSMENT                  ║"
echo "╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝"
echo
echo "CUSTOMER : $CUSTOMER"
echo "DATE     : $(date '+%d %b %Y %H:%M')"
echo

# ==============================================================
# 00. CONNECTIVITY TEST
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "00. MICROSOFT GRAPH CONNECTIVITY"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

ME=$(az rest \
    --method GET \
    --url "$GRAPH/organization" \
    --headers "Content-Type=application/json" \
    2>&1)

if echo "$ME" | jq -e '.value' >/dev/null 2>&1
then

    TENANT_NAME=$(echo "$ME" |
        jq -r '.value[0].displayName // "Unknown Tenant"')

    TENANT_ID=$(echo "$ME" |
        jq -r '.value[0].id // "Unknown"')

    echo "Tenant      : $TENANT_NAME"
    echo "Tenant ID   : $TENANT_ID"
    echo "Graph       : CONNECTED"

else

    echo
    echo "❌ Microsoft Graph connection failed."
    echo
    echo "$ME"
    echo
    echo "Check:"
    echo "  1. az login"
    echo "  2. Microsoft Graph permissions"
    echo "  3. Admin consent"
    echo
    exit 1

fi

echo

# ==============================================================
# 01. IDENTITY & MFA
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "01. IDENTITY & MFA"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

USERS=$(graph_all \
"$GRAPH/users?\$select=id,displayName,userPrincipalName,accountEnabled,userType,assignedLicenses,signInActivity&\$top=999")

if [ $? -ne 0 ]
then
    echo "❌ Unable to retrieve users"
    STATUS_REVIEW
    REVIEW=$((REVIEW+1))
else

    USER_COUNT=$(echo "$USERS" | jq 'length')

    ACTIVE_USERS=$(echo "$USERS" |
        jq '[.[] |
            select(
                .accountEnabled==true
                and
                .userType=="Member"
            )
        ] | length')

    GUEST_USERS=$(echo "$USERS" |
        jq '[.[] |
            select(.userType=="Guest")
        ] | length')

    echo "Total Users        : $USER_COUNT"
    echo "Active Members     : $ACTIVE_USERS"
    echo "Guest Users        : $GUEST_USERS"

    MFA=$(graph_all \
    "$GRAPH/reports/authenticationMethods/userRegistrationDetails?\$top=999")

    if [ $? -ne 0 ]
    then

        echo "MFA Report         : Permission / API issue"
        STATUS_REVIEW
        REVIEW=$((REVIEW+1))

    else

        MFA_REGISTERED=$(echo "$MFA" |
            jq '[.[] |
                select(.isMfaRegistered==true)
            ] | length')

        MFA_NOT_REGISTERED=$(echo "$MFA" |
            jq '[.[] |
                select(.isMfaRegistered==false)
            ] | length')

        echo "MFA Registered     : $MFA_REGISTERED"
        echo "MFA Not Registered : $MFA_NOT_REGISTERED"

        if [ "$MFA_NOT_REGISTERED" -gt 0 ]
        then
            STATUS_REVIEW
            REVIEW=$((REVIEW+1))
        else
            STATUS_GOOD
        fi

    fi

fi

echo

# ==============================================================
# 02. PRIVILEGED ACCESS
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "02. PRIVILEGED / ADMIN ACCESS"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

ROLES=$(graph_all \
"$GRAPH/roleManagement/directory/roleDefinitions?\$select=id,displayName,isEnabled")

ASSIGNMENTS=$(graph_all \
"$GRAPH/roleManagement/directory/roleAssignments?\$select=principalId,roleDefinitionId,directoryScopeId")

if [ $? -ne 0 ]
then

    echo "Role Management : Unable to retrieve"
    STATUS_REVIEW
    REVIEW=$((REVIEW+1))

else

    ADMIN_ASSIGNMENTS=$(echo "$ASSIGNMENTS" |
        jq --argjson roles "$ROLES" '
        [
            .[]
            | . as $a
            | $roles[]
            | select(.id==$a.roleDefinitionId)
            | select(
                .displayName=="Global Administrator"
                or
                .displayName=="Privileged Role Administrator"
                or
                .displayName=="Security Administrator"
                or
                .displayName=="Conditional Access Administrator"
                or
                .displayName=="Application Administrator"
                or
                .displayName=="Cloud Application Administrator"
                or
                .displayName=="Exchange Administrator"
                or
                .displayName=="SharePoint Administrator"
                or
                .displayName=="Intune Administrator"
                or
                .displayName=="Authentication Administrator"
                or
                .displayName=="Privileged Authentication Administrator"
            )
            | {
                role:.displayName,
                principalId:$a.principalId,
                scope:$a.directoryScopeId
            }
        ]
        ')

    ADMIN_COUNT=$(echo "$ADMIN_ASSIGNMENTS" | jq 'length')

    TENANT_WIDE=$(echo "$ADMIN_ASSIGNMENTS" |
        jq '[.[] |
            select(.scope=="/")
        ] | length')

    echo "Privileged Assignments : $ADMIN_COUNT"
    echo "Tenant-Wide Admins     : $TENANT_WIDE"

    if [ "$TENANT_WIDE" -gt 3 ]
    then
        STATUS_HIGH
        HIGH_RISK=$((HIGH_RISK+1))
    elif [ "$ADMIN_COUNT" -gt 0 ]
    then
        STATUS_REVIEW
        REVIEW=$((REVIEW+1))
    else
        STATUS_GOOD
    fi

fi

echo

# ==============================================================
# 03. SECURITY POSTURE
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "03. SECURITY POSTURE"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

SECURE_SCORE=$(graph_get \
"$GRAPH/security/secureScores?\$top=1" \
2>/dev/null)

if echo "$SECURE_SCORE" | jq -e '.value[0]' >/dev/null 2>&1
then

    CURRENT_SCORE=$(echo "$SECURE_SCORE" |
        jq -r '.value[0].currentScore // 0')

    MAX_SCORE=$(echo "$SECURE_SCORE" |
        jq -r '.value[0].maxScore // 0')

    if [ "$MAX_SCORE" -gt 0 ]
    then

        SCORE_PERCENT=$(awk \
            -v current="$CURRENT_SCORE" \
            -v maximum="$MAX_SCORE" \
            'BEGIN {printf "%.1f", (current/maximum)*100}')

    else

        SCORE_PERCENT=0

    fi

    echo "Secure Score : $CURRENT_SCORE / $MAX_SCORE"
    echo "Score %      : $SCORE_PERCENT%"

    LOW_SCORE=$(awk \
        -v score="$SCORE_PERCENT" \
        'BEGIN {if (score < 50) print "yes"; else print "no"}')

    MEDIUM_SCORE=$(awk \
        -v score="$SCORE_PERCENT" \
        'BEGIN {if (score < 70) print "yes"; else print "no"}')

    if [ "$LOW_SCORE" = "yes" ]
    then
        STATUS_HIGH
        HIGH_RISK=$((HIGH_RISK+1))

    elif [ "$MEDIUM_SCORE" = "yes" ]
    then
        STATUS_REVIEW
        REVIEW=$((REVIEW+1))

    else
        STATUS_GOOD

    fi

else

    echo "Secure Score : Unable to retrieve"
    STATUS_REVIEW
    REVIEW=$((REVIEW+1))

fi

echo

# ==============================================================
# 04. EMAIL / DEFENDER
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "04. EMAIL SECURITY"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

ALERTS=$(graph_get \
"$GRAPH/security/alerts_v2?\$top=100" \
2>/dev/null)

if echo "$ALERTS" | jq -e '.value' >/dev/null 2>&1
then

    ACTIVE_ALERTS=$(echo "$ALERTS" |
        jq '[.value[] |
            select(
                .status=="new"
                or
                .status=="inProgress"
            )
        ] | length')

    echo "Active Security Alerts : $ACTIVE_ALERTS"

    if [ "$ACTIVE_ALERTS" -gt 10 ]
    then
        STATUS_HIGH
        HIGH_RISK=$((HIGH_RISK+1))

    elif [ "$ACTIVE_ALERTS" -gt 0 ]
    then
        STATUS_REVIEW
        REVIEW=$((REVIEW+1))

    else
        STATUS_GOOD

    fi

else

    echo "Security Alerts : Unable to retrieve"
    STATUS_REVIEW
    REVIEW=$((REVIEW+1))

fi

echo

# ==============================================================
# 05. SHAREPOINT / ONEDRIVE
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "05. SHAREPOINT / ONEDRIVE DATA SECURITY"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

echo "Guest Users : ${GUEST_USERS:-0}"

if [ "${GUEST_USERS:-0}" -gt 0 ]
then

    echo "External identities exist and require governance review."
    STATUS_REVIEW
    REVIEW=$((REVIEW+1))

else

    STATUS_GOOD

fi

echo

# ==============================================================
# 06. OAUTH APPLICATION SECURITY
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "06. OAUTH APPLICATION SECURITY"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

GRANTS=$(graph_all \
"$GRAPH/oauth2PermissionGrants?\$select=id,clientId,consentType,principalId,resourceId,scope")

if [ $? -ne 0 ]
then

    echo "OAuth Grants : Unable to retrieve"
    STATUS_REVIEW
    REVIEW=$((REVIEW+1))

else

    GRANT_COUNT=$(echo "$GRANTS" | jq 'length')

    TENANT_WIDE_GRANTS=$(echo "$GRANTS" |
        jq '[.[] |
            select(.consentType=="AllPrincipals")
        ] | length')

    HIGH_RISK_SCOPES=$(echo "$GRANTS" |
        jq '
        [
            .[]
            | select(
                (.scope | contains("Directory.ReadWrite.All"))
                or
                (.scope | contains("Application.ReadWrite.All"))
                or
                (.scope | contains("RoleManagement.ReadWrite.Directory"))
                or
                (.scope | contains("Mail.ReadWrite"))
                or
                (.scope | contains("Files.ReadWrite.All"))
                or
                (.scope | contains("Sites.FullControl.All"))
            )
        ]
        | length
        ')

    echo "OAuth Grants                : $GRANT_COUNT"
    echo "Tenant-Wide Consent         : $TENANT_WIDE_GRANTS"
    echo "High-Privilege Grants       : $HIGH_RISK_SCOPES"

    if [ "$HIGH_RISK_SCOPES" -gt 0 ]
    then
        STATUS_HIGH
        HIGH_RISK=$((HIGH_RISK+1))

    elif [ "$TENANT_WIDE_GRANTS" -gt 0 ]
    then
        STATUS_REVIEW
        REVIEW=$((REVIEW+1))

    else
        STATUS_GOOD

    fi

fi

echo

# ==============================================================
# 07. INTUNE
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "07. ENDPOINT / INTUNE"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

DEVICES=$(graph_all \
"$GRAPH/deviceManagement/managedDevices?\$select=id,deviceName,operatingSystem,complianceState,managementState,userPrincipalName,lastSyncDateTime")

if [ $? -ne 0 ]
then

    echo "Intune : Unable to retrieve"
    STATUS_REVIEW
    REVIEW=$((REVIEW+1))

else

    DEVICE_COUNT=$(echo "$DEVICES" | jq 'length')

    NONCOMPLIANT=$(echo "$DEVICES" |
        jq '[.[] |
            select(
                .complianceState=="noncompliant"
                or
                .complianceState=="unknown"
            )
        ] | length')

    echo "Managed Devices : $DEVICE_COUNT"
    echo "Review Devices  : $NONCOMPLIANT"

    if [ "$NONCOMPLIANT" -gt 0 ]
    then
        STATUS_REVIEW
        REVIEW=$((REVIEW+1))
    else
        STATUS_GOOD
    fi

fi

echo

# ==============================================================
# 08. AUDIT
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "08. COMPLIANCE & AUDIT"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

AUDIT=$(graph_get \
"$GRAPH/auditLogs/directoryAudits?\$top=1" \
2>/dev/null)

if echo "$AUDIT" | jq -e '.value' >/dev/null 2>&1
then

    echo "Directory Audit : AVAILABLE"
    STATUS_GOOD

else

    echo "Directory Audit : UNAVAILABLE"
    STATUS_REVIEW
    REVIEW=$((REVIEW+1))

fi

echo

# ==============================================================
# 09. LICENSE OPTIMIZATION
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "09. LICENSE OPTIMIZATION"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

LICENSED_COUNT=$(echo "$USERS" |
    jq '
    [
        .[]
        | select(
            .accountEnabled==true
            and
            .userType=="Member"
            and
            (.assignedLicenses | length)>0
        )
    ]
    | length
    ')

CUTOFF_90=$(date -u -d "90 days ago" +"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")

INACTIVE_LICENSED=$(echo "$USERS" |
    jq --arg cutoff "$CUTOFF_90" '
    [
        .[]
        | select(
            .accountEnabled==true
            and
            .userType=="Member"
            and
            (.assignedLicenses | length)>0
        )
        | select(
            .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime==null
            or
            .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime < $cutoff
        )
    ]
    | length
    ')

echo "Licensed Active Users       : $LICENSED_COUNT"
echo "90+ Day Review Candidates   : $INACTIVE_LICENSED"

if [ "$INACTIVE_LICENSED" -gt 0 ]
then
    STATUS_OPPORTUNITY
    OPPORTUNITY=$((OPPORTUNITY+1))
else
    STATUS_GOOD
fi

echo

# ==============================================================
# 10. USER LIFECYCLE
# ==============================================================

echo "------------------------------------------------------------"
echo "10. USER LIFECYCLE"
echo "------------------------------------------------------------"

DISABLED_USERS=$(echo "$USERS" |
    jq '[.[] |
        select(.accountEnabled==false)
    ] | length')

STALE_USERS=$(echo "$USERS" |
    jq --arg cutoff "$CUTOFF_90" '
    [
        .[]
        | select(
            .accountEnabled==true
            and
            .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime!=null
            and
            .signInActivity.lastSuccessfulSignInDateTime < $cutoff
        )
    ]
    | length
    ')

echo "Disabled Users : $DISABLED_USERS"
echo "90+ Day Stale  : $STALE_USERS"

if [ "$STALE_USERS" -gt 0 ]
then
    STATUS_REVIEW
    REVIEW=$((REVIEW+1))
else
    STATUS_GOOD
fi

echo

# ==============================================================
# FINAL CUSTOMER SCORECARD
# ==============================================================

echo
echo "╔════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗"
echo "║                 ZENAIOPS M365 ASSESSMENT                  ║"
echo "╚════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝"
echo
echo "CUSTOMER : $CUSTOMER"
echo "DATE     : $(date '+%d %b %Y %H:%M')"
echo

echo "SECURITY"
echo "────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────"

echo -n "🔐 Identity & MFA             : "

if [ "${MFA_NOT_REGISTERED:-0}" -gt 0 ]
then
    STATUS_REVIEW
else
    STATUS_GOOD
fi

echo -n "👑 Admin Access               : "

if [ "${TENANT_WIDE:-0}" -gt 3 ]
then
    STATUS_HIGH
else
    STATUS_REVIEW
fi

echo -n "🛡️ Security Posture          : "

if [ "${SCORE_PERCENT:-0}" = "0" ]
then
    STATUS_REVIEW
elif [ "$LOW_SCORE" = "yes" ]
then
    STATUS_HIGH
elif [ "$MEDIUM_SCORE" = "yes" ]
then
    STATUS_REVIEW
else
    STATUS_GOOD
fi

echo -n "📧 Email Security             : "

if [ "${ACTIVE_ALERTS:-0}" -gt 10 ]
then
    STATUS_HIGH
elif [ "${ACTIVE_ALERTS:-0}" -gt 0 ]
then
    STATUS_REVIEW
else
    STATUS_GOOD
fi

echo
echo "DATA"
echo "────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────"

echo -n "☁️ SharePoint / OneDrive     : "

if [ "${GUEST_USERS:-0}" -gt 0 ]
then
    STATUS_REVIEW
else
    STATUS_GOOD
fi

echo -n "🤖 OAuth Applications        : "

if [ "${HIGH_RISK_SCOPES:-0}" -gt 0 ]
then
    STATUS_HIGH
elif [ "${TENANT_WIDE_GRANTS:-0}" -gt 0 ]
then
    STATUS_REVIEW
else
    STATUS_GOOD
fi

echo
echo "OPERATIONS"
echo "────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────"

echo -n "💻 Endpoint / Intune         : "

if [ "${NONCOMPLIANT:-0}" -gt 0 ]
then
    STATUS_REVIEW
else
    STATUS_GOOD
fi

echo -n "📋 Compliance & Audit        : "

if echo "$AUDIT" | jq -e '.value' >/dev/null 2>&1
then
    STATUS_GOOD
else
    STATUS_REVIEW
fi

echo
echo "COST"
echo "────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────"

echo -n "💰 License Optimization      : "

if [ "${INACTIVE_LICENSED:-0}" -gt 0 ]
then
    STATUS_OPPORTUNITY
else
    STATUS_GOOD
fi

echo
echo "────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────"
echo "              ZENAIOPS RECOMMENDATION"
echo "────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────"
echo
echo "🔴 $HIGH_RISK High-Priority Areas"
echo "🟠 $REVIEW Areas Require Review"
